# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Chewa translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

Lamulo Lalikulu La Zimbabwe Lokonzedwa Mwatsopano (No. 20)

2013

ZIMBABWE

LAMULO Kuchotsa ndi kuyikanso Lamulo lalikulu la Zimbabwe. ZOPANGIDWA ndi Mtsogoleli wa dziko ndi Nyumba yaMalamulo yaZimbabwe. 1


Mutu mwachidule

Lamulo lalikuluili likhoza kuchulidwa Lamulo lalikulu la Zimbabwe Lokonzedwa Mwatsopano (No. 20) 2013. 2

Kumasulila MuLamulo limeneli— “Lamulo Lalikululi limene likugwira ntchito” kutanthauza Lamulo lalikulu la Zimbabwe limene linayamba kugwila ntchito pa tsiku la 18th April, 1980, mongalinga ndi momwe linakonzedwela; “Lamulo lalikulu la Zimbabwe” kutanthauza Lamulo lalikulu la Zimbabwe limene lomwe lili mu dongosolo lachinayi.


Kuchotsa Lamulo laLikulu lilipo poika Lamulo laLikulu latsopano

(1) Malingana ndi chigawo (2), Lamulo Lalikulu likugwila ntchito lachotsedwa ndi kuikidwa lamulo Latsopano.

(2) Pofuna kuthetsa kukaikila kwalamulidwa kuti—

(a)

lamulo latsopano lakonzedwa kuyambila pa “tsiku lasindikizidwa” moyendelana ndi dongosolo lachi sanu ndi chimodzi la Lamulo latsopano, kutanthauza kuti, pa tsiku lamuloli lasindikizidwa mu buku la Gazette moyendelana ndi chigawo 51(5) cha Lamulo lalikulu latsopano; ndipo Printed by the Government Printer, Harare. 1

DISTRIBUTED BY VERITAS e-mail: veritas@mango.zw; website: www.veritaszim.net Veritas makes every effort to ensure the provision of reliable information, but cannot take legal responsibility for information supplied.

(b) kuyambila pa tsiku limene lamulo latsopano lakonzedwa moyendelana ndi chiganizo chachikulu (a), lamulo Lalikulu likugwila ntchito likhala likugwila ntchito moyendelana ndi dongosolo la chisanu ndi chimodzi la Lamulo lalikululi. MNDANDANDA (CHIGAWO 2) LAMULO___________________ LALIKULU LA ZIMBABWE NDONDOMEKO YA ZIGAWO Chiyambi.

MUTU 1 ZOFUNIKILA ZOYENELA KUYANG’ANIDWA Chigawo

## Section 6

## Section 7

Dziko la Zimbabwe. Kufunikila kwa lamulo lalikulu. Mfundo zofunikila za dziko. Mbendela, Nyimbo ya fuko lathu ndi Chizindikilo cha Boma. Mgwilizano wa Boma. Zilankhulo. Kulimbikitsa kuziwisidwa kwa lamulo lalikulu. MUTU 2 ZOLINGA ZA BOMA

## Section 31

## Section 32

Zolinga zotsogolela Boma. Ulamulilo wabwino. Mgwilizano, bata ndi mtendele. Kulimbikitsa udindo ndi ufulu. Malamulo akunja kwa maiko. Chitukuko cha Boma. Kulimbikitsa ufulu wachibadwidwe ndi mutendele. Chakudya. Chikhalidwe. Kufanana kwa amuna ndi akazi. Kuyimililidwa koyenela kwa muzigawo. Ana. Achinyamata. Nkhalamba/Achikulile. Anthu opuwala/olemala. Ngwazi zinamenya nkhondo. Mgwilizano wa ntchito ndi anthu. Kuteteza banja. Ukwati/mabanja. Maphunzilo. Nyumba. Zipatala. Chithandizo cha anthu. Chithandizo cha lamulo. Masewelo ndi zipangizo zosewelela.

Chigawo

## Section 33

## Section 34

Kusunga ndi kusamalila nzelu za chikhalidwe. Kulimbikitsidwa kwa malamulo okhudza maiko. MUTU 3 NZIKA ZA DZIKO

## Section 35

## Section 42

## Section 43

Nzika za dziko la Zimbabwe. Nzika zakubadwa. Nzika za makolo. Nzika za kalembetsedwe. Kuthetsedwa kwa kukhala nzika. Kukhala nzika posatengela kutha kwa ukwati. Bungwe la nzika ndi kuyan’anila anthu olowa ndi otuluka m’dziko. Mphamvu ya nyumba ya malamulo moyendelana ndi nzika. Kupitiliza kukhala nzika. MUTU 4 UDINDO WA ANTHU

GAWO1 KUGWILITSA NDI KUMASULILIA KWA MUTU 4

## Section 44

## Section 45

## Section 46

## Section 47

Kulemekezedwa ndi ufulu wa anthu. Ntchito ya Mutu 4. Kumasulilidwa kwa Mutu 4. Mutu 4 suvhomeleza maudindo ena. GAWO 2 UDINDO NDI UFULU WA ANTHU

## Section 48

## Section 63

## Section 64

## Section 65

## Section 66

Udindo wokhala ndi Moyo. Udindo ndi uflu wokhala momasuka. Udindo wa anthu omangidwa ndi otsekeledwa mu ndende. Udindo wolemekezedwa. Udindo wotetezedwa. Ufulu wosavhutitsidwa, kupatsidwa chilango kapena kugwidwa ngati munthu. Ufulu wosakhala kapolo. Ufulu wosakakamizidwa pakugwira ntchito. Anthu onse ndi ofanana ndipo mposafunikila kupondelezana. Udindo ndi ufulu wa chinsisi. Ufulu wosonkhana ndi kulowa m’mabungwe. Ufulu wochita chiwonetselo ndi kupeleka pempho. Ufulu womasuka pa kulankhula chili chonse. Ufulu wolankhula ndi ufulu wa wolemba nkhani. Udindo wokumva nkhani ndi mauthenga. Chilankhulo ndi chikhalidwe. Ufulu wa ntchito ndi malonda. Maudindo a ntchito. Ufulu wa mayendedwe ndi kakhalidwe.

Chigawo

## Section 67

## Section 68

## Section 69

## Section 70

## Section 71

## Section 72

## Section 73

## Section 74

## Section 75

## Section 76

## Section 77

## Section 78

Udindo wa ndale. Udindo woyanjanilitsidwa mwa lamulo. Udindo wa chigamulo choyelela. Udindo wa munthu wozengedwa. Udindo ndi Ufulu wa zinthu. Udindo wa munda ya ulimi. Udindo wazinthu zachilengedwe. Ufulu wosathamangitsidwa pa malo. Udindo ndi ufulu wophunzila. Udindo ndi ufulu wachisamalilo cha umoyo. Udindo ndi ufulu wokhala ndi chakudya ndi madzi. Udindo ndi ufulu wa maukwati. GAWO 3 KUFOTOKOZEDWA KWA MAUDINDO ENA79

## Section 79

## Section 80

## Section 81

## Section 82

## Section 83

## Section 84

Kufotokozedwa Kwa Gawo 3. Udindo wa akazi. Udindo wa ana. Udindo ndi ufulu wa nkhalamba/achikulile. Udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu olemala. Udindo ndi ufulu wa ngwazi zinamenya nkhondo inabweletsa ufulu wa dziko. GAWO 4 KULIMBIKITSA UDINDO NDI UFULU WA ANTHU

## Section 85

Kulimbikitsa udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu. GAWO 5 MALILE A MPHAMVU YA UDINDO NDI UFULU WA ANTHU

## Section 86

## Section 87

Malile a mphamvu ya udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu. Malile pa nthawi ya dzidzidzi yokhudza anthu. MUTU 5 UTSOGOLELI WA PAMWAMBA

GAWO 1 UTSOGOLELI WA MPHAMVU

## Section 88

Mphamvu za pamwamba. GAWO 2 MTSOGOLELI WA DZIKO NDI WACHIWILI WA MTSOGOLELI WA DZIKO

## Section 89

## Section 90

## Section 91

## Section 92

## Section 93

## Section 94

Mtsogoleli wa dziko. Ntchito za mtsogoleli wa dziko. Zofunikila posankha mtsogoleri wa dziko pamodzi ndi wachiwili wa mtsogoleli wa dziko. Chisankho cha mtsogoleli wa dziko ndi wachiwili wa mtsogoleli wa dziko. Kutsutsana ndi zotuluka muchisankho cha mtsogoleli wa dziko. Kuyamba kugwira ntchito kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko pamodzi ndi wachiwili wotsatila mtsogoleli wa dziko.

Chigawo

## Section 95

## Section 96

## Section 97

## Section 98

## Section 99

## Section 100

## Section 101

## Section 102

## Section 103 — Kuyamba kugwila ntchito kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko pamodzi ndi wotsatila wa

mtsogoleli wa dziko. Kuchotsedwa kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko ndi wotsatila wa mtsogoleli wa dziko. Kusiya ntchito kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko ndi wotsatila wa mtsogoleli wa dziko. Kutetezedwa kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko. Ntchito za awachiwili wa mtsogoleli wa dziko. Kugwila ntchito yoyimilila mtsogoleli wa dziko. Wolowa m’malo mwa mtsogoleli wa dziko kapena wachiwili wa mtsogoleli wa dziko ngati amwalila, kusiya ntchito kapena kulephela kugwila ntchito. Malipilo a mtsogoleli wa dziko pamodzi ndi wachiwili a atsogoleli a dziko. Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi wotsatila mtsogoleli wa dziko pamodzi ndi ena ankagwila nawo ntchitosayenela kugwira ntchito muBoma. GAWO 3 NDUNA, WOTSATILA NDUNA NDI BUNGWE LANDUNA

## Section 104

## Section 105

## Section 106

## Section 107

## Section 108

## Section 109

Kusankhidwa kwa nduna pamodzi ndi wotsatila nduna za Boma. Bungwe la nduna za Boma. Makhalidwe a wotsatila mtsogoleli wa dziko, nduna pamodzi ndi wotsatila nduna za Boma. Kukhulupililika kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko, nduna pamodzi ndi wotsatila Nduna. Nthawi yogwila ntchito ya nduna ndi wotsatila nduna. Kuvoteledwa kusiya ntchito m’Boma. GAWO 4 NTCHITO ZAPAMWAMBA

## Section 110

## Section 111

## Section 112

## Section 113

Ntchito za pamwamba za mtsogoleli wa dziko ndi bungwe la nduna za Boma. Nkhondo ndi mtendele. Mphamvu yokhululukira anthu. Kayimidwe ka phokozo. GAWO 5

## Section 114

## Section 115 — LOYA LA BOMA

Loya la Boma. Kuchotsedwa ntchito kwa loya la Boma.

MUTU 6 KAKONZEDWE KAMALAMULO

GAWO 1 MPHAMVU ZOKONZA MALAMULO

## Section 116

## Section 117

Malamulo. Bungwe yokonza malamulo. GAWO 2 NYUMBA YA MALAMULO

## Section 118

## Section 119

Nyumba ya malamulo. Ntchito za nyumba ya malamulo.

GAWO 3 NYUMBA YA SENETI Chigawo

## Section 120

## Section 121

## Section 122

## Section 123

Nyumba ya Seneti. Kasankhidwe ka phungu wa Seneti. Mtsogoleli wa mu nyumba ya Seneti. Wotsatila mtsogoleli wa nyumba ya Seneti GAWO 4 NYUMBA YA MALAMULO

## Section 124

## Section 125

## Section 126

## Section 127

Wokhalamu nyumba yamalamulo. Kusankhidwa ndi kuchotsedwa kukhala phungu wa munyumba ya malamulo. Wolankhula mu nyumba ya malamulo. Wotsatila wolankhulila mu nyumba ya malamulo. GAWO 5

## Section 128

## Section 129 — NTHAWI YA APHUNGU AMUNYUMBA YA MALAMULO

Kulumbila kuti ukhale phungu ya myumba ya malamulo. Nthawi ya aphungu a nyumba ya malamulo.

GAWO 6 MPHAMVU ZA MUNYUMBA YA MALAMULO

## Section 130

## Section 131

## Section 132

## Section 133

## Section 134

Mphamvu ndi ntchito za nyumba ya Seneti ndi nyumba ya malamulo. Lamulo lokonzedwa mu nyumba ya malamulo ndi dongosolo ya kakonzedwe kake. Kuyamba kugwila ntchito kwa lamulo. Kuvomelezedwa kwa lamulo. Lamulo lapadela. GAWO 7 KAYENDETSEDWE KA NTCHITO MUNYUMBA YAMALAMULO

## Section 135

## Section 136

## Section 137

## Section 138

## Section 139

## Section 140

## Section 141

## Section 142

Mtsogoleli wa nyumba ya malamulo. Anthu amene angathe kutsogolela mu nyumba ya malamulo. Kuchuluka kwa aphungu a munyumba ya malamulo. Udindo wovhota mu nyumba ya malamulo. Lamulo lotsatilidwa. Mawu ndi mauthenga ochokela kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko ku nyumba ya malamulo. Anthu ayenela kutenga mbali muzokambilana za munyumba ya malamulo. Kuvomelezeka kwa zochitika mu nyumba ya malamulo. GAWO 8 NTHAWI, KUYIMITSIDWA NDI MISONKHANO YAMUNYUMBA YAMALAMULO

## Section 143

## Section 144

## Section 145

## Section 146

## Section 147

Nthawi ndi kuyimitsidwa kwa nyumba ya malamulo. Chisankho chobwela pambuyo poyimitsa nyumba ya malamulo. Msonkhano woyamba mu nyumba ya malamulo chisankho chitatha. Misonkhano ndi nthawi yopuma. Kuthetsedwa kwa malamulo ankakonzedwa, pempho, zokambilana ndi ntchito panthawi Nyumba ya malamulo iyimikidwa.

GAWO 9 ZINA ZOKHUDZA NYUMBA YAMALAMULO Chigawo

## Section 148

## Section 149

## Section 150

## Section 151

## Section 152

## Section 153

## Section 154

Mwayi ndi kutetezedwa mu nyumba ya malamulo. Udindo wopeleka pempho. Malo a nyumba ya malamulo. Komiti yokonza malamulo ndi dongosolo. Komiti yowona za malamulo kuNyumba ya Malamulo.. Malipilo a Mtsogoleli wa Seneti, Mneneli ndi Aphungu a Nyumba ya Malamulo. Mabalani wa Nyumba ya Malamulo ndi wogwila ntchito ena. MUTU 7 CHISANKHO

GAWO 1

## Section 155

## Section 156

## Section 157 — DONGOSOLO YA CHISANKHO NDI KAYENDETSEDWE

Mfundo za dongosolo ya chisankho. Machitidwe a chisankhon pamodzi ndi zisankho za maganizo a anthu. Malamulo akayendetsedwe ka chisankho.

GAWO 2 NTHAWI YOCHITITSA CHISANKHO

## Section 158

## Section 159

Nthawi yochititsa chisankho. Kusankha anthu pamalo popanda aphungu. GAWO 3 KAGAWIDWE KA MALIRE NDI MALO OCHITILA CHISANKHO

## Section 160

## Section 161

Kuchuluka kwa madela ochitila chisankho ndi mawadi. Kagawidwe ka madela ochitila chisankho. MUTU 8 KAYENDETSEDWE KA MILANDU NDI MABWALO OWELUZILA MILANDU

GAWO 1 DONGOSOLO YA BWALO LA MILANDU

## Section 162

## Section 163

## Section 164

## Section 165

## Section 166

## Section 167

## Section 168

## Section 169

## Section 170

## Section 171

## Section 172

## Section 173

## Section 174

Udindo ndi mphambu ya malamulo. Za malamulo. Kudziyimilila kwa malamulo. Mfundo zotsogolela malamulo. Bwalo lozenga milandu ya lamulo lalikulu. Magwilidwe a anchito a bwalo lamilandi la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo. Bwalo la lilandu la pamwamba mwamba. Magwilidwe anthcito a la pamwamba mwamba. Bwalo la Lapamwamba. Magwilidwe a ncthtio la Lapamwamba. Bwalo lozenge nkhani za ogwila ntchito. Bwalo la milandu la ntchito. Mabwalo ena a milandu ndi kusuma.

Chigawo

## Section 175

## Section 176

Mphamvu za mabwalo amilandu la kamba nkhani za lamulo. Mphamvu zakale zomwe zakhala zilipo ndipo sizisitha a mabwalo okhudza za lamulo lalikulu, bwalo la milandu lapamwama mwamba ndi Lapamwamba. GAWO 2

KUSANKHIDWA NDI ZAKA ZA THUMWI ZOGWIRA NTCHITO YOZENGA LIMANDU

## Section 177

## Section 178

## Section 179

## Section 180

## Section 181

## Section 182

## Section 183

## Section 184

## Section 185

## Section 186

## Section 187

## Section 188

Zofunikila kukha zozenga milandu ku bwalo la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo. Zofunikila kukhala zozenga milandu ku bwalo lapamwamba-mwamba. Zofunikila zokhala wozenga milandu ku bwalo lalikulu la Lapamwamba, Loyang’anila anthu ogwila ntchito ndi bwalo lozenge atsogoleri. Kusankhidwa kwa ozenga milandu ku mabwalo apamwamba. Wogwilizila nchito za milandu. Kasankhidwe ka wozenga milandu ndi ena ogwila ntchito. Ozenga milandi sayenela kugwila ntchito ku bwalo oposa limodzi. Masankhidwe azamalamulo ayene kutsata zofuna za anthu. Kulumbila mu ofesi. Zaka zogwila ntchito ozenga milandu. Kuchotsedwa ntchito kwa ozenga milandu kwa bwalo lalikulu la milandu. Magwilidwe a nthcito ndi zaka zawo kwa ogwila ntchito ya milandu. GAWO 3 BUNGWE LOYANG’ANILA NKHANI ZA MALAMULO

## Section 189

## Section 190

## Section 191

Kukonzedwa ndi thumwi za mubungwe loyang’anila nkhani zalamulo. Nthcito za bungwe Loyang’anila malamulo. Kusakondwela ndi zochitika ku bungwe lozenge milandu. GAWO 4

## Section 192

## Section 193 — NKHANI ZINA

Lamulo logwila ntchito. Kuzengedwa kwa milandu ndi mabwalo.

MUTU 9 MFUNDO ZA KAYENDETSEDWE KA NTCHITO NDI UTSOGOLELI

## Section 194

## Section 195

## Section 196

## Section 197

## Section 198

Zochitika ndi mfundo zakayendetsedwe kanthcito. Makampani a Boma. Ntchito za ogwila ntchito ndi zofunikila pa ulamulilo. Zaka zogwila ntchito akulu amu Boma. Malamulo olimbikitsa mutu 9. MUTU 10 OGWILA NTCHITO MU BOMA

## Section 199

## Section 200

## Section 201

## Section 202

## Section 203

## Section 204

## Section 205

Ogwila ntchito m’Boma. Makhalidwe a ogwila ntchito m’Boma. Nduna yoyang’anila ogwila ntchito m’Boma. Kukonzedwa kwa bungwe loyanga’anila anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma. Ntchito za bungwe loyang’anila anthu ogwila nthcito m’Boma. Akazembe ndi akulu ena oyimilila dziko la Zimbabwe. Alembi a nduna za m’Boma.

MUTU 11 ZACHITETEZO

GAWO 1 ZINTHU ZINA ZOYENELA

Chigawo

## Section 206

Chitetezo cha mu dziko.

## Section 207

Ntchito za oteteza dziko.

## Section 208 — Makhalidwe a ogwila ntchito za chitetezo

## Section 209

Bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za chitetezo.

## Section 210

Njila zoziyimilila za kuthetsa madandaulo. GAWO 2 CHITETEZO/ASILIKALI

## Section 211

## Section 212

## Section 213

## Section 214

## Section 215

## Section 216

## Section 217

## Section 218

Asilikali. Ntchito za asilikali. Kutumizidwa kwa achitetezo. Kutumizidwa kwa asilikali moyenela Ntchito ya nduna oyang’anila nkhani za chitetezo. Kulamula nkhani za chitetezo/asilikali. Kukonzedwa kwa bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za chitetezo. Ntchito za bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za chitetezo. GAWO 3 NTCHITO ZA A POLISI

## Section 219

## Section 220

## Section 221

## Section 222

## Section 223

A polisi pamodzi ndi ntchito zawo. Nduna woyang’anila nkani za a polisi. Wamukulu woyang’anila nkhani za a polisi. Kukonzedwa kwa bungwe loyang’anila bungwe la a polisi. Nthcito za bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za a polisi. GAWO 4 CHITETEZO CHA CHINSINSI

## Section 224

## Section 225

## Section 226

Kukonzedwa kwa achitetezo achinsisi. Nduna woyang’anila nkhani za chinsinsi. Kulamula kapena kuteteza nkhani za chinsinsi za Boma. GAWO 5 NDENDE NDI KULANGIZIDWA

## Section 227

## Section 228

## Section 229

## Section 230

## Section 231

Ndende ndi kukonzedwa pamodzi ndi ntchito zalo. Nduna woyang’anila nkhani zaku ndende ndi kulangizidwa kwa anthu. Wamukulu woyang’anila nkhani zaku ndende ndi kulangizidwa kwa anthu. Kukonzedwa ndi thumwi za mu bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za ku ndende ndi kulangizidwa kwa anthu. Ntchito za bungwe loyang’anila nkani za ndende ndi kuulangizidwa kwa anthu.

MUTU 12 BUNGWE LODZIYIMILILA LOKHA LOTHANDIZILA ULAMULILO WA BWINO

GAWO 1 ZINTHU ZINA

Chigawo

## Section 232

## Section 233

## Section 234

## Section 235

## Section 236

## Section 237

Mabungwe odziyimilila. Zolinga za mabungwe odziyimilila. Ogwila ntchito ku mabungwe odziyimilila. Ufulu wa mabungwe odziyimilila. Ogwila ntchito ku mabungwe odziyimilila okha sayenela kutenga mbali za ndale. Kusankhidwa ndi kuchotsedwa ntchtio kwa anthu ogwila ntchit ku mabungwe odziyimilila okha. GAWO 2 BUNGWE LOYANGA’NILA CHISANKHO

## Section 238

## Section 239

## Section 240

## Section 241

Kukonzedwa kwa bungwe loyang’anila chisankho. Ntchito za bungwe loyang’anila chisankho. Kuchotsedwa/kuthetsedwa kwa bungwe loyang’anila chisankho. Bungwe loyang’anila chisankho iyenela kupeleke repoti ya chisankho ndi chisankho cha maganizo a anthu. GAWO 3 BUNGWE LOYANG’ANILA UDINDO WA ANTHU

## Section 242

## Section 243

## Section 244

Kukonzedwa kwa bungwe loyang’anila udindo wa anthu. Nthcito za bungwe loyang’anila udundo wa anthu. Ma ripoti ochokera ku bungwe loyang’anila udindo wa anthu. GAWO 4

## Section 245

## Section 246

## Section 247 — BUNGWE LOYANG’ANILA KUFANANA KWA AMUNA NDI AKAZI

Kukonzedwa kwa bungwe loyang’anila kufanana kwa amuna ndi akazi ndi onse ogwila ntchito. Nthcito za bungwe loyang’anila kufanana kwa akazi ndi amuna. Malipoti ochokela ku bungwe loyang’anila kufanan kwa amuna ndi akazi.

GAWO 5 BUNGWE LOYANG’ANILA NKHANI ZA MAUTHENGA NDI MAWU

## Section 248

## Section 249

## Section 250 — Kukonzedwa ndi thumwi za mu bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za mauthenga

ndi mawu. Nthcito za bungwe loyang’anila nkhani ndi mauthenga. Malipoti ochokela ku bungwe loyang’anila nkhani ndi mawu. GAWO 6 KOMISHONI YOWONA ZOBWELETSA BATA NDI KUKHULULUKILANA

## Section 251

## Section 252

## Section 253 — Kukonzedwa ndi kukhazikitsidwa kwa bungwe yobweletsa bata ndi

kukhululukilana. Ntchito za bungwe yobweletsa bata ndi kukhululukilana. Maripoti opelekedwa kuchokela ku bungwe yobweletsa bata ndi kukhululukilana.

MUTU 13 MABUNGWE OLIMBIKANA NDI UTANGALE NDI MILANDU

GAWO 1 KOMISHONI YOLIMBIKANA NDI UTANGALE MU ZIMBABWE

Chigawo

## Section 254

## Section 255

## Section 256

## Section 257 — Kukhazikidwa kwa bungwe ndi wogwila ntchito ku Komishoni Yolimbikana

## Section 345

Kusiyana kwa malemba a Lamulo Likulu. Mdandanda Woyamba: Mbendele ya dziko, Nyimbo ya fuko lathu, Chizindikilo cha Boma. Mdandanda Wachiwili: Malile a udindo panthawi ya maphokoso. Mdandanda Wachitatu: Kulumbila ndi kusindikiza. Mdandanda Wachinai: Zofunikila kwa anthu woponya mavoti. Mdandanda Wachisanu: Dongosolo ya makonzedwe a malamulo ndi nkhani zina ya munyumba ya malamulo. Mdandanda Wachisanu Ndi Chimodziedule: Kayambidwe ka lamulo lalikululi, kasinthidwe ndi masungidwe ake.

CHIYAMBI ______

Ife anthu a mu Zimbawe, Mukugwilizana mukusiyana kwathu ndi cholinga chimodzi chaufulu, chilungamo ndi chiyanjano, ndi kutsutsana ndi utsamunda, kusankhana ndi mitundu yonse ya kupondelezedwa, Kulemekeza ndi kuthokoza amuna ndi akazi omwe anapeleka umoyo wawo pa nthawi ya nkhondo ya Chimurenga/Umvekela ndi nkhondo inabwelesa ufulu, Tikuthokoza makolo athu ndi anthu omwe aliko lero omwe anagwila ntchito yaikulupachitukuko cha dzikolathu, Kuzindikila kufunika kwa ulamulilo wa bwino, zabwino, chilungamo ndi utsogoleli wotsatila Lamulo la dziko, Tikulimbikitsa kuzipatsila kupititsa mtsogolo ndikuteteza maufulu a m’badwe, maufulu ndi kumasuka, Tikuzindikila kuti mphatso yadziko ili ndi chuma, Kusangalala Ubwino wa chikhalidwe chathu ndi zochitidwa ndi anthu, Kuzipatsila kuthesa mavuto osiyanasiyana omwe akubwelesa mbuyo chitukuko, Kukondwela ufulu, kuyanjana, bata ndi mtendele, chilungamo, Kumvana, chuma, ndi kukonda dziko pofuna kupeza zina zatsopano, Kuzindikila mphamvu za Mulungu, emwe mumanja ake lamawa lathu likudalila, Tikuvomelana ndi zamkati mwa Lamulo pofuna kukhazikitsa dziko lokhalila pa chigwilizano, chilungamo ndi dziko lolemela, lokhazikika pa kugwila ntchito bwino, kuyanjana, ufulu, zoona, ndi kugwila ntchito ndi mphamvu, Ndikufuna-funa chithandizo potsogololedwa ndi Mulungu, pokonza lamulo lalikululi ndi kudzipeleka kwa ilo chifukwa cha dziko lanthu.

MUTU 1 ZOFUNIKILA ZOYENELA KUYANG’ANIDWA


Dziko la Zimbabwe Dziko la Zimbabwe ndi lamgwilizano, lomasuka ndi lodzilamulila.


Kufunikila kwa lamulo lalikulu

(1) Lamulo lalikululi ndi lamulo lofunikila kwa malamulo onse amu Zimbabwe ndipo lamulo lililonse, pozenga, chikhalidwe ndi machitidwe liyenela kutsatilidwa momwe lilili.

(2) Zofunikila zili mu lamulo lalikululi ziyenela kutsatilidwa ndi munthu aliyense, kapena wa zamilandu, ngakhale a Boma ndi olamulila, amalamulo ndi mabungwe a zamalamulo ndi nthambi za Boma ku madela onse, ayenela kukwanilitsidwa ndi iwo. 3

Mfundo zofunikila za dziko

(1) Dziko la Zimbabwe liyenela kulemekeza mfundo ndi malangizo—

(a)

kufunikila kwa lamullo lalikululi;

(b) ulamulilo wa bwino;

(c)

udindo ndi uflu wa anthu;

(d) kusiyana kwa chikhalidwe ndi mitundu ya anthu;

(e)

kulemekeza umunthu wa munthu aliyense;

(f)

kuzindikila kufunikila kwa kufanana kwa munthu aliyense;

(g) kufanana kwa amuna ndi akazi;

(h) ulamulilo wabwino; ndi

(i)

kuzindikila ndi kulemekeza nkhondo inabweletsa ufulu.

(2) Mfundo za ulamulilo wa bwino, zimene Boma ndi mabungwe onse ndi nthambi zonse ziyenela kutsata kupatikizil—

(a)

dziko lokhla ndi ndale zosiyana siyana;

(b) kayendetsedwe ka chisankho koyenela—

(i) kutenga mbali kwa munthu aliyense ndi mavoti abwino

(ii) chisankho, chabwino ndi chosakondela; ndipo

(iii) kuyimililidwa kokwana kwa anthu;

(c)

kusinthana koyenela kulamulila pambuyo pa chisankho;

(d) kulemekeza udindo wa zipani zonse zandale;

(e)

kuvomeleza mfundo za kusiyana kwa mphamvu;

(f)

kulemekeza anthu amu Zimbabwe, kumene mphamvu ya ulamulilo ukuchokela;

(g) ulamulilo woyenela, wabwino ndi womveka;

(h) kulimbikitsa umodzi, bata, mtendele ndi kukhazikika polemekeza zilankhulo zosiyanasiyana, chikhalidwe cha makolo;

(i)

kulemekeza udindo wa—

(i) anthu, mitundu, chikhalidwe ndi magulu a zipempelo

(ii) anthu alemala

(iii) akazi, nkhalamba, achinyamata ndi ana

(iv) ngwazi zinamenya nkhondo

(j)

kugawana kwa zinthu za chilengedwe moyenela kuphatikizila;

(k) kulemekeza udindo; ndi

(l) 4

kupeleka ndi kugawa mphamvu ya ulamulilo ndi ntchito

Mbendela, Nyimbo ya fuko lathu ndi Chizindikilo cha Boma

Dziko la Zimbabwe lili ndi mbendela ya dziko, Nyimbo ya Fuko lathu, ndi chizindikilo cha Boma monyendelana ndi dongosolo ya mu lamulo la chisanu ndi chimodzi. 5

Mgwilizano wa Boma Mphamvu za ulamulilo wa dziko la Zimbabwe

(a)

Boma;

(b) Makansulo a zigawo ndi mu muatauni ndi;

(c)


makansulo a mumaBoma—

(i) makansulo a kumidzi, ndi maina awo amatchulidwa nawo, ayenela kuyimilila ndi kulimbikisa zofuna za anthu ku madela akumidzi ku ma boma awo ku zigawo za dziko

(ii) makansulo a mu tauni, ndi maina awo amatchulidwa nawo, ayenela kuyimilila ndi kulimbikisa zofuna za anthu ku madela akumidzi ku ma boma awo ku zigawo za dziko

Zilankhulo

(1) Zilankhulo ndi izi, Chewa, ChiBarwe, English, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Shangani, Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga, Tswana, Venda ndi Xhosa, ndizo zilankhulo zovomelezeka mu dziko la Zimbabwe.

(2) Lamulo la mu nyumba ya malamulo lingathe kuvomeleza zilankhulo zina monga zilankhulo za mbili.

(3) Boma ndi mabungwe onse pamodzi ndi nthambi za Boma ku madela onse ziyenela—

(a)

kuwonetsetsa kuti zilankhulo zonse zikulandilidwa mofanana; ndipo

(b) pozindikila zilankhulo zimene zikukhudzidwa ndi njila za boma kapena mumalankulidwe

(4) Boma liyenela kulimbikitsa kugwilitsidwa kwa zilankhulo zonse kuphatilikiza Sign language, ndi kuthandikiza pokweza zilankhulozi. 7

Kulimbikitsa kuziwisidwa kwa lamulo lalikulu Boma liyenela kulimbikitsa kumveka kwa lamulo lalikululi makamaka—

(a)

kumasulila mu zilankhulo zonse zovomelezela ndi kufalitsa uthengawu;

(b) kuwonetsetsa kuti lamulo lalikululi likuphunzitsidwa ku sukulu zonse ndipo monga mbali ya maphunzilo yophunzilila nalo asililali, ogwila ntchito zoma, onse olembedwa ntchito ndi Boma; ndipo

(c)

kulimbikitsa anthu onse pamodzi ndi mabungwe kuphatikizila mabungwe odziyimilila okha kuti aziwe ndi kufalitsa uthenga wa lamulo lalikululi kwa anthu onse.

MUTU 2 8

ZOLINGA ZA BOMA Zolinga zotsogolela Boma pamodzi ndi mabungwe ndi nthambi za m’Boma.

(1) Zolinga zili m’chigawo chino ndi zotsogolela Boma, mabungwe ndi nthambi za m’Boma pokonza ndi kulimbikitsa malamulo ndiponso pokonza, kulimbikitsa chikhalidwe cha anthu kuti akhale mwaufulu pa umoyo wawo.

(2) Mpofunikilitsa kusamalila pomasulila malamulo ali m’chigawochi cha lamulo lalikulu lam’dziko kuphatikizila la malamulo onse. 9

Kulamulila Boma moyenela

(1) Boma liyenela kukonza ndi kulimbikitsa malamulo m’mabungwe onse polimbikitsa kagwilidwe ndi mayendetsedwe abwino anthito m’mabungwe ndi nthambi za m’Boma zopezeka ku madela onse, makamaka—

(a)

Kusankhidwa anthu agwira ntchito m’Boma mpoyenela kusankha anthu odziwa ntchito yawo.

(b) Mpofunikira kuthetsa machitidwe a katangale, ziphuphu Kwa awo ogwila ntchito m’Boma.

(2) Boma liyenela kuwonetsetsa kuti mabungwe ndi nthambi za m’Boma kumadela onse amene akonzedwa kudzela ndi lamulo lalilkululi, ayenela kupatsidwa zinthu zokwanila pofuna kuti ntchito yam’Boma iyende bwino. 10

Mgwilizano wa m’dziko, bata ndi mtendele

Boma ndi munthu aliyense, kuphatikizila ogwira ntchito za malamulo, nthambi ndi mabungwe am’Boma ayenela kulimbikitsa bata ndi mtendele. 11

Kulimbikitsa ufulu wachibadwidwe ndi mtendele.

Boma liyenela kulimbikitsa ndi kuteteza ufulu wa chibadwidwe momwe ulili muchigawo cha chinayi 4 pothandizila kulimbikitsidwa kwa lamuloli. 12

Mfundozogwilitsa ntchito kumaiko akunja.

(1) Malamulo ogwilitsidwa a nthito kumaiko akunja ndi dziko la Zimbabwe ayenela kuchokwela ku mfundo izi”—

(a)

kulimbikitsa ndi kuteteza zolinga za m’dziko la Zimbabwe;

(b) kulemekeza malamulo a maikoena kukhala mwa mtendele ndi maiko ena ndinso;

(c)

kukhala mwamtendele ndi mayiko ena;

(d) kuthetsa mikangano ndi maphokoso a maiko ena mwa mtendele.

(2) Boma liyenela kulimbikitsa chikhalidwe cha anthu a mu Afrika, mgwilizano wa wachuma ndi ndale ndinso liyenela kuthandizila m’mabungwe obweletsa bata ndi mtendele kuzigawo za m’maiko. 13

Chitukuko cha m’dziko

(1) Boma kuphatikizilamabungwe ndi nthambi zam’dziko kumadela onse liyenela kulimbikitsa chitukuko kumadela onse moyenela, makamaka potsata njiraizi—

(a) kulimbikitsa njira zobweletsa chitukuko kuchokera kumabungwe odziimilira okha;

(b) kulimbikitsa chitukuko cha ulimi, malonda, makampani pamodzi ndi luso la sayansi;

(c)

kulimbikitsa chitukuko cha makamapani ndicholinga chopatsa mphamvu yachuma kwa anthu onse am’dziko ndinso;

(d) kubweretsa chitukuko chofanana ku Madera onse ali m’dziko la Zimbabwe, makamaka chitukuko ku Madera akumidzi ndi amtauni.

(2) Njira zikufotokozedwa mu chigawochi ziyenela kuchokera kwa anthu pokonza dongosolo ndi ndondomeko za chitukuko zowakhudza.

(3) Njira zikufotokozedwa mu chigawochi ziyenela kuteteza ndi kulimbitsa udindo wa anthu, makamaka akazi powapatsa mwayi wofanana pa nkhani za chitukuko.

(4) Boma liyenela kuwonetsetsa kuti anthu akupeza pindu kuchokera ku zinthu za chilengedwe zochokela ku amadela awo. 14

Kupatsidwa mphamvu ya chuma ndi kuyambitsidwa kwa zintchito

(1) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi zonse za Boma ndi mabungwe odziyimila okha kumadela onse ayenela kuthandizila popeza njira zopatsa anthu chuma moyenela popanda tsankho, kwa anthu, magulu ndi ku mamidzi konse m’dziko lonse la Zimbabwe.

(2) Nthawi zonse Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’Boma ndi mabungwe oziyimilila okha ziyenera kuwonetsetsa kuti anthu ku madela onse ayenera kupeza njira zoyambitsa ntchito makamaka kwa akazi ndi achinyamata. 15

Chakudya chokwanila Boma liyenela—

(a)

kulimbikitsa anthu kulima ndi kusunga chakudya chokwanila;

(b) kukonza nkhokwe zosungila chakudzya andinso;

(c) 16

kulimbikitsa ncha magulu atatu kudzela ndi maphunzilo ndi njira zina zonse zoyenela kuphunzitsira anthu.

Chikhalidwe

(1) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse ayenera kulimbikitsa ndi kuteteza miyambo ya chikhalidwe chanthu pofuna kubweletsa ulemelelo ndi kufanana kwa anthu onse am’Zimbabwe.

(2) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse ayenera kulimbikitsa nzika zonse kuti asungwe ndi kuteteza malo achikhalidwe otetezedwa.

(3) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse ayenera kupeza njira zolemekeza mabungwe achikhalidwe. 17

Kufanana kwa amuna ndi akazi

(1) Boma liyenela kulimbikitsa kufanana kwa amuna ndi akazi ku madela awo, makamaka—

(a)

Boma liyenela kulimbikitsa akazi kutenga mbali pa zinthu zonse zochitika ku madela awo mofanana ndi amuna;

(b) Boma liyenela kupeza njira, kuphatikizira njila za malamulo zoyenela pofuna kuti;

(i) Amuna ndi akazi ayenela kuyimililidwa mofanana ku mabungwe ndi thambi zones za m’Boma ku madela onse;

(ii) Akazi ayenala kukwana theka la kuchuluka kwawo ku mabungwe okhazikitsidwa pansi pa lamulu lalilkululi kapena lochokera ku nyumba ya malamulo;

(c)

Boma, mabungwe odalila pa Boma ayenela kukhazikitsa njila zoti akazi akupeza zinthu, kuphatikizila nthaka, mofanana ndi amuna

(2) Boma liyenela kuthetsa kusankhana kwa amuna ndi akazi kumene kunalipo pa malamulo ndi machitidwe akale. 18

Kuyimililidwa koyenera kuchokera ku zigawo za dziko

(1) Boma liyenela kulimbikitsa kuyimililidwa koyenela kuchokera ku zigawo zonse za m’Zimbabwe ku mabungwe ndi nthambi zonse za m’dziko kumadela onse.

(2) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse ayenela kupeza njira zogawa zinthu pofuna kulimbikitsa chitukuko cha madela awo. 19

Ana

(1) Boma liyenela kuvhomeleza malamulo ndi kuonetsetsa kuti nkhani zokhudza ana zikuikidwa patsogolo.

(2) Boma liyenera kuvhomeleza malamulo ndi njira moyenderana ndi ndalama zilipo, powonetsetsa kuti ana—

(a)

akupatsidwa chisamalilo pa banja ndi makolo, ndinso akupatsidwa chisamalilochi ngakhale popanda mabanja;

(b) akukhala m’nyumba ndiponso ali ndi chakudya, umoyo wabwino ndi zonse zofunikila;

(c)

akutetezedwa mukuvhutitsidwa kuli konse ndi posowa chisamaliro;

(d) ali ndi mwayi wophunzila;

(3) Boma liyenera kuika malamulo pamodzi ndi njira zina—

(a)

kuteteza ana mukugwira ntchito zosayenera;

(b) powonetsetsa kuti ana savhomelezedwa kugwirira ntchito—

(i) yosayendelana ndi msinkhu wawo;

(ii) kuika ana ma ngozi pa maphunziro, umoyo, pamodzi ndi chikhalidwe. 20

Achinyamata

(1) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse ayenera kupeza njira, kuphatikizira ma programu aachinyamata, omwe ali ndi zaka khumi ndi mphambu zisanu (15) kufikira zaka makumi atatu ndi mphambu zisanu—

(a)

ali ndi mwayi wophunzira;

(b) ali ndi mwayi motenga mbali ndi kuimililidwa pa nkhani za ndale, chuma, chikhalidwe ndi zinthu zina zokhudza umoyo wawo;

(c)

akupatsidwa mwayi wa ntchito ndi njira zina zopezela nazo chuma/ ndalama;

(d) akupatsidwa mwayi wochita masewero ndi kugwiritsa ntchito zipangizo zochitira masewero ndiponso;

(e)

2013

ndi otetezedwa pa zikhalidwe zosayenrapamodzi ndi njira zonse zowasokoneza.

(2) Dongosolo ya nyumba ya malamulo iyenera kupatsa achinyamata ma programu a achinyamata.

(3) Njira zones zafotokozedwa mu chigawo (1) ndi (2) ayenera kukhudza achinyamata aliyense, posatengera kuti ndi wachipani chiti pokhudza munthu onse m’dziko. 21

Achikulile

(1) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse kupeza njira, kuphatikizira malamulo, wolemekeza ndi kuteteza achikulile ndiponso kuwonetsetsa kuti zikutenga mbali pa umoyo wawo ku madela.

(2) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse kuwonetsetsa kuti ngati pali zipangizo ndi ndalama zokwanira—

(a)

kulimbikitsa nkhalamba kuti zizitenga mbali pa chikhalidwe chawo;

(b) kupatsa chakudya, zipangizo ndi kusamalila nkhalamba zikusowa/zilibe/ zosauka;

(c)

kukonza ma progiramu opatsa mwayi nkhalamba kuti adzichita zinthu zibweretsa phindu moyenderana ndi chidwi ndi kudziwa kwawo;

(d) kulimbikitsa mabungwe ali ndi cholinga chokweza umoyo wa nkhalamba. 22

Anthu olemala

(1) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse iyenera kuvhomeleza udindo wa anthu olemala ziwala za m’thupi kapena amisala, makamaka udindo wawo uyenera kulemekezedwa moyenela.

(2) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse moyenderana ndi chuma/ndalama zilipo, liyenera kuthandiza anthu olemala ziwalo kapane amisala kuti anthe kupambana paumoyo wawo ndi kuthetsa mavuto akukumana nawo.

(3) Makamaka, Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha kumadera onse—

(a)

kuyambitsa ma purogiramu othandiza oplemala ziwalo zamthupi ndi a misala, makamaka poyambitsa ntchito zomwe angathe kudzigwira kapena kudzera ndi owayimilira;

(b) kuganizila zofunikira kwa anthu onse olemala pokonza dongosolo ya chitukuko;

(c)

kulimbikitsa mugwirizano wa chitukuko kwa anthu olemala ziwalo ndi amisala powaganizila;

(d) kulimbikitsa mabungwe ndi cholinga chokweza umoyo wa anthu olemala/ upuwala.

(4) Boma liyenela kuwonetsetsa kuti malo ndi nyumba za m’Boma, anthu olemala angathe kulowamo. 23

Ngwazi zinamenya nkhondo inabweretsa ufulu/mtendere

(1) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse liyenela kulemekeza ndi kupeleka ulemu kwa ngwazi zonse zinamenya nkhondo inabweretsa ufulu kuphatikizila—

(a)

onse anamenya nkhondo inabweretsa ufulu wa dziko;

(b) onse anathandizila omenya nkhondo;

(c)

onse anamangidwa, kutsekeledwa m’ndende pa nkhani za ndale pa nthawi ya nkhondo.

(2) Boma liyenera kupeza njira kuphatikizira malamulo, abweletsa chisamaliro ndi mphamvu ya chuma kwa ngwazi zonse zinamenya nkhondo ina bweretsa ufulu wa dziko. 24

Ntchito

(1) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse liyenera kutsatira malamulo, moyendelana ndi ndalama/zipangizo zilipo kupatsa mwayi kwa munthu aliyense kuti agwire ntchito mwa ufulu pofuna akhale bwino pamodzi ndi banja lake.

(2) Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse liyenera kuteteza.

(a)

Ntchito;

(b) kuthetsa machitidwe ndi kukaniza anthu kuti athe kugwira ntchito yokweza chuma cha dziko;

(c)

chitukuko cha maphunziro a ntchito kuphatikizira anthu olemala;

(d) kulimbikitsa njira zothandizira kusamalira mabanja powonetsetsa kuti akazi akupatsidwa mwayi wogwila ntchito. 25

Kuteteza mabanja

Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse liyenera kuteteza banja ndi kuwonetsetsa ngati ndalama/zipangizo zilipo, ayenera kupeza njira—

(a)

kupereka chithandizo popereka chithandizo kwa azimai, azibambo pamodzi ndi banja lonse amene akuyan’anira ana ndinso;

(b) kuteteza ndi kuthetsa mikangano ya m’mabanja. 26

Maukwati Boma liyenera kupeza njira zowonetsetsa kuti—

(a)

palibe ukwati wokwatilidwa popanda okwatirana kuvomeleza ukwatiwo;

(b) ana sakukwatilidwa;

(c)

kufanana kwa udindo kwa okwatilana m’banja ngakhale pakutha pa banjali ndiponso;

(d) ngati banja latha/kulekana kudzera kwa imfa kapena kukanana, mpofunikira kuteteza ana ndi mkazi. 27

Maphunzilo

(1) Boma liyenera kutsata njira zolimbikitsa—

(a)

Maphunzilo aulele kwa ana ndiponso;

(b) Maphunzilo apamwamba.

(2) Boma liyenera kuwonetsetsa kuti asikana akupatsidwa mwayi wofanana ndi anyamata pophunzira ku madela onse.

2013

Nyumba

Boma pamodzi ndi nthambi za m’dziko ndi mabungwe odziyimilira okha ku madela onse liyenera kuika malamulo ndi njila, moyenderana ndi ndalama/zipangizo zilipo, kuwonetsetsa kuti munthu aliyense ali ndi nyumba yokhalamo. 29

Zipatala

(1) Boma liyenela kuwonetseta kuti yamanga zipatala ku malo konse m’dziko lonse la Zimbabwe.

(2) Boma liyenera kuwonetsetsa kuti palibe munthu akukanidwa kupatsidwa chithandizo cha mankhwala ku zipatala zonse.

(3) Boma liyenela kupeza njira zofalitsira uthenga, malingana ndi ndalama/ zipangizo zilipo pophunzitsa anthu za matenda pofuna kupewa kufalikira kwa matenda. 30

Chithandizo kwa anthu

Boma liyenera kupeza njira, malingana ndi ndalama/zipangizo zilipo, kupereka chakudya ndi kusamalila anthu ovhutika. 31

Chithandizo mu zamalamulo ku anthu

Boma liyenela kupeza njira, moyendelana ndi ndalama/zipangizo zilipo, kuthandiza anthu akufuna kuimililidwa kwa ku bwalo la milandu kwa onse alibe ndalama zolipilira owayimilira ku bwalo la milandu. 32

Masewero ndi zipangi zosewelela

Boma liyenela kupeza njira zolimbikitsila masewero pokonza malo osewelela kwa anthu onse. 33

Kusunga ndi kusamalira nzeru za chikhalidwe cha makolo anthu

Boma liyenera kupeza njira zosungira, kuteteza ndi kulimbikitsa nzeru za makolo anthu kuphatikizila mankhwala pamodzi ndi nyama ndi kuteteza mitengo ili m’madela onse kokhala anthu. 34

Kulimbikitsidwa kwa malamulo okhudza maiko onse

Boma liyenela kuwonetsetsa kuti malamulo onse ovomelezedwa ndi maiko onse amene dziko la Zimbabwe linawalandira ayenera kuvomelezedwa ndi lamulo la m’dziko muno. MUTU 3 NZIKA ZA DZIKO


Nzika za dziko la Zimbabwe

(1) Anthu angathe kukhala Nzika za Zimbabwe kudzera pakubadwa, kapena polembetsedwa.

(2) Nzika zonse za m’dziko ziyenera kupatsidwa mwayi mofanana pa udindo, ubwino ndiponso zinthu zonse zimapatsidwa ku nzika zonse.

(3) Nzika zonse za dziko la Zimbabwe ziyenera kupatsidwa udindo ndi mwayi kuphatikizira ubwino uli m’malamulo.

(a)

kutezedwa ndi Boma kuli konse kumene ali;

(b) kukhala ndi passport pamodzi ndi makalata ena onse ogwiritsa ntchito pa ulendo ndiponso;

(c)

kukhala ndi makalata osonyeza kubadwa ndi zitupa zonse zimaperekedwa ndi Boma;

(4) Nzika zonse za dziko ziyenera kugwira ntchito izi, kuphatikizira zili m’malamulo—

(a)

kukhulupilika ku dziko la Zimbabwe;

(b) kuvhomeleza lamulo lalikululi ndi kulemekeza mabungwe onse;

(c)

kulemekeza mbendela ndi nyimbo ya fuko lathu ndiponso;

(d) kuteteza dziko la Zimbabwe ndi ulamulilo walo. 36

Nzika za kubadwa

(1) Munthu angathe kukhala Nzika za Zimbabwe ngati anabadwira m’dziko la Zimbabwe ndipo pa nthawi anabadwa—

(a)

mayi kapena bambo ake anali Nzika ya Zimbabwe pa nthawi yobadwa;

(b) agogo ake anali Nzika zomwe zinabadwa m’dziko la Zimbabwe.

(2) Anthu anabadwira ku dziko la kunja monga Nzika za m’Zimbabwe ngati, makolo awo onse alinali Nzika za Zimbabwe ndiponso.

(a) anthu onse okhala m’dziko la Zimbabwe;

(b) anthu akugwira ntchito ya Boma kapena ku mabungwe ali kunja kwa dziko.

(3) Mwana aliyense ali mu Zimbabwe, amene ali ndi zaka zochepera zaka khumi mphambu zisanu, amene makolo ake sakudziwika, ndi Nzika ya dziko pa kubadwa. 37

Nzika kudzera ku makolo

Malingana ndi chigawo 36(2) anthu anabadwira kunja kwa dziko la Zimbabwe ndi Nzika za Zimbabwe kudzera ku makolo awo ngati anabawa—

(a)

ngati makolo awo kapena agogo awo anabadwira kapena makolo awo anali Nzika za Zimbabwe kapena;

(b) ngati makolo awo zinali Nzika za Zimbabwe kudzera ndi kulembetsedwa. Ndiponso pa kubadwa mdziko la Zimbabwe moyendelana ndi malamulo a kulembetsedwa kwa maina akubadwa m’dziko. 38

Nzika kudzela ndi kulembetsedwa

(1) Munthu aliyense yemwe anakwatila Nzika yam Zimbabwe kwa zaka zoposa zisanu, amene akwanilitsa thambi ya m’nyumba ya malamulo ya (Act of Parliament) kudzela polembela mophempha kuti adzakale Nzika ya dziko la Zimbabwe.

(2) Munthu ali yense yemwe wakhala m’dziko la Zimbabwe kwa zaka zokwana khumi, yemwe akwanilitsa thambi ya m’nyumba ya malamulo ya (Act of Parliament) kudzela polembela mophempha kuti adzakale Nzika ya dziko la Zimbabwe.

(3) Mwana amene Sali wa dziko la Zimbabwe koma akukhala ndi Nzika za m’Zimbabwe kudzela polembela mophempha kuti adzakale Nzika ya dziko la Zimbabwe. 39

Kuthetsedwa kwa kukhala Nzika

(1) Kuthetsedwa kokhala Nzika ya Zimbabwe kudzera munjira yolembedwedwa—

(a)

2013

ngati munthu anakhala Nzika kudzera mu njira yochita katangale ndi ziphuphu, kupeleka umboni wa bodza, kucheka mfundo zolembedwa;

(b) pa nthawi ya nkhondo ya Zimbabwe, ngati munthuyo anapeleka uthenga kwa adani adziko kapena kuchita nawo malonda ngakale kuti ankachita nawo ma bizinesi ndi adani adziko pofuna kuthandizila adani pa nkhondo.

(2) Kuthetsedwa kukhala Nzika inabadwila m’dziko la Zimbabwe—

(a)

ngati Nzika anaupeza kudzela mu njira yochita katangale ndi ziphuphu, kupeleka umboni wa bodza, kucheka mfundo zolembedwa;

(b) malingana ndi chigawo 36(3) ngati Nzika za munthu kapena makolo ake akuziwika, kuti munthu anali Nzika ya dziko lina.

(3) Sipofunikira kuthetsa kukhala Nzika ya Zimbabwe malingana ndi chigawochi chifukwa munthu angathe kukhala opanda dziko lomuyimilila. 40

Kukhala zika posatengela kutha kwa ukwati Nzika ya Zimbabwe sithedwa kudzela ndi kutha kwa ukwati.


Bungwe la Nzika pamodzi ndi loyan’anila anthu olowa ndi kutuluka m’dziko

Moyendelana ndi lamolo lochokela ku nyumba ya malamulo payenela kukonzedwa bungwe loyan’anila anthu olowa ndi kutuluka m’dziko limene litsogoleledwa ndi wapampando pamodzi ndi thumwi ziwili zimene ziyenela kusankhidwa ndi mtsogoleri wa dziko, nthcito ya bungweli ndi—

(a)

kupatsa ndi kuthetsa kulembetsedwa kukhala Nzika za dziko;

(b) kuvhomeleza anthu pamodzi ndi Nzika zina kuti zizigwira ntchito ndi kugwira ntchito m’dziko la Zimbabwe poyika lamulo la kagwiridwe ka ntchito ndi kakhalidwe;

(c) 42

kugwira ntchito zina zimene zingapelekedwe ndi bungweli kapene nthambi ya nyumba ya malamulo.

Mphamvu ya nyumba ya malamulo moyendelana ndi Nzika

Nthambi ya nyumba ya malamulo liyenela kupeleka magwilidwe a nthito a chigawochi otele—

(a)

njira zimene anthu ayenela kutsata polembetsedwa kukhala Nzika za Zimbabwe;

(b) kusankha kukhala Nzika la Zimbabwe;

(c)

njila zothetsedwa kulembetsedwa kukhala Nzika za Zimbabwe;

(d) kakhazikitsidwe kokhala Nzika ya Zimbabwe;


(e)

kukanizidwa kwa munthu kuti akhale Nzika za maiko awili kudzela polemetsedwa kapena makolo;

(f)

kupereka zoyenela ku chigawochi.

Kupitiliza kukhala Nzika za dziko

(1) Munthu aliyense, emwe anali Nzika dziko la Zimbabwe pambuyo posindikidwa kwa lamuloli, ayenela kukhalanso Nzika ya Zimbabwe.

(2) Munthu aliyense yemwe anabadwa mu Zimbabwe pamene lamuloli linasindikizidwa ndi Nzika ya Zimbabwe pakubadwa ngathi—

(a)

m’modzi wa makolo ake kapena makolo ake onse anali Nzika za dziko yomwe ili mu nthambi loyan’anila chitukuko cha maiko akuchigawo cha kumwela cha Southern African Development Community pagwilizano unasainidwa ku mzinda wa Windhoek mdziko la Namibia tsiku la 17 August mchaka cha 1992 ndipo;

(b) ali Nzika ya Zimbabwe pamene lamuloli linasindikizidwa. MUTU 4 CHIGAWO CHA UDINDO NDI UFULU

GAWO 1 KUMASULILIDWA KWA CHIGAWO 4


Kulemekezedwa kwa udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu

Boma pamodzi ndi munthu aliyense, kuphatikizila anthu a lamulo, ndi mabungwe onse ndi nthambi zonse za m’dziko ku madela onse ayenela kulemekeza, kuteteza ndi kulimbikitsa udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu moyendelana ndi chigawo-chi.


Ntchito ya chigawo 4

(1) Mutu umenewu ukugwila pamodzi Boma ndi Utsogoleli wa pamwamba, wokonza malamulo ndi wogwila ntchito kumabwalo a milandu ndi zina zigawo ndi utumikili wa Boma kuli konse.

(2) Mutu umenewu ukugwila anthu abadwa muno ndo omwe anapatsidwa nzika pa lamulo pofika pakuti akuwugwilitsa ntchito, akuyang’ana pa ufulu wokhudzidwa ndi udindo wopatsidwa.

(3) Anthu opeza ufulu pa lamulo ndi anthu obadwila muno onse ayenela kupeza ma ufulu ndi kumasuka zopezeka mu mutu umenewu kufika pakuti zonsezi ziyenela kupatsidwa ku anthu wa. 46

Kumasulilidwa kwa chigawo 4

(1) Pomasulira chigawo-chi, bwalo la milandu, ozenga milandu—

(a)

ayenela kulemekeza udindo ndi ufulu uli m’chigawo-chi;

(b) ayenela kulimbikitsa mfundo kukhudza anthu onse kudzera muzochitika poyera, mwachilungamo, umunthu, mofanana ndi mwa ufulu moyendelana ndi zolinga zina ikidwa mu chigawo 3;

(c)

ayenela kutsatila malamulo anakhazikidzidwa a maiko onse apansi lino pamodzi ndi zivhomelezano zimene dziko la Zimbabwe linavhomeleza;

(d) ayenela kulemekeza zigawo zonse zili m’buku la lamulo lalikulu la m’dzikoli makamaka zolinga zili mu chigawo 2 ndiponso;

(e)

kutsatila malamulo ena akunja.

owonjezela mfundo zina zoyenela pofuna kumasulila lamulo la likululi.

(2) Pomasulira lamuloli mfundo zina ziyenela kutsatilidwa pomasulila lamulo lalikululi. Pomasulia Lamuloli ndi kutukula Lamulo logwila anthu onse ndi Lamulo la chikhalidwe, bwalo lililonse, kapena chiyani ayenela kulimbikitsa ndikutsogoleledwa ndi chigawochi.

2013

Chigawo 4 sichivomeleza maudindo ena

Mutu umenewu siwukusokoneza kukhala kwa ena maufulu ndi kumasuka zomwe zikuzindikilidwa kapena kupatsidwa ndi lamulo, kufika pakuti zikugwilizana ndi Lamulo Lalikulu limeneli. GAWO 2 UDINDO NDI UFULU WA ANTHU


Udindo wokhala ndi umoyo

(1) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo wokhala ndi moyo.

(2) Lamulo likhoza kuloleza kuti munthu atemeledawe chilango chakuphedwa ngati apha munthu mwa dala ndipo:

(a) Lamuloli liyenela kupatsa mpata bwalo lozenge mulanduwu ufulu wogamula kuphedwa kapena ayi;

(b) chigamulochi chiyenela kupelekedwa moyendelana ndi bwalo la mulandu limene ukuzenga mlanduwu;

(c)

chigamulo chophedwachi sichiyenera kupelekedwa kwa munthu;

(i) amene ali zaka zosafika makumi awili ndi chimodzi (21 years) pa nthawi munthuyu apalamula mulandu kapena;

(ii) amene ali ndi zaka zoposa makumi asanu ndi awili (70 years);

(d) chigamulo cha kuphedwachi chiyenela kutemeledwa kwa munthu wa mukazi ndiponso;

(e) munthu atemelemedwa chigamulo chophedwa/cha imfa ayenela kupempha kukhululukilidwa kuchokera kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko;

(3) lamulo loteteza ana asanabadwe liyenela kuyika njila yolola kuti munthu achotse pakati, moyendelana ndi lamulo. 49

Udindo ndi ufulu wokhala momasuka

(1) munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo ndi ufulu wokhala womasuka kuphatikizila—

(a)

kutsekeledwa mundende popanda kuweluzidwa kapena;

(b) kupondelezedwa udindo ndi ufulu wake popanda chifukwa;

(2) Kutsekeledwa mu ndende chifukwa cholephela kukwanilitsa mfundo ina ili yonse. 50

Udindo wa anthu momangidwa kapena kutsekeledwa m’ndende

(1) munthu aliyense amene amangidwa—

(a)

ayenela kudziwitsidwa chifukwa chake chomangilidwa kapena kutsekeledwa mu’ndende;

(b) ayenela kuloledwa mwa msanga—

(i) kuloledwa kulankhula ndi mwamuna kapane mukazi wake ngakhale m’bale wake kapena loya wake ndipo Boma liyenela kulipila;

(ii) kupatsidwa chilolezo chozisankhila loya kapana dokotala wake yemwe alipilidwa ndi iye;

(c)

ayenela kugwidwa mwachilungamo ndi moyenela;

(d) ngati mulandu wake usanazengedwe ndi kuweluzidwa ayenela kumasulidwa popanda zifukwa ziti akhale ali m’ndende;

(e)

ayenela kupatsidwa mwayi wolimbana kapena kutsutsana ndi kumangidwa kapena kutsekeledwa kwake m’ndende ku bwalo la milandu.

(2) Munthu aliyense amene amangidwa kapena kutsekeledwa m’ndende—

(a)

ndi cholinga choti azengedwe mulandu;

(b) poganizilidwa kuti analakwa ayenela kuima pa bwalo la milandu mukati mwa maola makumi anai ndi asanu ndi atatu (48hrs) posatengela kuti maolawa akutha tsiku loweluka, lamulungu kapena ndi tsiku la tchuti.

(3) Ngati patha maola makumi anayi ndi asanu ndi atatu asanazengedwe mulandu wake moyendelana ndi chigawo 2 ayenela kumasulidwa pokha pokha ngati bwalo la mulandu ndi imene yalamula kuti akhale ali m’ndende.

(4) Munthu aliyense yemwe amangidwa kapena kutsekeledwa mu ndende pa mulandu wake ali ndi udindo ndi ufulu wo—

(a)

khala chete;

(b) kuziwisidwa moyenela;

(i) Udindo ndi ufulu wake wokhala chete;

(ii) Kuipa kokhala chete ndi kusakhala chete;

(c)

saumilizidwa kavhomeleza kapana kukana;

(d) ngati munthu amangidwa kapena kutsekeledwa ku ndende ayenela kuzengedwa mulandu wake kumene ayenela kumasulidwa kapena kutsekeledwa.

(5) Munthu aliyense womangidwa kapena anatsekeledwa ali ndi undindo—

(a)

kudziwisidwa chifukwa chake atsekeledwa;

(b) ayenela kusankha loya wake amene atha kumulipila pomuziwisa za udindo ndi ufulu wake;

(c)

ayenela kuziwisa achibale ake ndipo abalewo ayenela kubwera kuzamuona ndi—

(i) mukazi kapena mwamuna wake ngakhale mzake;

(ii) wachibale;

(iii) abusa kapena wamkulu wampingo amene iye amusankha;

(iv) loya amene amusankha;

(v) dotolo wosankhidwa;

(vi) posatengela chiteetezo choikidwa ndi akundende aliyense munthu akufuna ayenela kubwera kuzamuona;

(d) malo omwe munthu atsekeledwa ayenela kukhala abwino ku umunthu, okhala ndi malo chitila masewela ndipo Boma iyenela kulipila malo okhala ndi zimbuzi, pali ndi ukhondo, zakudya zabwino, pokhala ndi zipangizo zowelenga komanso pali ndi mankhwala ndipo;

(e)

ayenela kutsutsana ndi kutsekeledwa kwake ku bwalo la milandu ndipo ngati palibe mulandu ayenela kumasulidwa.

(6) Munthu aliyense kutsekeledwa podikilira kuti azengedwe mlandu, ndipo sakuzengedwa pa nthawi yoyenela afunikila kuti atulutsidwe ndi kumasulidwa popanda zifukwa—

(a) ndipo ayenela kuza zengedwa mulandu wake;

(b) sayenela kusokoneza kafukufuku akuchitika pa nkhani yake;

(c)

sayenela kupalamula mulandu wina woyamba usati wa zengedwaMunthu aliyense ali ndi udindo ndi ufulu kupita kuka dandaula ku bwalo la milandu kupempha kuti munthu wotsekeledwayo atulutsidwe ndi ku masulidwa.

(7) Pakakhala chifukwa chilichense chotanthauzila kuti munthu akumangidwa zakunja kwa lamulo, kapena ndikovuta kuti apeze komwe munthuyo ali, munthuyo apatsidwe thandizo kuchokela ku bwalo lapamwamba zolenga milandu—

(a)

pempho lopelekedwa ku bwalo la milandu lopempha kumasulidwa kwa munthu otsekeledwa kuti ayenela kuzengedwa mulandu wake ngakhale kufunsa kuti anatsekeledwelanji;

(b) kulamulidwa kuti munthu wotsekeledwayo amasulidwe chifukwa anatsekeledwa moswa lamulo ndipo bwalo la mulandu lidzagamula mulanduwu moyendelana.

(8) Ndipo kumangidwa kapena kutsekeledwa kotsutsana ndi chigawochi ndi kosavhomelezeka.

(9) Munthu aliyense yemwe angamangidwe kapena kutsekeledwa zotsavhomelezedwa ndi lamulo ayenela kulipilidwa ndi munthu anampatsa mulandu ndiponso lamulo lingathe kuteteza munthu malingana ndi chigawochi mu munjila izi—

(a)

wozenga mulandu amene akuzenga mulandu bwino;

(b) wogwila ntchito yozenga mulandu bwino ndipo wodziwa nthito yake. 51

Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo pamodzi ndi ufulu wolemekezedwa

Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo pamodzi ndi ufulu wolemekezedwa ndi kutetezedwa muchikhalidwe chake. 52

Udindo wotetezedwa Munthu aliyense ali ndi ufulu pamodzi ndi maudindo monga—

(a)

kutetezedwa ku chipolowe chilichonse chingachitike;

(b) kupereka maganizo a nkhani za kulela, potsata zigawo zonse zili mu lamulo lalikulu la dzikoli;

(c) 53

kukana kugwilitsidwa ntchito matupi awo pachita kafufuku wa mankhwala popanda kufunsidwa.

Munthu ali ndi ufulu wosavhutitsidwa, kupatsidwa chilango kapena kugwidwa ngati simuthu

Munthu aliyense sayenela kuvhutitsidwa, kulangidwa kapena kanza ngakhale kupondelezedwa. 54

Ufulu wosakhala kapolo

Palibe munthu yemwe adzatengedwe ngati kapolo kapena wantchito wosalipidwa ngakhale kugwiritsidwa ntchito yokakamizidwa. 55

Ufulu wosakakamizidwa pakugwira ntchito Palibe munthu yemwe adzaumilizidwe kugwila ntchito mokakamizidwa.


Anthu onse ndi ofanana ndipo mposafunikila kupondelezana

(1) Anthu onse ndi ofanana mwa malamulo ndipo ali ndi udindo wotetezedwa ndi kupatsidwa phindu lofanana.

(2) Mwamuna kapena mukazi ali ndi udindo wofanana pa nkhani za ndale, chuma, chikhalidwe ndi umoyo wawo.

(3) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo ndi ufulu wosapondelezedwa ndi kusankhidwa chifukwa cha mtundu, kungu, malo anabadwila, chikhalidwe chake, kapena chifukwa choti ndi wa mukazi kapena kuti wa mwamuna, chipemphezo chake, wokwatila kapena sanakwatile, ali ndi mimba, kapena kuti ndi wopuwala/wolemala, ali kapena chikhalidwe chake pa nkhani ya chuma ngakhale kuti anabadwila m’banja kapena sanabadwile m’banja.

(4) Munthu angathe kusankhidwa moyendelana ndi chigawo 3 chili pamwambachi ngati—

(a)

malingana ndi chikhalidwe chake, kuletsedwa kapena ndi wopuwala/ wolemala kotelo kuti asiyana ndi anzake;

(b) ena mwa anthuwa amapatsidwa mwayi umene ena alibe chifukwa cha chikhalidwe chawo.

(5) Kusankhidwa mu njila yafotokozedwa mu chigawo 3 nkosaloledwa pokhapokha nhati kusankhidwaku kufotokozedwe poyela, mwachilungamo, mwa ufulu ndi molemekezana ndinso mofanana.

(6) Boma liyenela kuika malamulo pamodzi ndi kulimbikitsa njila zina zothandizila kubweletsa ufanana ndi kuteteza anthu avhutikawa pothetsa kupondelezedwa ndi kusankhidwa ndipo—

(a) njilazi ziyenela kuthandizila pali pofunikila;

(b) njila zotelezi siziyenela kuonedwa ngati sizili bwino malingana ndi chigawo 3. 57

Udindo wa chinsisi Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo, umene ukuphatikiza izi.

(a) kuti anthu sayenela kulowa m’nyumba kapena pa bwalo popanda chilolezo;

(b) kuti aliyense, za m’nyumba ndi katundu wake azifukulidwa popanda chilolezo;

(c)

kuti katundu wake alandidwa ndia anthu ena;

(d) kuti nkhani zake za chinsisi zilankhulidwe;

(e) 58

kuulula za umoyo ndi matenda ake;

Ufulu wosonkhana ndi kulowa m’mabungwe

(1) Munthu ali yense ali ndi udindo ndi ufulu wosonkhana ndi aliyense komanso kulowa m’mabungwe akufuna, ndiponso akhoza kusasonkhana ndi kulowa m’mabungwe.

(2) Palibe munthu aletsedwa kulowa m’bungwe kapena kusonkana ndi anthu ena. 59

Ufulu wochita chiwonetselo ndi kupeleka pempho

Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo wa ufulu wochita chiwonetselo pamodzi ndi kupeleka pempho ku Boma, koma udindowu uyenela kuchitika mwa mtendele. 60

Ufulu womasuka pa kulankhula chili chonse

(1) Munthu aliyense ali ndi ufulu womasuka pa kulankhula chili chonse ndipo ufulu-wu ukuphatikizila—

(a) ufulu wopeleka maganizo, wachipemphelo kapena chikhulupililo ndinso;

(b) ufulu wopeleka maganizo, chipemphelo kapena chikhalidwe, kaya pali anthu kapena pachinsisi, uli wekha kaya kuti pali anthu ambiri.

(2) Palibe munthu awumulizidwa kulumbila motsutsana ndi chipembezo kapena chikhalidwe kapena kulumbila motsutsana ndi chipemphelo ndi chikhulupililo chake.

(3) Makolo pamodzi ndi achibale ali ndi ana ali ndi udindo wotsogolela chikhulupililo chawo, chikhalidwe ndi chipembezo cha wo pofuna kuti ana akule bwino, posatsutsana ndi udindo wa ana unapelekedwa mu lamulo lalikulu lino umene ukuphatikizila udindo wophunzila, wa umoyo woteteza ndi wa umoyo.

(4) Anthu opemphela angathe kuyambitsa mabungwe achipembezo ngakhale kuti mabungwe otelewa angathe kupatsidwa chithandizo cha ndalama kuchokela ku Boma. 61

Ufulu wolankhula ndi ufulu wa wolemba nkhani

(1) Munthu aliyense ali ndi ufulu wolankhula, umene ukuphatikizila –

(a)

ufulu wofufuza, kumva nkhani ndi kupeleka maganiza pamodzi ndi mauthenga ena;

(b) ufulu wakafukufuku ka nkhani za sayansi ndi luso lopeka maganizo;

(c)

ufulu wophunzila.

(2) Munthu aliyense ali ndi ufulu womva mauthenga, kuphatikizila ufulu woteteza nkhani za atolankhani.

(3) Ufulu wokhazikitsa nyumba zoulutsira mawu ndi mauthenga, potsata njira za Boma zinaikidwa zopelekela chilolezo monga—

(a)

mphempo zotenga mauthenga mu mlengalenga;

(b) chodziimilira potetezedwa ndi Boma, ndale kapena malonda.

(4) Nyumba zoulutsila mau ndi kusindikiza mauthenga za Boma ziyenela—

(a)

kukhala zoziimilila pankhani zosankha mauthenga owulutsidwa;

(b) kukhala zosakondela poulutsa nkhani ndi mauthenga;

(c)

kupeleka mwayi kwa anthu kupeleka maganizo awo;

(5) Ufulu wa ankhani ndi mauthenga sulola izi—

(a)

kulimbikitsa chipolowe;

(b) kulimbikitsa mawu odana kapena osemphanitsa anthu;

(c)

mawu otsitsa chikhalidwe cha umunthu;

(d) kuswa udindo wa mchinsisi cha munthu; 62

Udindo wokumva nkhani ndi mauthenga

(1) Nzika zonse za m’Zimbabwe pamodzi ndi okhala m’dziko ali ndi udindo wakumva nkhani ndi kuwelenga mauthenga kuchokela ku Boma kapena ku bungwe, kapena nthambi za Boma ku madela onse ndi cholinga choti anthu adziwe zomwe zikuchitika.

(2) Nzika zonse za m’Zimbabwe pamodzi ndi okhala m’dziko ali ndi udindo wakumva nkhani ndi kuwelenga mauthenga kuchokela ku Boma kapena ku bungwe, kapena nthambi za Boma ku madela onse ndi cholinga choteteza maudindo awo.

(3) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo wokonza mauthenga kapena nkhani zatulutsidwa molakwika ku mabungwe ndi nthambi za Boma kuchokera ku madela onse ngati nkhani pamodzi ndi mauthengawo akumukhudza iye.

(4) Malamulo ayenela kuikidwa oteteza udindo-wu ngakhale kuti mpofunikira kuteteza anthu ndi chinsisi moyendelana kuti zonsezi zikuchitika poyera, mwalamulo ndi mwa ufulu pofuna kulemekeza umunthu.

Chilankhulo ndi chikhalidwe Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo—

(a)

wolankhula chilankhulo chomwe akufuna;

(b) kuchita chikhalidwe chomwe afuna ndipo palibe munthu angachite maudindowa angatsutsane ndi chigawochi; koma palibe munthu emwe angafune kutsatila ndi kusangalala ma ufulu amenewa pa njila yosakomela zopezeka pa mutu umenewu. 64

Ufulu wa ntchito ndi malonda

Munthu alliyense ali ndi udindo wosankha ndi kugwira ntchito akufuna pamodzi ndi kuchita malonda pokha pokha ngati zikuyendelana ndi malamulo. 65

Maudindo a ntchito

(1) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo wogwira ntchito bwino ndi kutetezedwa pa ntchitopo ndipo ayenela kulandila malipilo ake oyenela.

(2) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo woyambitsa kapena kulowa m’mabungwe oyan’anila nkhani za ogwila ntchito kapena olemba ntchito, amene iye akufuna ndi kutsatila zochitika m’mabungwemu mwalamulo kupatula onse ogwila ntchito za chitetezo m’dziko.

(3) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo wonyanyala kugwira ntchito, kudzela pokhala osagwira ntchito, komanso lamulo limapatsa mpata wa udindo kuti ogwira ntchito yofunikira kuti asanyanyale kupatula onse ogwila ntchito za chitetezo m’dziko.

(4) Munthu aliyense ayenela kugwila ntchito bwino ndi mokondwa.

(5) Kupatula onse ogwila ntchito za chitetezo m’dziko, ogwila ntchito aliyense, kaya ndi olemba anthu ntchito, bungwe loyang’anila anthcito, mabungwe onse ali ndi udindo—

(a)

wokambilana pa nkhani za malipilo;

(b) kusonkhanitsa ndi;

(c)

kuyambitsa ndi kulowa m’mabungwe ndi nthambi zoyan’anila ntchito.

(6) Akazi pamodzi ndi amuna ali ndi udindo wofanana malipilo pa ntchito yofanana.

(7) Akazi ali ndi udindo wolipilidwa malipilo pa nthawi apita ku tchuti yokalela mwana abadwa kwa myezi itatu. 66

Ufulu wa mayendedwe ndi kakhalidwe

(1) Nzika yonse ya m’dziko la Zimbabwe ili ndi.

(a)

udindo wolowa m’dziko la Zimbabwe;

(b) kutetezedwa pochoka m’dziko la Zimbabwe ndipo;

(c)

udindo wokhala ndi passport kuphatikizila makalata ena onse ogwilitsa ntchito pa ulendo.

(2) Nzika zonse za M’Zimbabwe pamodzi ndi aliyense akukhala m’dziko La Zimbabwe lino ali ndi udindo—

(a)

woyenda momasuka ndi mwaufulu m’Zimbabwe;

(b) kukhala kumadela konse m’Zimbabwe muno ndipo;

(c)

kutuluka kupita ku maiko akunja kwa Zimbabwe.

2013

Udindo wa ndale

(1) Nzika zonse m’Zimbabwe muno zili ndi udindo.

(a)

kuchita chisankho momasuka moyendelana ndi lamulo lalikulu la dziko kapena malamulo ena onse;

(b) kusankha ndale momasuka mwa ufulu.

(2) Malingana ndi lamulo lalikululi, Nzika ili yonse ya m’Zimbabwe ali ndi udindo—

(a)

kukonza, kulowa ndi kutenga mbali pa nkhani za ndale ngakhale kuyambitsa bungwe lake momasuka;

(b) kuchititsa misonkhano ya ndale momasuka ndi mwaufulu;

(c)

kusonkhana nawo pa misonkhano ya ndale mwa mtendele;

(d) kusonkhana pa misonkhano ya ndale ndicholinga chothandizila ndale kapena malamulo a Boma kapena aliwonse iye afuna.

(3) Malingana ndi lamulo lalikululi, Nzika ili yonse ya m’Zimbabwe imene ili ndi zaka khumi ndi mphambu zisanu ndi zitatu (18 years) ili ndi udindo(a)

kuponya voti mwachinsisi mu zisankho zonse zingachitike m’dziko yoyendelana ndi lamulo lalikululi;

(b) kuima nawo pa chisankho ndipo ngati wasankhidwa, kugwira ntchitoyo.

(4) Pofuna kulimbikitsa kukonzedwa kwa zipani zosiyanasiyana, lamulo la mnyumba ya malamulo la (Act of Parliament) liyenela kuthandizila ndi ndalama zipani zonse zakonsedwazo. 68

Udindo woyanjanilitsidwa mwa lamulo

(1) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo woyanjanitsidwa mwalamulo popanda kukondela.

(2) Ngati udindo wa munthu aliyense wasokonezedwa poyanjanitsidwa ali ndi ufulu wopatsidwa mwayi kuti alembe zifukwa zomwe sanakondwele nazo.

(3) Lamulo la nyumba ya malamulo liyenela kulimbikitsa ma udindowa ndipo.

(a)

kupatsa mpata bwalo la milandu kuti liwonetsetse ngati chigamulo chinapelekedwa bwino kapena ayi;

(b) ndi ntchito ya Boma kulimbikitsa maudindo moyendelana ndi muzigawo 1 ndi 2 ndipo;

(c) 69

Iimbikitse maweluzo ndi njila zogwila nazo ntchito.

Udindo wa chigamulo choyenela

(1) Munthu aliyense yemwe ali ndi mulandu wake ali ndi udindo woti uzengedwe bwino mosakondela mwachangu ku bwalo la milandu la Boma asanapita kukafunafuna loya wake.

(2) Malingana ndi maudindo, munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo woti mlandu wake uzengedwe bwino ku bwalo la milandu popanda kukondela asanakasume ku mabwalo ena amilandu.

(3) Munthu aliyense ali ndi ufulu kapena udindo wokasuma mulandu wake ku bwalo la milandu ili yense.

(4) Munthu aliyense ali ndi ufulu woyimililidwa ndi loya lake ku bwalo la milandu.

Udindo wa munthu wozengedwa

(1) Munthu ali yense yemwe akuzengedwa mulandu alinso ndi ufulu wakuti.

(a)

ndiopanda mulandu kufikila bwalo la zenga kuti ndi wolakwa;

(b) ayenela kudziwisidwa za chigamulo chapelekedwa ndi bwalo la milandu kuti athe kuyankha;

(c)

ayenela kupatsidwa nthawi yokwana kuti athe kuziteteza poyankha;

(d) kusankha loya womuyimilila pa nkhani yake ku bwalo la mulandu;

(e)

kuyimilidwa ndi loya la Boma pa mulandu wake ngati chigamulo chinapelekedwa sichinayende bwino;

(f)

kuziwitsidwa za udindo wake malingana ndi ndine (d) ndi (e);

(g) kukhala pa bwalo lamulandu pamene mulandu wake ukuzengedwa;

(h) kutsutsana ndi umboni ukupelekedwa pa mulandu wake;

(i)

kukana umboni womwe akutsutsana nawo ngakhale kukhala chete ndi cholinga kuti apatsidwe mwayi wopeleka mfundo zake;

(j)

kuti mulandu uzenge dwe muchilankhulo chake chomwe amvetsetsa bwino;

(k) kukana chigamulo chopelekedwa ndi bwalo la milandu ngati iye palibe pomwe mulandu uzengedwa;

(l)

sayenela kumangidwa pa mulandu pamene palibe kulakwa;

(m) sayenela kuzengedwa mulandu umene anakhululukilidwa kale;

(n) ayenela kutsekeledwa kwa masiku/zaka zochepa pa chilango chake ngati chilango chake chinasinthidwa pa nthawi anapalamula mulandu;

(2) Ngati munthu akufunikira kuuzidwa uthenga malingana ndi chigawo-chi—

(a)

uthengawo uyenela kulankhulidwa mu chilankhulo chomwe munthu amvetsetsa ndipo;

(b) ngati munthu sathe kuwelenga kapena kulemba, ayenela kuuzidwa zonse zalembedwa ndipo ayenela kumvetsetsa.

(3) Pa mulandu wakupha wuli wonse, umboni uliwonse wopezeka ndikupatsidwa mosagwilizana ndi mutu umenewu uyenela kuyikidwa padela ngathi kupatsidwa kwa umboni umenewu kudzasokoneze ndikuchita kuti mulandu usatuluke bwino kapena kuletsa kayendesedwe kabwino ka chilungamo ndi zoyang’anilidwa ndi anthu onse.

(4) Munthu aliyense amene azengedwa mulandu wake, ali ndi udindo, atalipila ndalama zokhazikitsidwa ndi lamulo, wopatsidwa malemba a momwe mulandu wake unayendela pakazengedwe kake pa nthawi yoyenela mbuyo makupatsidwa kwa gamulo ya mulandu wake.

(5) Munthu aliyense emwe azengedwa mulandu wake ndikupezeka ndi mulandu ali ndi udindo, patayanganidwa zonse za lamulo, kuti—

(a) mulandu wake ukazengedweso ndi bwalo la milandu la Lapamwamba kapena;

(b) kukasumanso ku bwalo la milandu yapamwamba motsutsana ndi chigamulo chapelekedwa; 71

Udindo wa zinthu

(1) Muchigawochi— “Ndalama zaPensheni” ndi ndalama kapena zolemekedwa nazo popita kukapuma ndiye ikupatsidwa—

(a)

2013

munthu kuchokela ku nthambi yosunga ndalama zaBoma;

(b) ndalama zimapelekedwa kuchokela ku kampani za munthu wogwila ntchito;

(c)

ndalama zimapelekedwa kwa munthu pambuyo podwala matenda kapena kuvholala;

(d) ndalama zopelekedwa pambuyo popuma kugwila ntchito kamba kokula kapena kudwala ngakhale zifukwa zina; “Zinthu” izi ndizinthu zonse zoikidwa ndinso udindo kapena kukhala ndi chidwi cha zinthu uli nazo.

(2) Malingana ndi chigawo 72, munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo/ufulu wokhala ndi zinthu, kuchokela ku mbali zonse za dziko la Zimbabwe kaya kugula, kugwilitsa ntchito, kupatsa anthu ena kuti agwilitse ntchito, zinthu zingathe kukhala zake kapena za mgwilizano ndi ena.

(3) Malingana ndi chigawo 72, palibe munthu angaletsedwa kukhala ndi zinthu kupatula kuti—

(a)

kutengeledwa zinthu pa lamulo lokhudza zosiyana-siyana;

(b) kutengeledwa ndikofunikila chifukwa cha zotsatilazi—

(i) pofuna kuteteza, kakhalidwe kabwini, kuzigwila, zaumoyo ndi tauni ndi kulongosola malo osiyana-siyana;

(ii) pofuna kutukula kapena kugwilitsa ntchito malo amenewo kapena ena pachifukwa chofuna kutukula malo amenewa;

(c)

lamulo limafuna kuti nthambi yogula zosiyana-siyana;

(i) kupeleka zifukwa zotenga malo paganizo lofuna kutenga malo ku munthu emwe angavutisidwe ndi zimenezi;

(ii) kupepesedwa ndi ndalama pa malo onse atengedwa pofuna kugwilitsidwa ntchito;

(iii) ngati pali kukanganilana, bwalo loyanjanitsa liyenela kupeleka chigamulo, malowo asanayambe kugwilitsidwa ntchito pasanathe masiku makumi atatu;

(d) lamulo limapatsa mphamvu munthu aliyense amene ali ndi malo kusuma ku bwalo la milandu kupempha kuti abwezeledwe malo ake ndipo ngati bwalo la milandu silivhomeleza ndipo;

(e)

lamulo limalola mwini malo kuti akusume ku bwalo la milandu kuti—

(i) malo ake ndi mtengo wake;

(ii) makalata oyenela a malowa;

(iii) ndalama zachipepeso cha malo; Ndipo ayenela akasume ku bwalo la milandu kuti apatsidwe.

(4) Ngati malo a munthu, ndalama zake zikuyendetsedwa ku penjeni, udindo wotele uyenela kuimisidwa moyendelana ndi chigawo 3, pofuna kuti malowo atengedwe. 72

Udindo wa malo aulimi

(1) Malingana ndi chigawochi— “malo a ulimi” kutanthauza kuti malo ogwilitsidwa ntcihto mu ulimi uli wonse wakudimba, wa mitengo, ulimi wa nsomba kapena ulimi wa ziweto kuphatikizila—

(a)

ulimi wa ziweto, nyama za mtchile/nkhalango, nkhuku, nyama kapena njuchi kapenanso;

(b) malo odyesela ziweto kapena nyama za mtchile kupatula malo okhala anthu kapena malile a madela am’tauni “malo” akuphatikizila malo a ulimi “malo ogawidwila ntchito za ulimi” kutanthauza kuti malo anaikidwa padela mwa lamulo kuchokela ku bungwe la Deeds Registry. Koma siyikuphatikiza ko nthaka yakumidzi kapena nthaka yopezeka pa malire a konsolo yakutawuni kapena mkati ma tawuni yokhazikitsidwa pa lamulo lodalila ku dongosolo yatawuni kapena monga zikulongosoledwa pa lamulo loyang’anila za nthaka. “nthaka” kuphatikiza chili chonse chogwilika chilipo kapena china chomwe chikukula pa nthaka; “malo a nthaka yolima” kunena kuti malo a nthaka yolima yozindikilidwa ngati nthambi ya nthaka ya zaulimi yokhazikitsidwa ku ofesi ya Uweni ndi Umwini wa nthaka.

(2) Kudela kuli malo amene akufuna kugwilitsidwa ntchito yothandiza anthu monga—

(a)

malo ogwilitsidwa ntchito ya ulimi ndi ntchito zina;

(b) malo osungila zinthu za chilengedwe, olima mitengo ndi ntchito zina zonse;

(c)

malo okhalitsidwa anthu (Mudzi) anatsamutsidwa ndi cholinga cha chifukwa chinafotokozedwa mu gawo (a) kapena (b) malo angathe kutengedwa ndi Boma posindikiza chiziwiso mu buku la Boma la Gazette limene liyenela kufotokoza nthawi malowo akufunikila kuyambila pa tsiku lasindikizidwa m’bukuli.

(3) Ngati malo aulimi otele akufunidwa kutengedwa moyendelana ndi chigawo 2.

(a)

malingana ndi chigawo 295(1) ndi (2) palibe ndalama zachipepeso zingalipilidwe;

(b) palibe munthu angathe kukasuma ku bwalo la milandu kufunsa ndalama za chipepeso za malowa ndipo palibe bwalo la milandu lingavomelezekusumidwaku;

(c)

ndipo kutengedwa kwa malo otelewa palibe angatsutsana nawo ku bwalo la milandu malingana ndi chigawo 56;

(4) Malo onse aulimi omwe—

(a)

anaikidwa mwa mundandanda wa chisanu ndi chiwilic ha lamulo lalikulu la dziko lakale kapena;

(b) tsiku lisanakhazikitsidwe, malo anasankhidwa moyendelana ndi chigawo 16B (2)(a) (ii) kapena (iii) kuchokela ku lamulo lalikulu la dziko lakale. Ndipo malo-wa akadali m’manja mwa Boma ndipo palibe ndalama za chipepeso zingapelekedwe.

(5) Pa nthawi yoyenela mbuyo mwakutengedwa kwa nthaka ya zaulimi malinga ndi ichigawo (2), munthu emwe ali ndi ntchito yolembetsa zaUweni wa nthaka ayenela, popanda chidziwitso chili chonse, kuchita kuti makalata a Uweni atuluke ndikulembedwa mu buku kuchitila kuti makalatawa ayimisidwe ndikulemba Uweni wa Boma pa nthaka imeneyi.

(6) Lamulo limaletsa munthu aliyense kukhala ku malo aulimi a Boma;

(7) Malingana ndi kutengedwa kwa malo olima ndi cholinga chokhalitsa anthu ku malo atsopano, mfundo zotsatilizi ziyenela kuganizilidwa kuti ndi zofunikila—

(a)

2013

pansi pa ulamulilo wa chitsamunda wopendelezedwa anthu am’Zimbabwe analandidwa malo ndi minda yawo popanda kulandila ndalama zowapepesa;

(b) kotelo kuti anthu anamenya nkhondo ndi cholinga kufuna malo ndi minda yawo komanso kuti pakhale kudzilamulila pa nkhani za ndale, ndipamene panabwela ufulu wodzilamulila wa dziko la Zimbabwe m’chaka cha 1980;

(c)

anthu a m’dziko la Zimbabwe ayenela kubweletsa udindo/ufulu wawo potenga malo ndi minda yawo Ndipo moyenela—

(i) ndi atsamunda akalewa ayenela kulipila ndalama zopepesa pa minda yonse inakhalitsidwa anthu ndi thumba la ndalama inaikidwa padela ya ntchitoyi;

(ii) ndipo ngati ulamulilo wa chitsamunda ulephela kulipila ndalamazi, Boma la dziko la Zimbabwe silingapeleke ndalama ku malo ndi minda yonse inatengedwa ndi cholinga chokhalitsa anthu ku malo atsopano.

(8) Chigawo sichitsatila kulephela kumene ulamulilo wa boma la asamunda unachita posalipila ndalama za malo ndi minda imene ikufotokozedwa mu chigawochi. 73

Udindo wa zinthu za chilengedwe

(1) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo/ufulu—

(a)

wokhala ku malo a bwino pa umoyo wake ndipo;

(b) malo achilengedwe ayenela kukhala otetezedwa pofuna kuti azathandize anthu ndiponso m’badwa wa anthu adzabadwe m’tsogolo poikidwa malamulo pamodzi ndi njila zimene;

(i) zingateteze malo pamodzi ndi mitsinje pamodzi ndi zonse zokhala m’madzi;

(ii) kulimbikitsa chisamalilo cha malo achilengedwe ndiponso;

(iii) kuteteza malo onse achilengedwe ndinso kulimbikitsa chitukuko cha chuma ndi chikhalidwe cha anthu.

(2) Boma liyenela kuika malamulo pamodzi ndi njila zoyenela, moyenelana ndi ndalama zilipo pofuna kulimbikitsa udindo/ufulu wafotokozedwa mu chigawochi. 74

Ufulu wosathamangitsidwa pa malo

Palibe munthu ayenela kuthamangitsidwa kuchokela ku nyumba kwake kapena nyumba yake kupwanyidwa pogumulidwa popanda chilolezo kuchokela ku bwalo la milandu. 75

Udindo wophunzila

(1) Nzika zonse pamodzi ndi anthu onse akukhala m’dziko ali ndi udindo—

(a) wophunzitsidwa ndi Boma, kuphatikizila akulu ndipo;

(b) kupitiliza kuphunzila, moyendelana ndi malamulo pamodzi ndi njila Boma lakhazikitsa;

(2) Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo/ufulu woyambitsa kumanga malo ophunzilila ndi ndalama zake ngati sizitsutsana ndi lamulo lalikululi.

(3) Lamulo lilipo limene liyan’anila kulembetsedwa kwa sukulu zotelezi zafotokozedwa mu chigawo (2) ndipo kutsekedwa kwa sukulu ngati sizitsata lamulo la kalembesedwe kacho.

(4) Boma liyenela kukonza malamulo ndi njila zina zimene zingathe kulimbikitsa udindo/ufulu wafotokozedwa mu chigawo (1) moyendelana ndi zipangizo/ ndalama zilipo.

Udindo wa chisamalilo cha umoyo

(1) Nzika zonse zili mdziko pamodzi ndi anthu akukhala m’Zimbabwe ali ndi udindo wolandila chithandizo cha umoyo, kuphatikizila nkhani za kulela.

(2) Munthu aliyense wodwala ali ndi udindo/ufulu wopatsidwa mankhwala a matenda ake.

(3) Palibe munthu ayenela kukanidwa kupatsidwa chithandizo ngati adwala mwadzidzi kuchipatala chili chonse m’dziko.

(4) Boma liyenela kukhazikitsa malamulo pamodzi ndi njila zina zolimbikitsa udindo wafotokozedwa mu chigawo-chi ngati zipangizo/ndalama zilipo. 77

Udindo wokhala ndi chakudya ndi madzi Munthu aliyense ali ndi udindo—

(a)

kumwa ndi kugwilitsa ntchito madzi abwino ndi otetezedwa;

(b) kukhala ndi chakudya chokwanila; Ndipo boma liyenela kukhazikitsa malamulo pamodzi ndi njila zokwanilitsa udindo-wu ngati zipangizo/ndalama zilipo. 78

Udindo wa maukwati

(1) Munthu aliyense ali ndi zaka khumi ndi mphambu zisanu ndi zitatu (18 years) ali ndi udindo wopeza banja/ukwati.

(2) Palibe munthu angakanizidwe kulowa m’banja, kukhala pa ukwati ngati iye akufuna.

(3) Anthu ofanana (mwamuna ndi mwamuna kapena mukazi ndi mukazi) saloledwa kukwatilana. GAWO 3 KUFOTOKOZEDWA KWA MAUDINDO ENA


Kufotokozedwa Kwa Gawo 3 Chigawochi chikufotokoza maudindo ndi maufulu a anthu ena.

(1) Chigawo chimenechi chikumasulila ena maufulu ndi maudindo ochita ku ena anthu apadela.

(2) Chigawo chimenechi sichiyenela kutengedwa ngathi chofuna kupondeleza maudindo ndi ufulu wa ena anthu. 80

Udindo wa azimai

(1) Mukazi aliyense ayenela kulemekezedwa limodzi ndi amuna kuphatikizila kupatsidwa mwayi wofanana pa nkhani za ndale, chuma ndi chikhalidwe.

(2) Akazi ali ndi udindo wofanana ndi amuna pa nkhani zosunga ndi kukhala ndi ana awo koma lamulo la mnyumba ya malamulo la Act of Parliament liyenela kufotokoza njila za udindo-wu.

(3) Malamulo onse achikhalidwe amene angapondeleze udindo/ufulu wa akazi wafotokozedwa mu lamulo lalikululi savomelezedwa. 81

Udindo wa ana

(1) Mwana aliyense ali ndi zaka khumi ndi mphambu zisanu ndi zitatu (18 years) mnyamata kapena mtsikana ali ndi udindo—

(a)

2013

wofanana mwa lamulo kuphatikizila kulankhula ndi kumveledwa;

(b) kupatsidwa dzina lapa banja;

(c)

kwa mwana wabadwila mu—

(i) Mu Zimbabwe; kapena

(ii) Kunja kwa dziko la Zimbabwe ndipo ndi Nzika la Zimbabwe kudzela ku makolo, ayenela kukhala ndi kalata lakubadwa.

(d) wosamalilira pa banja kapena ndi makolo ake;

(e)

wotetezedwa ku nkhani za chuma pamodzi ndi chiwelewele, kugwilitsidwa ntchito ali wa mun’ono/wachichepele, ndipo sayenela kuvhutitsidwa, kunyalanyazidwa kapena kuvhutitsidwa kulikonse;

(f)

wophunzila,wolandila chithandizo cha mankhwala, chakudya ndi kukhala m’nyumba;

(g) sayenela kulowa ntchito ya usilikali ngakhale kukamenya nawo nkhondo;

(h) sayenela kukanizidwa pa nkhani za ndale;

(i)

sayenela kutsekeledwa pokhapokha ndi njila yomalizila—

(i) kutsekeledwa kwa nthawi yochepa;

(ii) ayenela kutsekeledwa ku ndende kopanda anthu akulu oposa zaka khumi mphambu zisanu ndi zitatu (18 years);

(iii) ayenela kugwilidwa ndi kukhala pa malo poziwa kuti ndi mwana malingana zaka zake;

(2) Chidwi cha ana chiyenela kukhala patsogolo kwa mwana aliyense.

(3) Ana ayenela kutetezedwa ku bwalo la milandu makamaka ku bwalo la milandu la Lapamwamba. 82

Udindo wa achikulile Achikulile a zaka makumi asanu ndi awili (70 years) zili ndi ufulu.

(a)

ayenela kulandila chithandizo kuchokera ku mabanja awo pamodzi ndi Boma;

(b) kulandila mankhwala kuchokera ku Boma;

(c)

Boma liyenela kuthandiza powapatsa ndalama pamodzi ndi chisamalilo cha umoyo;

Boma liyenela kukhazikitsa malamulo ndi njila zothandizila udindo/ufulu wa nkhalamba ngati ndalama zilipo. 83

Udindo wa anthu olemala

Boma liyenela kukonza njila malingana ndi ndalama/zipangizo zilipo zothandizila anthu olemala makamaka odwala misala ndi opuwala ziwalo za mthupi ndi njila zina monga

(a)

kuti athe kuziimilila;

(b) kuti athe kukhala ndi mabanja awo pochita masewelo a chikhalidwe;

(c)

kuwateteza kuti akhale wosavutitsidwa;

(d) kuwapatsa ndi kuwathandizila ndi mankhwala;

(e)

kuwathandizila ndi zofunikila pa maphunzilo awo;

(f)

Boma liyenela kuwalipilila maphunzilo awo;

Udindo wa ngwazi za nkhondo ya ufulu

(1) Ngwazi zinamenya nkhondo inabweletsa ufulu wa dziko ndi—

(a)

onse anathandizila pomenya nkhondo inabweletsa ufulu;

(b) onse anathandizila pa nkhondo yi;

(c)

onse anamangidwa pa nkhani za ndale, ena natsekeledwa m’ndende;

Onsewa ayenela kupatsidwa ulemu chifukwa cha chithandizo chinabweletsa ufulu wa dziko la Zimbabwe ndipo ayenela kulandila chithandizo cha penjeni ndi mankhwala ngati adwala.

(2) Lamulo la m’nyumba ya malamulo liyenela kulemekeza ngwazi zonse zinathandizila mu nkhondo inabweletsa ufulu wa dziko. GAWO 4 KULIMBIKITSA UDINDO NDI UFULU WA ANTHU


Kulimbikitsa udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu

(1) Onse anthu awa—

(a)

munthu aliyense ali ndi chidwi chake;

(b) munthu aliyense woyimilila wina amena sangathe kuziyimilila;

(c)

munthu aliyense amene angathe kuimilila zolinga za gulu la anthu;

(d) munthu ali yense akuyimilila chidwi cha anthu ambili;

(e)

bungwe lililonse limene liyimilila anthu awo.

Ndi oyenela kukasuma ku bwalo la milandu ngati ufulu pamodzi ndi udindo wa anthu ukupondelezedwa moyendelana ndi chigawochi, ndipo bwalo lamilandu liyenela kupeleka chigamulo choteteza udindo wa anthu ndi kulipilitsa ndalama za chipepeso.

(2) Ngati munthu aswa lamulo sizitanthauza kuti sangathe kukasuma ku bwalo la milandu moyendelana ndi chigawo (1).

(3) Lamulo liyenela kutsatilidwa ku bwalo lamilandu moyendelana ndi chigawo(1) ndipo malamulo ayenela kuti—

(a)

kuthandizilidwa pokasuma ku bwalo la milandu pa udindo wa anthu moyendelana ndi chigawo (1);

(b) kutsata njila zonse zoyenela za dongosolo yonse;

(c)

bwalo la milandu liyenela kutsata malamulo onse oyenela;

(d) munthu aliyense wodziwa za udindo wa anthu angathe kukhala nawo ku bwalo la milandu.

(4) Ngati palibe lamulo loyendelana ndi chigawo 3 sizikaniza kuti mulandu uyambe kuzengedwa ku bwalo la milandu malingana ndi chigawo (1). GAWO 5 MALILE A MPHAMVU YA UDINDO NDI UFULU WA ANTHU


Malile a mphamvu ya udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu

(1) Ufulu ndi udindo wa anthu umene wafotokozedwa mu chigawochi kuyenela kuchenjeleledwa posafunika kusokoneza ndi kulakwitsa udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu ena.

(2) Udindo pamodzi ndi ufulu wa anthu uli mu chigawochi uli ndi malile malingana ndi lamulo kotelo kuti malilewa ndi osakondela potengela mfundo zonse zoyenela monga—

(a)

ufulu ndi udindo wacho;

(b) cholinga cha malile ake kapena moyendelana ndi chitetezo cha dziko, umoyo wa anthu;

(c)

mitundu ya malile ake;

(d) powonetsetsa kuti ufulu ndi udindo wa anthu silikusokonezanso udindo ndi ifulu wa athu ena;

(e)

kugwilizana wa malile ndi cholinga;

(f)

ngati pali kuletsedwa kwina pofuna kukwanilitsa zolinga.

(3) Palibe lamulo lingaletse udindo moyendelana ndi chigawochi ndipo sayenela kulakwitsidwa ndi munthu aliyense—

(a)

udindo wokhala ndu umoyo pokhapokha moyendelana ndi chigawo 48;

(b) udindo wolemekezedwa ngati munthu;

(c)

udindo wosavutitsidwa kapena nkhaza kapena, kusagwilidwa ngati simunthu kapena kulangidwa;

(d) udindo wosayesedwa kukhala kapolo kapena kugwilitsidwa ntchito mwaukapolo;


(e)

udindo woweluzidwa mulandu moyenela;

(f)

udindo wolandila chigamulo malingana ndi chigawo 50(7)(a).

Malile pa nthawi ya maphokoso

(1) Powonjezela malile ali mu chigawo 86 a udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu wafotokozedwa mu chigawochi ungathe kuthetsedwa ndi lamulo moyendelana ndi zochitika pa nthawi ya maphokoso, koma moyendelana ndi chivomelezo chili mu chigawochi ndi mndandanda wachiweli.

(2) Lamulo lolembedwa ili mu chigawo chaching’ono (1) ndipo njila za lamulo zogwilitsidwa ntchito pansi pa lamuloli, liyenela kusindikizidwa mu Gazette.

(3) Ngati pali malile a lamulio linalembedwa moyendelana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (1) loikidwa pa udindo ndi ufulu wa anthu limene lafotokozedwa mu chigawochi siliyenela kupyola ndi momwe likuyendelana ndi maphokoso.

(4) Palibe lamulo lingavomelezane ndi maphokoso ochitika m’dziko, ndipo palibe lamulo kapena njila ili yonse ingatsutsane ndi chivomelanochi, kapena—

(a)

Chivomelezo, kapena chilolezo kapena ulamulilo wa chivomelano, Boma kapena bungwe lililonse kapena nthambi ya Boma ku madeala onse, kapena munthu aliyense, posalemekeza machitidwe otsutsana ndi lamulo; kapena

(b) Malile a udindo wofotokezedwa muchigawo 86(3), kapena ulamulilo kapena vomelezo la maudindo kuti aswedwe; palibe lamulo limene lingatsutsane ngati paikidwa lamulo lofuna kuteteza anthu ambili monga la kusakhazikitsika kwa zinthu m’dziko.

MUTU 5 Utsogoleli Wapamwamba

GAWO 1 MPHAMVU ZAPAMWAMBA


Mphamvu zapamwamba
