(1) Bwalo lokhudza za Lamulo Likulu ladziko ndi lomwe lili bwalo lapamwamba mudziko, ndiye likukhalila ndi—

(a)

woweluza milandu Wamkulu ndi Wachiwili ku mtsogoleli wa bwaloli;

(b) woweluza milandu asanu ku bwalo la milandu yalamulo.

(2) Ngati akufuna kuti pabwele wina woweluza milandu kubwalo loyang’anila za lamulo kwa nthawi yaingono Mtsogoleli wa wowelua milandu akhoza kufuna ndi kupatsa ntchito woweluza milandu kukhala nawo kubwalo lapamwamba pa nkhani za lamulo lalikulu pa nthawi yomweyo.

(3) Milandu yozengedwa ku bwalo la Lamulo lalikulu—

(a)

kupondelezedwa kwa udindo ndi ufulu wa athu moyendelana ndi chigawo 4, kapena yokhudza kasankhidwe ka mtsogoleli wa dziko kapena wachiwili wa mtsogoleli wa dziko, kuyenela kuweluzidwa ndi akulu onse ozenga milandu;

(b) mosiyana ndi milandu yafotokozedwa mu ndime (a), liyenela kuweluzidwa ndi wozenga milandu ofika atatu; koma lamulo kapena chiweluzo chochokela ku bwalo la milandu limavomeleza kuti nkhaniyi ithe kuzengedwa ndi woweluza m’modzi kapena angapo ku bwalo la milandu.

(4) Woweluza milandu kapena womwe anali woweluza milandu wokhazikitsidwa pa lamulo la chigawo (2) akhoza kupitilila kukhala ngati wozenga milandu kubwalo la pamwamba la bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo pambuyo pakutha kwa nthawi yawo yogwila ntchito, kuchitila kuti amalize nkhani zawo zomwe anali nazo. 167 Malile bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo

(1) Bwalo la lokhudza nkhani zamalamulo— ndi bwalo lapamwamba pa nkhani zones zokhudza Lamulo ladziko ndiye zogamula zake zikugwila mabwalo onse otsatila;

(b) limagamula nkhani zokhudza Lamulo Lalikulu, maka-maka nkhani zopezeka pa chigawo 131(8)(b) ndi chiganizo chikulu cha 9(2) ya dongosolo ya chisanu; ndi

(c) kukhazikitsa maganizo pozindikila ngathi nkhani ikukhudza bwalo lapamwamba kapena iyayi.

(a)

(2) Malinga ndi Lamulo likulu, ndi bwalo la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulolomwe—

(a)

kunena kukwanila kwa mulandi ngathi uyenela kukamveka mubwalo lapamwamba limeneli kapena lamulo lofuna kukhazikitsidwa ndiye amachita zimenezi ngathi lamulo limenelo lomwe likukhudzidwa ndi lamulo lochekela pa Lamulo limeneli;

(b) kumvera ngathi pakakhala kusagwilizana pa nkhani yakuti kodi munthu ndiwoyenela kukhala Wachiwili ku Mtsogoleli wadziko;

(c) kukhazikitsa nkhani zokhala ngathi Mtsogoeleli wadziko alephela kugwila ntchito yake, kapena Nyumba yaMalamulo yalephela kugwila ntchito yache.

(d) kuyang’ana ngathi nyumba ya malamulo kapena mtsogoleli wadziko

walephela kugwila ntchito yawo mokwanila malinga ndi lamulo ladziko.

(3) Bwalo lapamwamba la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo ndi lomwe lili ndi gamulo yapamwamba. Maganizo onse okhazikidwa ndi bwaloli ndi zomwe zidzatsatilidwe ndi ena mabwalo onse.

(4) Lamulo lalikulu ladziko likhoza kupatsila mphamvu zokhazikitsa malire ndi bwalo la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo.

(5) Malamulo a Bwalo lokhudza zamalamuloakhoza kulola munthu malinga ndi lamulo la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo, patakhala chilolezo kapena palibe—

(a)

kubwelesa nkhani kubwalo lapamwambali poyamba asanapite kwina kwake, nkhati zikukomela chilungamo;

(b) kupilila kubwalo la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo kuchokela ku lina bwalo lamilandu;

(c)

kuwonekala ngathi mbale wa bwalo lamilandu.

168 Bwalo lapamwamba-mwamba

(1) Bwalo la pamwamba-mwamba ndi bwalo lozindikilidwa ndi kudalilika ndiye likukhala ndi—

(a)

mtsogoleli waWoweluza ndi womutsatila;

(b) woweluza wosachepela awili a bwalo lapamwamba-mwamba; ndi

(c)

woweluza ena wokhazikitsidwa pa lamulo la chagawo chaching’ono (2).

(2) Ngati pali ndichifunilo chakuti pakhale wina woweluza milandi emwe akufunika ku bwalo la pamwamba mwamba kwa nthawi ingono, Mtsogoleli wa woweluza milandu akhoza kukhazikitsa wina woweluza.

(3) Woweluza kapena womwe anali wozenga milandu wokhazikitsidwa pa chigawo chaching’ono (2) akhoza kupitilila ndi ntchito ngathi woweluza milandu. 169 Malire a bwalo lapamwamba-mwamba

(1) Bwalo la pa mwamba mwamba ndilomwe lili lomalizila pozenga ndi kudandaulila pankhani mudziko lino kusiya kwa nkhani zomwe zingatengedwe ndi bwalo la Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo.

(2) Malinga ndi chigawochaching’on (1), Lamulo ladziko akhoza kuyikako ena malile ndi mphamvu ku bwalo la pamwamba mwamba.

(3) Lamulo ladziko likhoza kupatsila malire ndi bwalo la pamwamba mwamba ndiye pachifukwa chimenechi likhoza kupatsilanso mphamvu zakuti bwaloli likonze malamulo ake.

(4) Malamulo a bwalo amenewa akhoza kupatsila mabalani wa bwalo la pamwamba mwamba malire owonjezela mu nkhani zopanda umboni, zosamvana pakati pa anthu—

(a)

kuyika ko gamulo munkhani zomwe saneyambe kukamba, osati zina zosokoneza zakusungidwa kwa ana;

(b) kukhazikitsa nkhani zoyambilila ndi zina zochitika movomelezana mu kunja kwa mabwalo amilandu kuphatikiza pempho louzidwa njila yotsata, koma osati nkhani zosokoneza ufulu wa munthu; malamulowa apadela ayenela kupatsa munthu mpata wakuti nkhani yake imvedwe ndi bwalo la kuti abwele pa bwalo la pamwamba mwamba komwe woweluza akhoza kulandila, kukana, kukuuza kuti uyime, kapena kutsimikiza gamulo yopatsidwa yo.

170 Bwalo la lalikulu Bwalo la Lalikulu lozindikilidwa ndiye likukhala ndi anthu awa—

(a)

woweluza milandu wamkulu ndi womutsatila, ndiponso mtsogoleli wa woweluza milandu kubwaloli; ndi

(b) mtsogoleli wa woweluza milandu ndi ena woweluza kumeneko. 171 Malile la kaweluzidwe ka bwalo lalikulu

(1) Bwalo la Lalikulu—

(a)

lakhala likuchita nkhani zonse zolakwilana anthu ndi zosokoneza chikhalidwe cha anthu pakati pawo mudziko lonse;

(b) likuyang’anilanso ena mabwalo apansi ngati magisitileti. Limayang’ananso zigamulo zoperekedwa kumeneko, munthu akadandaula amapita kubwaloli;

(c)

limayangana nkhani zikulu zokhudza Lamulo likulu, koma zimenezo zomwe mabwalo apamwamba sakuchita kawili-kawili;

(d) lili ndi zina mphamvu zoyangana zina nkhani monga lingapatsidwe malinga ndi Lamulo ladziko.

(2) Lamulo ladziko limalamulila bwalo limeneli kuti lichitepo kanthu, ndiye mpaka kukonzanso malamulo awo apadela kubwaloko.

(3) Lamulo ladziko likhozanso kuchita kuti bwalo limeneli ligamulidwe pochita mabwalo ang’onong’ono, amene amachita kuti bwaloli lidziyang’ana milandu yokhudza bwalo limeneli chabe.

(4) malamulo akubwaloli akupatsa mlembi wake kuti adziyangana zochitika kumabwalo komwe kulibe nkhani zaumbala—

(a)

kulamula pa nkhani zomwe palibe kutsutsana, osati nkhani zokhudza zakayimidwe ka munthu kapena kupatsidwa ana woyanganila pakachita vuto lolimbilana ana;

(b) kuyang’anila nkhani zija komwe kulibe zakuba, zomwe zingapatsidwe chigamulo zilibe kutha, kuphatikiza ko zonse zomwe zinapatsidwa kuti zichitike. koma malamulo amenewa akupatsa munthu mpata wakuti nkhani zake zimveke ndikupititsa nkhani zosatha kubwalo Lalikulu, mwina mwake woweluza mlandu amapeleka chigamulo padela napatsa chigamulo china chomwe akuwona kuti ndiyoyenela. 172 Bwalo lokhudza za zintchito

(1) Bwalo loyang’anila milandu za wogwila ntchito ndiwogwililidwa zintchito ndi bwalo lozindikilidwa ndikufunikila kwambili ndipo likuphatikizila ogwila ntchito awa—

(a)

wolamulila wamkulu; ndiponso

(b) ena woweluza nkhani omwe amasankhidwa nthawi ndi nthawi. [ka gawo kang’ono kokonzetsedwa ndi gawo 3 ya Lamulo 10 ya 2017]

(2) Bwalo loyang’ana nkhani zawogwila ntchito ndi wogwililidwa ntchito limeneli ili ndi mphamvu pa nkhani zokhudza zintchito ndi zina zomwe lingapatsidwe palamulo ladziko kuti lichite.

(3) Lamulo ladziko limapatsila bwalo limeneli lawogwila ntchito ndi wogwililidwa zintchito mphamvu, ndiye pachifukwa chimenechi, lamulo limeneli likupatsanso mphamvu zakuti bwalo lawogwila ntchito ndi kugwililidwa ntchito likonze malamulo a bwalo limeneli. 173 Bwalo loyang’ana nkhani zautsogoleli

(1) Bwalo laUtsogoleli ndi bwalolofunikila kwambili lozindikilidwa ndiye likukhalila ndi anthu otsatilawa—

(a)

wolamulila wamkulu; ndiponso

(b) ena woweluza milandu wa bwalo laUtsogoleli omwe akukhazikidwa nthawi ndi nthawi. [ka gawo kang’ono kokonzetsedwa ndi gawo 4 ya Lamulo 10 ya 2017]

(2) Bwalo la zaUtsogoleli lili ndi mphamvu zoyanganila nkhani zaUtsogoleli ndizina zomwe lingapatsidwe ndi Lamulo ladziko komwe amachitanso zina zake.

(3) Lamulo ladziko likhoza kupatsa mphamvu ku bwalo loyang’anila zaUtsogoleli ndiye chifukwa cha zimenezi likhoza kukonza malamulo a bwalo limeneli lokhudza za Utsogoleli. 174 Mabwalo a milandu ndi mabwalo a nkhani zapadela

(1) Lamulo lalikulu ladziko likupatsila kuti pakhazikidwe, ndikuyika wogwila ntchito ndi malile ndi mphamvu za—

(a) bwalo loyang’anila nkhani za anthu olakwilana wina ndi nzake pa milandu;

(b) bwalo loyang’anila nkhani zachikhalidwe cha anthu komwe amagamula malinga ndi nkhani zopalamulidwa pa chikhalidwe ka anthu;

(c) mabwalo ena ali pansi pa bwalo lalikulu; ndi

(d) mabwalo ena apadela okhazikitsidwa ndi Lamulo lalikulu ladziko kuti aziyang’ana ozenga nkhani zina zapadela, kuyanjanitsa anthu ndi zina njila zokhazikitsa kuthetsa kusamvana.

(2) Malingana ndi chigawo188(4) cha Lamulo la Likulu, malipilo, ndalama zina pamodzi ndi zina zomwe amapatsidwa wozenga milandu ku Bwalo loyang’ana wogwila ntchito ndi wolemba ntchito pamodzi ndi Bwalo loyang’ana nkhani zaUtsogoleli amene akugwila ntchitoyi kapena omwe akugwilizila patsiku lakuyamba kugwilisidwa ntchito kwa Lamulo limeneli sidzichepesedwa. [ka gawo kang’ono kokonzetsedwa ndi gawo 5 ya Lamulo 10 ya 2017]

175 Mphamvu za mabwalo amilandu malinga ndi lamulo ladziko

(1) Ngati bwalo lamilandu lipeleka chigamulo chotsutsana ndi lamulo lililonse kapena motsutsana ndi langizo lochokela kwa mtsogoleli wa dziko kapena nyumba ya malamulo, kutsutsana chigamulo sichiloledwa pokhapokha chavomelezedwa ndi bwalo loyang’anila nkhani za lamulo.

(2) Bwalo lamilandu lapeleka chigamulo chotsutsana ndi ndi lamulo moyendelana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (1) payenela kupelekedwa gamulo loyembekezela kapena kupatsa mpata mbali yosuma kapena kuyimika mulandu, podikilila chigamulo chochokela ku bwalo la milandu Loyang’anila nkhani za lamulo moyendelana ndi chigawo chaching’ono cha (1).

(3) Munthu aliyense yemwe sanakondwelendi chiweluzo chapelekedwa ndi bwalo pokhudza nkhani za lamulo angathe kukaasuma mulandu wake kubwalo loweluza nkhani za lamulo limene bwaloli liyene kuyang’ana nkhani yo kupeleka chigamulo potsimikiza moyendelana ndi lamulo pa chigawochaching’ono (1).

(4) pakakhala nkhani yokhudza za Lamulo kubwalo lamilandu lomwe likutsogolela bwaloli liyenela kupititsa nkhani yake kubwalo lokhudza za lmulo.

(5) Lamulo lililonse pamena chigamulo chapelekedwa payenela kukhala umboni kuchokela ku bwalo lowona nkhani za lamuklo za kuweluzidwa kotsutsana ndi lamulo moyendelana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (1) kuchokela ku bwalo lowona nkhani za lamulo.

(6) Pogamulila nkhani yokhudza zamalamulo bwalo lililonse lomwe lili ndi mphamvu zomvela nkhani zimene liyenela—

(a)

kukhazikitsa kuti lamulo lililonse lomwe likulephela kugwilizana ndi Lmulo lalikulu ladziko liyenela kukhazikitsa kuti palibe kugwilizana;

(b) pogamula pakakhala zina zosagwilizana ndi Lmulo ladziko lalikulu, woweluza nkhani ayenela kuyimitsa nkhaniyo, ayangane kuti kusagwilizana kunayamba lithi, ndiye kudzatenga gamulo yochokela kubwalo lapamwamba. 176 Mphamvu zosasintha za mabwalo amilandi oti bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo, bwalo lapamwamba-mwamba ndi bwalo loyangana zina nkhani zapamwamba Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo, lokhudza zina zapadela ndi bwalo loyang’anila nkhani za lamulo pamodzi ndi bwalo la pamwamba mwamba zonsezi zili ndi mphamvu yoteteza ndi kukwanilitsa dongosolo yazo ndi kukhazikitsa lamulo, mosunga chilungamo ndi kutsata lamulo lalikulu la m’dziko. GAWO 2 BUNGWE LOYANG’ANILA NKHANI ZA MALAMULO

177 Zofunikila kuti munthu akhale wozenga milandu kubwalo loyang’anila nkhani za lamulo malamulo

(1) Munthu ali wokwanila kusankhidwa kukhala wozenga milandu ku bwalo loyangana nkhani za malamulo ngati ali nzika ya Zimbabwe, ali ndi zaka zosachepela makumi anayi okhala ndi nzeru za malamulo ndipo powonjezela izi akhale ndi chimodzi cha zotsatila zi—

(a)

ayenela kukhala wozenga milandu ku bwalo losayikidwa malire pa nkhani zonse mudziko lomwe lamulo lakumeneko ndichiRoma ndi chiDachi, ndipo chizungu chikhale chilankhulo cholandilidwa mudziko limenelo akuchizindikila kuti ndi chilankhulo; kapena

(b) akahale atagwila ntchito ngati loya kwa zaka zosachepela khumi ndi ziwili, mwatsatane-tsatane kapena iyayi—

(i) mu Zimbabwe; kapena

(ii) mudziko komwe lamulo lawo ndi Roma ndiDachi kumene chizungu ndi chilankhulo chogwilisidwa ntchito mudziko lo, ndiyenso akhale munthu emwe akuloledwa kugwila ntchito ngati loya pakali pano.

(2) Kuti asankhidwe kukhala wozenga milandu kuBwalo lokhudza zamalamulo munthu ayenela kukhala wachilimbile wokhoza kutenga ntchito imeneyi. 178 Zofunikila kuti munthu akhale wozenga milandu ku bwalo lapamwamba-mwamba

(1) Munthu ali wokwanila kusankhidwa kukhala wozenga milandu ku bwalo lapamwamba-mwamba ngati ali nzika ya Zimbabwe, ali ndi zaka zosachepela makumi

anayi okhala ndi nzeru za malamulo ndipo powonjezela zimenezi, akhale ndi chimodzi cha zotsatila zi—

(a)

ayenela kukhala wozenga milandu ku bwalo losayikidwa malile pa nkhani zonse mudziko lomwe lamulo lakumeneko lachiRoma ndi lachiDachi, ndipo chizungu chikhale chilankhulo cholandilidwa mudziko limenelo akuchizindikila kuti ndi chilankhulo; kapena

(b) akhale atagwila ntchito ngati loya kwa zaka zosachepela khumi ndi ziwili, mwatsatane-tsatane kapena iyayi—

(i) mu Zimbabwe; kapena

(ii) mudziko komwe lamulo lawo lachiRoma ndichiDachi ndipo Chizungu ndi chilankhulo chogwilitsidwa ntchito mudzikolo, ndiyenso akhale munthu yemwe akuloledwa kugwila ntchito ngati loya pakali pano.

(2) Kuti asankhidwe kukhala wozenga milandu kuBwalo lapamwamba-mwamba munthu ayenela kukhala wodalilika kuti angathe kugwila ntchitoyi. 179 Zofunikila kuti munthu asankhidwe kukhala wozenga milandu kumabwalo awa; bwalo lalikulu, bwalo loyang’ana nkhani za wogwila ndi wogwililidwa zintchito ndi bwalo loyang’ana za Utsogoleli

(1) Munthu ndiwoyenela kusankhidwa kukhala wozenga milandu ku bwalo lalikulu, loyangana za wogwila ndi kugwililidwa ntchito ndi bwalo loyangana za Utsogoleli ngati munthuyu ali ndi zaka zosachepela makumi anayi—

(a)

ayenela kukhala wozenga milandu ku bwalo losayikidwa malire pa nkhani zonse mudziko lomwe lamulo lakumeneko ndi lachiRoma ndi lachiDachi, ndipo chizungu chikhale chilankhulo cholandilidwa mudziko limenelo akuchizindikila kuti ndi chilankhulo; kapena

(b) akhale atagwila ntchito ngati loya kwa zaka zosachepela khumi ndi ziwili, mwatsatane-tsatane kapena iyayi—

(i) mu Zimbabwe; kapena

(ii) mudziko komwe lamulo lachiRoma ndi chiDachi limagwilitsidwa ntchito ndipo Chizungu ndi chilankhulo chogwilisidwa ntchito mudzikolo, ndiyenso akhale munthu yemwe akuloledwa kugwila ntchito ngati loya pakali pano.

(2) Kuti asankhidwe kukhala wozenga milandu kuBwalo lapamwambamwamba munthu ayenela kukhala wodalilika kuti angathe kugwila ntchitoyi. 180 Kusankhidwa kwa wozenga milandu

(1) Wamkulu wozenga milandu, Wachiwili waWamkulu ndi Mt-sogoleli wamkulu wa Bwalo lapamwamba pamodzi ndi wozenga milandu ena amasankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko molingana ndi chigawochi.

(2) Wamkulu wozenga milandu, Wachiwili wa Wamkulu ndi Mt-sogoleli wa Bwalo lapamwamba adzasankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko pambuyo pokambilana ndikumva maganizo a Komishoni yoyang’anila za milandu.

(3) Ngathi kusankhidwa kwa Wamkulu wozenga milandu, Wachi-wili wa Wozenga milandu, Mtsogoleli wa Bwalo lapamwamba kukulephela kugwilizana ndi zoganizilidwa ndi Komishoni yoyang’anila zakayendesedwe ka ntchito za mabwalo a milandu, malingana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (2), Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kudziwisa a Seneti mwamsanga. Pofuna kuti zimveke bwino, zodziwisidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko sizitsutsidwa.

(4) Ngati pali pofunikila kusankha wozenga milandu, kusiya kwa Wamkulu wozenga milandu, Wachiwili wa Wamkulu wozenga milandu ndi Mtsogolei wa Bwalo lamilandu lapamwamba Komishoni yoyan’ganila nkhani za milandu iyenela—

(a)

kufalitsa uthenga wa ntchitoyi; ndipo

(b) kupempha Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi anthu kuti apeleke mayina a anthu omwe angaganizile kuti atenga ntchitoyi; ndipo

(c)

kufunsa mafunso olowa ntchito poyela; ndipo

(d) kukonza mundandanda wamayina a anthu atatu osankhidwa pa ntchitoyi; ndipo

(e)

kupeleka mayina kwa Mtsogoleli wadziko; amene, moyen-delana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (5), Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha m’modzi wodzalowa ntchito imeneyi.

(5) Ngathi Mtsogoleli wadziko akuwona kuti palibe pakati pa anthu atatuwa, moyendelana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (4)(e) woyenela kulowa ntchito imeneyi, ayenela kuuza Komishoni yoyang’ana za nkhani zakumab-walo amilandu kuti ipeleke mundandanda wa anthu ena atatu, mundandanda umene Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha m’modzi kuti agwile ntchitoyi.

(6) Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kuchita chidziwitso cha anthu onse wolowa ntchito malingana ndi chigawo chimenechi kuti atsindikizidwe mu Gazeti.

(7) Zi ntchito za wozenga milandu a pamwamba ku Bwalo loyang’anila wogwila ndi wolemba ntchito ndi akulu a bwalo long’ana nkhani zautsogoleli ziyenela kupatsidwa kwa wina wozenga milandu kapena wina wogwilizila, malinga momwe zilili, kubwalo la milandu lokhudzidwa ndiye akusankhidwa ndi Wamkulu wozenga milandi mokambilana ndi Komishoni yowona nkhani za malamulo. [ka gawo kang’ono kokonzetsedwa ndi gawo 6 ya Lamulo 10 ya 2017]

181 Kusankhidwa kwa wogwililila mipando ya wozenga milandu

(1) Ngati ntchito ya Mtsogoleli wa wozenga milandu ilibe munthu kapena kuti munthu wake akulephela kugwila ntchitoyi, Wachiwili ku Mtsogoleli wa wozenga milandu angathe kulowa pampandowu mogwilizila, koma ngati mipando yonse iyi ilibe anthu, wina wa akulu ku mabwalo kozenga milandu kuchokela ku bwalo loyang’ana nkhani zamalamulo ayenela kulowa ntchitoyi mogwilizila mpandowu.

(2) Ngati ntchito ya—

(a) Mtsogoleli wa wozenga milandu ku bwalo lalikulu;

(b) Wolamulila milandu wamkulu wochokela ku Bwalo loyang’ana wogwila ndi kulemba zintchito; kapena

(c) Wolamulila milandu wamkulu wochokela ku Bwalo loyang’anila za utsogoleli; ikakhala ilibe munthu kapena ngati ankagwila ntchito akulephela ntchito imeneyi, wozenga milandu wina wotsogolela wamkulu wa ntchito ayenela kulowa ntchito yozenga milandu. [ka gawo kang’ono kokonzetsedwa ndi gawo 6 ya Lamulo 10 ya 2017]

(3) Ngati pakufunika kuti ayikepo wina wozenga milandu kumabwalo lalikulu, bwalo loyang’anila ogwila ntchito ndi wolemba anthu ntchito kapena bwalo lowona nkhani za Utsogoleli bugwe loyanganila zakayendesedwe ka ntchito kumabwalo a milandu, akhoza kuyitana wozenga milandu ena omwe adapuma kale ntchito kuti abwele kuzagwila ntchitoyi. Ndiye ayenela kugwila ntchitoyi kwa miyezi khumi ndi iwili, ndikusiyila wina yemwenso ayenela kugwila ntchitoyi kwa mwezi khumi ndi iwili yomalizila.

(4) Anthu wosankhidwa kuti agwile ntchito imeneyi malinga ndi chigawo chaching’ono (3) akhoza kukhala ngati wozenga milandu ngakhale kuti nthawi yawo yatha kuchitila kuti nkhani zomwe analinazo zithe. 182 Kusankhidwa kwa wozenga milandu kubwalo lapansi la megisileti ndi ena mamembala a nthambi yoyang’anila zakayendesedwe ka ntchito zakuzengedwa kwa milandu Lamulo ladziko liyenela kupatsila zakukhazikisidwa kwa oyimba milandu kumabwalo apansi ndi ena wogwila ntchito kumeneko kusiya kwa wozenga milandu, koma—

(a)

wozenga milandu kubwalo lapansi la magisitereti ayenela kusankhidwa ndi bungwe loyang’anila zakayendesedwe ka ntchito kumabwalo a milandu;

(b) ogwila ntchito ena, kupatula kwa wozenga milandu ayenela kusankhidwa ndi bungwe loyang’ana zochitika kubwalo lamilandu;

(c)

ntchito zonse ziyenela kulowedwa posankha woweluza mwachilungamo.

183 Wogwila ntchito ku bwalo la milandu sayenela kusankhidwa kumabwalo a awili Kusiya kwa zomwe zingalembedwe mulamulo Lalikulu, munthu sayenela kulembedwa ntchito yozenga milandu ku kumabwalo awili. 184 Kupatsidwa kwa zintchito kuyenela kutulusa kayimidwe ka dziko Kusankhidwa ndi kulowa ntchito kwa oyimba milandu kuyenela kuyendelana ndi lamulo la kufanana kwa amuna ndi akazi moyenelana ndi kayimidwe kali m’dziko la Zimbabwe. 185 Kulumbira potenga ntchito

(1) Atsogoleli a zakuzengedwa kwa milandu ndi womwe amapita ku Mtsogoleli wadziko kuti alumbile asanayambe ntchito. Iwowa achite zimenezi pamaso pa Mtsogoleli wadziko, kapena munthu yemwe asankhidwa kuyimila Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kulumbilitsa moyendelana ndi mndandanda wachitatu woyikidwa.

(2) Pamaso pa wamukulu wozenga mulandu, kusiya kwa Mtsogoleli wa wozenga milandu, ndi womutsatila, aliyense ayenela kulumbila pamaso pa Mtsogoleli wa zakuzengedwa kwa milandu, kapena kuti alumbire pamaso pa wina mtsogoleli yemwe angakhale alipo malinga ndi dongosolo ya zochitika polumbila kuti ayambe ntchito.

(3) Lamulo lomwe kukupezeka ndikuwoneka kwa magisitiketi ayenela kulumbila asanayambe ntchito yake, mamembala a zakuzengedwa kwa milandu, kusiya kwa Mtsogoleli waZakuzengedwa kwa milandu ndi womutsatila, onsewa ayenela kulumbila asanayambe kuugwila ntchito. 186 Nthawi yogwila ntchito yaWozenga milandu

(1) Wozenga milanduku ma Bwalo lokhudza zamalamulo amagwila ntchito kwa zaka khumi ndi zisanu kosapyola, koma—

(a)

ayenela kusiya ntchitoyi akafikitsa zaka makumi asanu ndi awili zobadwa; ndipo

(b) atamaliza ntchito zawo pakutha kwa nthawiyi, angathe kusankhidwa kugwila ntchito ku bwalo lapamwamba mwamba kapena bwalo lalikulu, mofuna iwo, ngati ali woyenela kugwila ntchitoyi.

(2) Wozenga milandu ku bwalo la pamwamba mwamba, ndi bwalo lalikulu pamodzi ndi ozenga milandu ena amagwila ntchito kuyambila patsiku anayamba ntchito kufikira akwaniza zaka makumi zisanu ndi ziwili, pamene ayenela kupuma pa ntchito.

(3) Munthu akhoza kukusankhidwa kukhala wozenga milandu kubwalo lapamwamba-mwamba kapena ku bwalo lalikulu kwa ntahwi yokhazikidwa, koma akugwila ntchito imeneyi akafikitsa zaka makumi asanu ndi awili ayenelakupuma ndi kusiya ntchito ngakhale kuti nthawi yija yomwe anakambilana siyinathe ayenela kupuma posiya ntchito.

(4) Ngakhale kuti wozenga milandu uyu asiya ntchito yake, kapena wafikitsa zaka makumi asanu ndi makumi awili, kapena pa wozenga milandu kubwalo lokhudza zamalamulo kapena wozenga milandu malingana ndi chigawochaching’ono (3), atafika pa nthawi yake yopuma angathe kupatsidwa mwayi wopitiliza kugwila ntchito.

(5) Wozenga milandu akhoza kusiya ntchito yake nthawi iliyense koma alembe kalata ku Mtsogoleli wabwalo lamilandu kudzela ku bungwe loyang’anila zakayendesedwe ka ntchito ku bwalo lamilandu.

(6) Ofesi ya wozenga milandu siyingatsekedwe chifukwa chakuti iyeyu palibe, koma wina munthu akhoza kugwililila ntchitoyo mpaka atapeza wolowa m’malo mwake. 187 Kuchotsedwa ntchito kwa wozenga milandu

(1) Wozenga milandu akhoza kuchotsedwa ntchito ngati—

(a)

akulephela kugwila ntchito yake bwino, kulephela kapena atadwala misala kapena kupuwala ziwalo zina zoletsa thupi kugwila ntchito bwino;

(b) kusadziwa ntchito; kapena

(c)

pochita makhalidwe osayenela pa ntchito;

ndiye wozenga milandu sangachotsedwe ntchito pokhapokha ziyendelana ndi chigawochi.

(2) Ngati Mtsogoleli wadziko akawona kuti kuchotsedwa ntchito kwa wamkulu wozenga milandu nkofunikila kufufuzidwa, Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kukhazikitsa bungwe lapadela kuti liyangane nkhani imeneyi.

(3) Ngati komishoni loyang’anila nkhani za milandu liyenela kulangiza Mtsogoleli wadziko za kuchotsedwa ntchito kwa wamukulu wozenga milandu, ayenela kukonza bungwe loti lifufuze za nkhaniyi.

(4) Bungwe lokhazikitsidwa pansi pa chigawochi, liyenela kukhala ndi thumwi zokwana zitatu zosankhidwa ndi mtsogoleri wa dziko, zimene ziyenela zili—

(a)

m’modzi wa anthuwa ayenela kukhala—

(i) anagwila ntchito ku bwalo lamilandu lapamwamba mwamba kapena ku bwalo lalukulu mu Zimbabwe; kapena

(ii) anagwila ntchito kapena akugwila ntchito yozenga milandu ku mabwalo kumene kumagwilitsidwa ntchito lamulo lachi Roma ndi lachi Dachi kapena Chizungu, ndipo chizungu ndi chilankhulo cholankhulidwa m’dziko.

(b) m’modzi wa anthuwa ayenela kusankhidwa kuchokela pa mndandanda wa maina atatu okhala maloya omwe agwila ntchito kwa zaka zisanu ndi ziwili kapena kupambana ndi amene anasankhidwa ndi bungwe lamugwilizano wa maloya lokhazikitsidwa paLamulo ladziko Zimbabwe.

(5) Bungwe lonenedwa pa chigawo chaching’ono (4) (b) liyenela kukhazikitsa undandanda wa anthu wake ndikulekedwa kwa Mtsogoleli wadziko.

(6) Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha dzina limodzi kuchokela ku maina atatu anachokela ku bungweli.

(7) Bungwe losankhidwa malinga ndi chigawo chaching’ono (2) kapena (3) chiyenela kufunsa munkhani yakuchotsedwa kwa wozenga milandu yemwe akhudzidwa kuchoka ntchito, pambuyo pake, liyenela kupeleka malipoti kwa Mtsogoleli wadziko oyeneletswa kuchotsedwa ntchito kwa wamukulu wozenga milanduyu.

(8) Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kutsatila zokhazikitsidwa ndi bungweli malinga ndi zopezeka pa chigawo chaching’ono (7).

(9) Bungwe lokhazikitsidwa pa Lamulo limeneli lili ndi ufulu oyanjana ndi kufanafana ndi mphamvu ngati a Komishoni yokhazikitsidwa mwa lamulo kufufuzidwa kwa nkhani chigawo 10(7), kapena lina lamulo lakudzatenga malo a lina lochotsedwa

(10) Ngati nkhani yakuchotsedwa kwa wozenga milandu itapitisidwa ku bungwe lapadela limeneli, wozenga milanduyu akuyimisidwa ntchito pang’ono kuchitila kuti afufuze bwino za mulandu wake mpaka Mtsogoleli wadziko atamva ndi kugamula momwe akuchitila nthawi zonse. Gamulo likhoza kukhala yotele; Pamenepa Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi yemwe ali ndi mphamvu zopambana pa lamuloli.

(11) Lamulo limapatsa mphamvu bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za milandu kapena bwalo lozenge milandu yapadela moyendelana ndi chigawochi, kupempha wozenga mulandu aliyense kuti apelike makalata aumoyo wake kuchokela ku bungwe lowona nkhani za umoyo linakonzedwa pa ntchitoyi, kuti ipeleke umboni pa kudwala kwa kuti ndi misala kapena matenda wamba. 188 Kagwilidwe ka ntchito ndi nthawi zokhala pa ntchito ku mamembala la nthambi ya zakuzengedwa kwa milandu

(1) Wozenga milandu awa ayenela kupatsidwa malipilo, zina zapadela zomwe zimayikidwa padela nthawi ndi nthawi ndi bungwe loyang’anila zochitika pakuzengedwa kwa milandu. Zimenezi amachita pogwilizana ndi kulankhulana ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi nduna yoyang’anila wogwila ntchito zakuzengedwa kwa milandu ndi Nduna yoyang’ana za chuma.

(2) Lamulo liyenela kupatsila zakakhalidwe ka anthu amenewa pomwe amakhala ndi kuchita zintchito zawo. Liyenelanso kupatsila wogwila ntchito zina azidziwa za kupita kukapuma pachaka, kukwezeledwa pa ntchito, kusinthidwa malo ogwilila ntchito, ndi zakuyimisidwa ntchito—

(a)

Pakuvomelezedwa ndi bungwe loyang’anila za nkhani za milandu; ndipo

(b) Muchilungamo ndi mosabisalika, popanda mantha, kudalila ku mbali iliyense, poyela.

(3) Malipilo, zina ndalama zapadela, ndi zina mphatso za mamembala a wogwila ntchito zakuzengedwa kwa milandu zimachokela pathumba la ndalama la Boma.

(4) Malipilo, zina ndalama zapadela ndi mphatso zina za mamembala a nthambi ya zakuzengedwa kwa milandu, zisachepetsedwe pomwe anthuwa ali pa ntchito. GAWO 3 Komishoni Yoyang’anila Nkhani Za Malamulo

189 Kukonzedwa kwa bungwe loyang’anila malamulo

(1) Bungweli loyan’anila nkhani za milandu limakhala ndi anthu awa—

(a)

wamkulu ku bwalo lozenge milandu;

(b) wotsatila wamkulu wozenga milandu ku bwalo la milandu;

(c)

mtsogoleli wamkulu ku bwalo lalikulu la milandu;

(d) wozenga mlandu m’modzi wosankhidwa ndi wozenga milandu wochekela ku bwalo Loyangana Lamulo laLikulu, bwalo la milandu lapamwamba mwamba, bwalo lozenge milandu ya nkhani za ntchito ndi, bwalo la milandu yozenga nkhani za utsogoleli;

(e)

loya wamkulu wa Boma;

(f)

wamkulu wozenga milandu ku bwalo la mejasitireti;

(g) wapampando wochokera ku bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za ntchito;

(h) maloya atatu amene akhala akugwila ntchito kwa zaka zisanu ndi ziwili ndipo moyendelana ndi lamulo la mu nyumba ya malamulo, amene ayimilila ma loya onse a muZimbabwe;

(i)

munthu wophunzila kwambili pankhani za malamulo kapena mphunzitsi wamukulu wophunzitsa malamulo amene ayenela kuyimilila maphunzitsi kuma skulu apamwamba onse amu Zimbabwe kapena, ngati palibe mabungwe otelewa, amene amasankhidwa ndi mtsogoleli wa dziko;

(j)

munthu m’modzi yemwe wakhala akugwila ntchito yoyang’anila mabuku a ndalama kwa zaka zisanu ndi ziwili, amene amayimilila bungwe lake moyendelana ndi lamulo la mu nyumba ya malamulo, limene limavomeleza anthu otelewa ndipo;

(k) munthu m’modzi wogwila ntchito yoyang’anila ndi kulemba anthu pa ntchito ali ndi zaka zisanu ndiziwili, wosankhidwa ndi mtsogoleli wa dziko.

(2) Wamukulu wozenga milandu ku bwalo lapamwamba kapena, ngati palibe, wotsatiwa wamkulu wozenga milandu ku bwalo la milandu ya pamwamba amene atsogolela bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za milandu, ndipo awiliwa ngati palibe, thumwi zimene zabwela pa msonkhanowu ziyenela kusankha m’modzi waiwo kuti atsogolele msonkhanowu.

(3) Nthumwi za mu bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za milandu monga zalembedwa mu zigawo (d), (h), (i), (j) ndi (k) ya chigawo chaching’ono (1) zimasankhidwa kuti zigwile ntchito mu bungweli kwa zaka zisanu ndi chimodzi, ndipo siziyenela kupitiliza. 190 Ntchito za bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za malamulo

(1) Bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za malamulo lingathe kupeleka malangizo ku Boma pankhani zokhudza za malamulo, kapena kayendetsedwe ka nkhani za chilungamo, ndipo Boma liyenela kulipila ntchito yopatsidwa malangizo-wa.

(2) Bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za malamulo liyenela kulimbikitsa ndi kuthandizila kudziyimilila ndi kugwila ntchito poyela ndi mwa bwino pa kayendetsedwa ka nkhani za malamulo mu Zimbabwe, ndipo ili ndi mphamvu zofunikila.

(3) Bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za malamulo, movomelezedwa ndi nduna yoyang’anila nkhani za chilungamo, lingathe kukonza malamulo ali ndi cholinga chafotokozedwa mu chigawochi.

(4) Lamulo la mu nyumba ya malamulo likupatsa mphamvu bungwe loyang’anila nkhani zamalamulo ntchito yolowetsa anthu ntchito, kulanga ndi magwilidwe a ntchito kwa anthu alowa ntchito ku bwalo lamilandu lozenga nkhani za lamulo, bwalo la pamwamba mwamba, bwalo la pa mwamba ndi bwalo lalikulu, bwalo loyang’anila nkhani za pa ntchito, bwalo la nkhani wa atsogoleli a Boma pamodzi ndi mabwalo ena.

191 Dongosolo loyenela ndi lochita zinthu mwa bwino la bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za malamulo Bungwe loyang’anila nkhani za milandu liyenela kugwila ntchito lalo bwino, mosakondela ndi mwachilungamo. GAWO 4 NKHANI ZINA

192 Lamulo logwilitsidwa ntchito Lamulo likugwilitsidwa ntchito ku mabwalo ozenga milandi mu Zimbabwe muno ndi lamulo limene linayamba kugwila ntchito pa tsiku, kapena linakonzedwanso. 193 Milandu ina imene saloledwa kuzengedwa ku mabwalo Mabwalo ozenga milandu otsatilawa ndi amene ayenela kuzenga milandu ina—

(a)

bwalo lozenga milandu za malamulo, bwalo l apamwamba mwamba, bwalo lapa mwamba, bwalo lalikulu ndi mabwalo ena a majesitireti;

(b) bwalo la milandu kapena lozenge milandu ina yapadela, milandu yokhudza malangizo, kotelo kuti milandu yamtundu imalimbikitsa malandizo mu nkhani yokhudzidwa. MUTU 9 MFUNDO ZAKAYENDESEDWE KA NTCHITO ZA BOMA NDI UTSOGOLELI

194 Zofunikila zabwino ndi mfundo zokhudza zakayendesedwe ka ntchito zaBoma

(1) Kayendesedwe ka ntchito ku nthambi zonse za Boma, kuphatikiza mabungwe ndi utumikili waBoma, ndi makampani otsogoleledwa ndi Boma ndiponso zonse zamalonda zodalila kuBoma, ziyenela kutsogoleledwa ndi ubwino wademokirasi ndi mfundo zopezeka muLamulo limeneli, kuphatikiza mfundo zotsatilazi—

(a)

kagwilidwe ka ntchito kapamwamba ka anthu ophunzila ndikutsatila malamulo a ntchito kayenela kukwezeledwa ndi kusungidwa;

(b) kugwila ntchito mofulumila ndikusamalaila zogwilitsa ntchito zipititsidwe mtsogolo;

(c)

utsogoleli wa ntchito zaBoma udalile pachitukuko;

(d) ntchito zichitike mosasankhula, poyela, moyanjana ndiponso popanda zobisala;

(e)

zofuna za anthu ziyang’anidwe munthawi yoyenela, ndiye anthu aziwilizidwa kulowa nawo muntchito zokhazikitsa mfundo zosiyanasiyana;

(f)

utsogoleli wa ntchito zaBoma ukhale ukuyang’anidwa ndi Nyumba yaMalamulo ndi anthu onse mdziko;

(g) mabungwe ndi utumiki wa Boma ukhale ukugwilizana nthawi zonse;

(h) chilungamo chiwoneke kudzela mukupatsila anthu mawu ofunikila mu nthawi yabwino;

(i)

pakhale kutsogoleledwa kwa anthu wogwila ntchito ndi ndikutukula zintchito zawo, anthu azigwilitsa ntchito luso lawo, adzisamalidwa;

(j)

utsogoleli wa ntchito zaBoma udzizindikila kusiyana kwa anthu madela osiyana-siyana a mdziko la Zimbabwe;

(k) kulembedwa zitchito, kuphunzitsidwa ndi kutukulidwa kwa munthu kukhale malinga ndi kuyenela kwake, kudziwa, chifukwa cha ubwino, moyanjana, mipata yoyanjana pakati pa azimai ndi amuna ndiponso anthu opuwala; ndiye Boma liyenela kuchitako kanthu, kuphatikiza kukonzedwa kwa malamulo opititsa mtsogolo mfundo zimenezi.

(2) Kusankhidwa kukakhala mumaofesi ku nthambi zonse za Boma, kuphatikiza wabungwe a Boma ndi mautumikili ndi makampani otsogoleledwa ndi Boma ndi kwina kulikonse kwa Boma, kuchitidwe pakuyenela kwa munthu. 195 Makampani a Boma oziyimila paokha

(1) Makampani ndi ena mabungwe ndi ena ozigwilila zintchito koma okhalila mmanja maBoma, pamwamba pochita ndi kukwanilitsa mfundo zonenedwa mu nthambi ya, 194(1), ayenela ku kuchita ntchitozawo kuti akakhalile pachiwelengelo cha utsogoleli wabwino wolandilidwa muBoma.

(2) Makampani ndi ena mabungwe ozigwilila ntchito zawo onenedwa mu nthambi ya chigawo chaching’ono (1) ayenela kukhazikitsa chilungamo, ndikagulidwe kabwino ka zinthu ndi katundu. 196 Ntchito za ogwila ntchito m’Boma ndi mfundo zautsogoleli

(1) Mphamvu zopatsidwa ku munthu wogwila ntchito yaBoma ndi chikhulupililo chomwe chiyenela kuchitidwa panjila ya—

(a)

malinga ndi zifukwa pamodzi ndi zolingana ndi Lamulo lalikulu;

(b) kusonyeza ulemu kwa anthu ndi kufuna kuwagwilila ntchito osati kuwalamulila; ndi

(c)

kupititsa mtsogolo chikhulupililo muntchito zochitidwa ndi wogwila ntchito ya Boma.

(2) Wogwila ntchito yaBoma ayenela kuzigwila bwino pakakhalidwe kawo, kuntchito ndi mdziko, pofuna kuchitila kuti pasakhale kusagwilizana pakati pa zomwe amafuna pakakhalidwe kawo ndi zaBoma, ndikusiya zilizonse zomwe zingachepetse ntchito zawo.

(3) Wogwila ntchito zautsogoleli muBoma ayenela kutsatila mfundo zotsatilazi—

(a)

kudziwa chofunika ndi kusapatula anthu pokhazikitsa maganizo;

(b) kukhalila pa zoona pogwila ntchito yaBoma;

(c)

kuvomeleza pazochita ku anthu pazochita zawo; ndi

(d) ulemu ndi chifuno chogwilila anthu ntchito. 197 Nthawi yokhala pa ntchito ku atsogoleli kumabungwe a Boma Lamulo ladziko likhoza kugamula nthawi yomwe atsogoleli kumabungwe a Boma angakhale pa ntchito ndi ena otsgolela makampani a Boma. 198 Malamulo okhazikitsa zopezeka pa Mutu 9 Lamulo la Boma liyenela kukhazikitsa njila zochita kuti zolembedwa pa mutu umenewu zichitidwe, kuphatikizako—

(a)

2013

kuchita kuti atsogoleli azitulutsa zomwe alinazo, katundu ndi chuma;

(b) kukhazikitsa zoyenela kutsatilidwa ndi wogwila ntchito yaBoma;

(c)

kunena momwe akufuna kuti utsogoleli wabwino uziyendela ku makampani ndi mabungwe a Boma;

(d) kukhazikitsa njila zoweluza ndikupatsila zilango zoyenela kuonse omwe angasokoneze mfundo zonenedwa pa mutu umenewu kapena zina zonenedwa muchiganizo chikulu cha paragraph (b). MUTU 10 OGWILA NTCHITOM’BOMA

199 Ogwila ntchito m’Boma

(1) Pali gulu limodzi la ogwila ntchito waBoma, womwe uli ndi udindo wotsogolela ntchito za dziko la Zimbabwe.

(2) Gulu la ogwila ntchito waBoma lili ndi anthu olembedwa ntchito ndi Boma kusiya kwa—

(a)

mamembala a mabungwe achitetezo ndi ena achitetezo omwe angakhazikitsidwe;

(b) wozenga milandu, wozenga milandu kubwalo la majesitirete ndi anthu otsogolela mabwalo a milandi okhazikitsidwa paLamulo ladziko;

(c)

mamembala a maKomishoni okhazikitsidwa ndi Lamulo limeneli;

(d) wogwila ntchito kuNyumba yaMalamulo; ndi

(e)

aliyense yemwe ntchito yake ikunenedwa ndi, Lamulo limeneli kapena lina lokonzedwa ndi kunyumba yamalamulo, sakhala mu gulu la ogwilantchito m’Boma.

(3) Lamulo liliyenela kupatsila njila zokhazikitsa dongosolo, kayimidwe, utsogoleli, kayendesedwe ka ntchito, zaulemu, malingana ndi nthambi ya chigawo 203, zotsogolela kachitidwe kantchito zaBoma kugulu la anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma. 200 Zochitidwa ndi komishoni loyang’anila anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma

(1) Komishoni ya anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma liyenela kutsatila Lamulo lalikululi.

(2) Palibe membala ya Ukapolo waBoma emwe angalandile kuchita zowoneka kuti zili kunja kwa lamulo.

(3) Palibe ogwila ntchito m’Boma atsatila lamulo losayenela ndi losavomelezedwa—

(a)

kutsata makhalidwe ochokela ku chipan cha ndale;

(b) kupititsa mtsogolo zofuna zachipani cha ndale chili chonse;

(c)

kusokoneza zofuna za chipani cha ndale chilichonse; kapena

(d) kusokoneza ufulu wofunikila ndi kumasuka kwa munthu.

(4) Nthumwi zogwila ntchito m’Boma siziyenela kukhala zikugwilila ntchito kuchipani cha ndale chili chonse.

(5) Lamulo liyenela kupatsa mpata kuti ogwila ntchito za Boma asatenge mbali pa nkhani za ndale.

201 Nduna yoyang’anila ogwila ntchito m’Boma Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha ndikukhazikitsa nduna yoyang’anila anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma. 202 Kukhazikitsidwa kwa Komishoni yoyang’anila anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma

(1) Pali komishoni yoyang’anila wogwila ntchito muBoma yophatikizila—

(a)

wapampando ndi wachiwili ku wapampando; ndi

(b) mamembala woyambila pa awili koma wosapambana asanu; wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko.

(2) Mamembala a Komishoni ya anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma ayenela kusankhidwa chifukwa chakudziwa kwawo kapena luso muutsogoleli, kutsogolela muntchito zaBoma. 203 Ntchito zaKomishoni yoyang’anila wogwila ntchito muBoma

(1) Komishoni ya ogwila ntchito muBoma imachita zintchito zotsatila zi—

(a)

kulemba ntchito anthu wophunzila ndikudziwa ntchito kuti agwile ntchito muBoma;

(b) malinga ndi nthambi ya chigawo 65(5), kukhazikitsa malamulo zakayendesedwe ka ntchito, malipilo ndi zina zomwe angapatsidwe anthu ogwila ntchito muBoma;

(c)

kuyang’ana za mphamvu zobweletsa ulemu pakati pawogwila ntchito ku Komishoni ya anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma;

(d) kufufuza ndi kukhazikitsa madandaulo a anthu ogwila ntchito m”Boma zokhudza zina zake kapena zosachitidwa;

(e)

kukhazikitsa njila zochita kuti ntchito ndi kakhakidwe ka wogwila ntchito muBoma kakhale bwino;

(f)

kuchita kuti ogwila ntchito m’Boma agwile ntchito zawo bwino ndi moyenela;

(g) kulangiza Mtsogoleli wa dziko pamodzi ndi nduna yoyang’anila antho ogwila ntchito m’Boma;

(h) kupititsa mtsogolo ubwino ndi mfundo zokhazikitsidwa mulamulo limeneli; ndi

(i)

kuchita zina zonse zomwe komishoni imeneyi ingauzidwe ndi Lamulo limeneli kapena lina Lamulo la m’dziko.

(2) Komishoni Komishoni ya anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma ndi lothandizila Nduna yoyang’anila anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma ndipo lingathe kukonza malangizo ena moyendelana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (1).

(3) Komishoni ya anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma iyenela kuchita ntchito zake malinga ndi mfundo zolembedwa zomwe zingachokele ku Nduna yoyang’anila anthu ogwila ntchito.

(4) Mukukhazikitsa zamalipilo ndi zina zopatsidwa zapadela za ogwila ntchito m’Boma, Komishoni ya ogwila ntchito m’Boma iyenela kutsa ndi vomelezo mfundo zochokela kwa Mtsogoleli wadziko zitalandilidwa ndi Nduna yowona nkhani za chuma ndi Nduna yoyang’ana ogwil ntchito m’Boma.

204 Akazembe ndi ena woyimila Zimbabwe kumaiko akunja Mtsogoleli wadziko akhoza kusankha anthu oyimila Zimbabwe kumayiko kapena kumabungwe apadziko lonse lapansi, ndiye akhozanso kuchotsa anthuwa pazintchito zimenezi. 205 Alembi aakulu mu maunduna a m’Boma

(1) Alembi aakulu ogwila ntchito mu nthambi zonse zam’Boma amasankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko atakambilana ndi Komishoni yowona za anthu ogwila ntchito m’Bomali.

(2) Ntchito yaMlembi waMkulu ku Unduna ndi zaka zisanu, ndiye akhoza kupatsidwanso zina zisanu atawona kuti akugwila ntchito yake bwino. MUTU 11 MABUNGWE A ZACHITETEZO

GAWO 1 ZOSIYANA-SIYANA ZAPADELA

206 Chitetezo cha dziko

(1) Zolinga zachitetezo cha Zimbabwe ziyenela kusonyeza zakuti nzika zonse za Zimbabwe zikhale moyanjana, bata ndi mtendele, wosawopa, ndi kulemela.

(2) Chitetezo cha Zimbabwe chiyenela kukhalila kutsatila Lamulo limeneli ndi Malamulo adziko.

(3) Maka-maka, zofunikila pakuteteza dziko ziyenela kuchitidwa akuyang’ana nkhani zotsatilazi—

(a)

ufulu, kumasuka, mfundo za ulamulilo wa anthu onse, zokhazikidwa muLamulo limeneli; ndi

(b) Utsogoleli wotsatila pa lamulo. 207 Woyang’anila zachitetezo

(1) Woyang’anila za chitetezo a Zimbabwe ndi—

(a)

asilikali a chitetezo;

(b) a Polisi;

(c)

a Chitetezo chobisalika cha chinsisi;

(d) woyang’anila zakundende ndi anthu omangidwa; ndi

(e)

lina lililonse bungwe lachitetezo lokhazikidwa ndi Lamulo.

(2) Mabungwe a chitetezo akutsogoleledwa ndi Lamulo limeneli, Mtsogoleli wadziko, Gulu laNduna zaBoma amayang’anidwa ndi Nyumba yaMalamulo.

(3) Nthumwi zachitetezo ziyenela kusonyeza anthu onse a mdziko ndi komwe amachokela kosiyana-siyana. 208 Kagwilidwe ka ntchito ndi nthumwi zachitetezo

(1) Mamembala a zachitetezo ayenela kugwila ntchito zawo motsatila zolembedwa muLamulo limeneli.

(2) Palibe pakati pa mabungwe a zachitetezo kapena mamembala awo ayenela, kugwila ntchito zawo—

(a)

kugwila ntchito motenga mbali pa nkhani za ndale;

(b) kupititsa mtsogolo zofuna za chipani cha ndale chili chonse;

(c)

kusokoneza zofuna za chipani cha ndale chili chonse; kapena

(d) kupondeleza ufulu ndi kumasuka kwa munthu aliyense.

(3) Mamembala a zachitetezo sayenela kukhala wogwilila ntchito za chipani cha ndale chili chonse kapena lina bungwe.

(4) Mamembala a chitetezo omwe ali pantchito sangalembedwe ntchito kapena kulowa ku bungwe la anthu wamba, koma kuti ikakhala nthawi ya maphokoso mudziko. 209 Bungwe la asilikali loyang’anila chitetezo cha m’dziko

(1) Pali bungwe yoyang’anila zachitetezo m’dziko yokhazikitsidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wa m’dziko ngati wapampando, Wotsatila Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi Nduna zina, ndiponso mamembala a mabungwe a zachitetezo ndi ena anthu malinga ndi zokhazikitsidwa ndi Lamulo ladziko.

(2) Ntchito za bungwe la nkhani zaChitetezo cha m’dziko ndi—

(a)

kumanga mfundo zokhudza chitetezo cha Zimbabwe;

(b) kuuza ndikupatsila maganizo ndi mtsogoleli wa m’dziko nkhani zokhudza chitetezo cha dziko; ndiyenso

(c)

kugwila ntchito yomwe ingapatsidwe pa Lamulo ladziko.

(3) Atsogoleli a zachitetezo ayenela kupatsila malipoti a zakayimidwe ka nkhani zachitetezo mu Zimbabwe momwe bungwe la nkhani zachitetezo ingafunile. 210 Njila zotsatila popeleka nazo madandaulo Lamulo ladziko liyenela kukhazikitsa njila zoyenela pa kapelekedwe ndi kulongosola madandaulo a anthu pa nkhani yakusagwila ntchito bwino kwa mamembala a zachitetezo, ndikuthetsa madandaulo awo. GAWO 2 ASILIKALI A ZACHITETEZO

211 Asilikali a zachitetezo

(1) Asilikali a Zachitetezo mu Zimbabwe akukhalila ndi Asilikali, Asilikali apa ndege ndi ena omwe angakhazikitsidwe pa Lamulo ladziko.

(2) Asilikali a Chitetezo ndi omwe ali asilikali a nkhondo mu Zimbabwe.

(3) Asilikali a zachitetezo ayenela kulemekeza ufulu ndi kumasuka kwa anthu onse ndipo sayenela kukhala pambuyo pa chipani cha ndale chili chonse, ayenela kugwilila ntchito munthu ali yense, wokonda dziko lawo, ukatswili ndi kumvela ku anthu a m’dziko malinga ndi Lamulo ladziko.

(4) Asilikali a chitetezo ayenela kusungidwa ndikukhalila paulemu.

(5) Lamulo la m’dziko liyenela kukhazikitsa njila zochita kuti pakhale kalongosoledwe, kayimidwe, utsogoleli, ulemu ndi kukwezeleza ndikubwezela pansi asilikali ndi ena anthu wamba ndi, malingana ndi chigawo218, zokhudza zakagwilidwe kantchito ndi mamembala a mabungwe a zachitetezo.

212 Ntchito ya asilikali a chitetezo Ntchito ya Asilikali a Chitetezo ndiyoteteza Zimbabwe, anthu ake, chitetezo cha dziko ndizolinga za dziko ndi malo onse a dziko ndi kulemekeza Lamulo la dziko. 213 Kutumizidwa kwa Asilikali a chitetezo

(1) Malinga ndi Lamulo limeneli, Mtsogoleli wadziko yekha, ndiye Mtsogoleli waMukulu wa asilikali a zachitetezo, ali ndi mphamvu—

(a)

zotumiza Asilikali aChitetezo ku nkhondo; kapena

(b) ali ndi mphamvu zolamulila kagwilidwe ka ntchito ndi Asilikali aChitetezo.

(2) Ndi mphamvu za Mtsogoleli wadziko asilikali achitetezo akhoza kutumidwa—

(a)

poteteza Zimbabwe;

(b) kuthandizila a polisi pokhazikitsa bata mudziko; kapena

(c)

kuthandizila a polisi ndi anthu amudziko pa nthawi ya maphokoso ochitika dziko lonse.

(3) Ndi mphamvu za Mtsogoleli wadziko, asilikali a chitetezo akhoza kutumizidwa kunja kwa dziko la Zimbabwe—

(a) kupita kukasungitsa ndi kuyang’anila kuti pakhale bata pautsogoleli wa bungwe la mgwilizano wa maiko onse kapena mabungwe ena amuno, achigawo cha kumwela kuno ndi apadziko lonse lapansi komwe Zimbabwe ali nthumwi;

(b) kukateteza malo adziko lina;

(c)

kukwanilitsa zigwilizano; kapena

(d) kuteteza chitetezo cha dziko la Zimbabwe.

(4) Ndi chiwelengelo cha mphambu ziwili pa zitatu za chiwelengelo cha mamembala onse a Nyumba ya Malamulo pophatikizila ndi Nyumba ya Seneti akhoza kuletsa kuti asilikali a chitetezo asatuluke kupita ku maiko akunja, Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kuchita zonse kubweza asilikali a Chitetezo, poyang’anila kuti ateteze dziko ndi anthu ake.

(5) Pomwe Nyumba ya Malamulo yakhazikitsa kuti asilikali asatuluke m’dziko, Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusintha kapena kuyang’ana bwino ganizo lake pofuna kuteteza umoyo ndi katundu wa dziko. 214 Kufotokoza zakutumidwa kwa asilikali a chitetezo mu zandale Ngati Asilikali aChitetezo atatumizidwa—

(a)

mu Zimbabwe kuti athandizile kukhazikitsa bata ndi mtendele; kapena

(b) kunja kwa Zimbabwe; mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kuchititsa kuti Nyumba yaMalamulo iwuzidwe mofulumila, ndi njila yoyenela napatsidwa zonse, zifukwa zakutumilidwa kwawo ndi—

(i) komwe atumizidwa mu Zimbabwe, malo omwe atumizidwa;

(ii) komwe atumizilidwa kunja kwa Zimbabwe, mudziko lomwe atumizilidwa.

215 Nduna yoyang’ana za asilikali a chitetezo Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha Nduna yoyang’ana za nkhani za asilikali a Chitetezo. 216 Kutsogoleledwa kwa asilikali

(1) Lamulo limathandizila kuti—

(a)

asilikali a Chitetezo ayenela kutsogoleledwa ndi wamukulu wa asilikali m’modzi; kapena

(b) nthambi iliyonse ya Asilikali a Chitetezo, kapena ziwili kapena kupambana ali pamodzi, ali pautsogoleli umodzi.

(2) Mtsogoleli aliyense wa asilikali a chitetezo, ndi Mtsogoleli aliyense wa nthambi ya Asilikali a Chitetezo amasankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko akugwilizana ndi Nduna yoyang’anila nkhani za chitetezo.

(3) Atsogoleli a asilikali a chitetezo ndi atsogoleli a nthambi za asilikali achitetezo amasankhidwa kukhala pa ntchito kwa zaka zosapambana zisanu, ndiye munthu sayenela kugwila ntchito imeneyi kwa zaka zoposa khumi.

(4) Munthu emwe agwila ntchito yaUtsogoleli wa nthambi yina ya asilikali a chitetezo akhozanso kusankhidwa kutsogolela asilikali achitetezo, koma munthu emwe agwila ntchito ngathi Mtsogoleli wa Asilikali a Chitetezo sangasankhidwe kutsogolela nthambi ya asilikali a chitetezo kapena kukhala Mtsogoleli wa nthambi iliyense ya chitetezo.

(5) Mtsogoleli aliyense wa asilikali a chitetezo ndi wotsogolela nthambi zina za asilikali a chitetezo, ayenela kugwila ntchito zawo malinga ndi mfundo zokhazikitsidwa ndi Nduna yoyang’anila za chitetezo. Asilikali wogwila ntchito pautsogoleli wa Mtsogoleli wadziko. 217 Kakhazikitsidwe ka komishoni yowona za chitetezo ndi asilikali

(1) Pali Komishoni ya asilikali ya chitetezo yokhala ndi wapampando yemwe ali wapampando ku Komishoni yowona za anthu ogwila ntchito m’Boma, ndipo nthumwi zina ziwili kapena kupitilila koma zosafika zisanu, zimene zimasankhidwa ndi mtsogoleli wa dziko.

(2) Mamembala a Komishoni ya Asilikali a Chitetezo ayenela kusankhidwa chifukwa cha kudziwa kwawo ndi luso lomwe ali nalo pazakayendesedwe ka ntchito ndi utsogoleli, kudziwa za usilikali, kuphunzila kwawo ndikungokwanila kwawo kusankhidwa ndikupatsidwa ntchito, ndi—

(a)

theka la anthuwa amenewa ayenela kukhala anthu omwe sanalowe nchito ya usilikali ndipo sanakhalepo ntchito ya Usilikali;

(b) m’modzi waiwo ayenela kukhala kuti anagwila ntchito ya wamkulu wa Asilikali aChitetezo kwa zaka zoposa zisanu. 218 Ntchito za komishoni ya asilikali a chitetezo

(1) Komishoni ya Asilikali a Chitetezo ili ndi ntchito zatsatilazi—

(a)

kulemba ntchito anthu ophunzila ndi odziwa ntchito kuti athe kugwila ntchito ya Asilikali;

(b) kukhazikitsa ndi kuyang’anila kagwilidwe ka ntchito, kuphatikizako za malipilo, ena malipilo apadela za mamembala a Asilikali;

(c)

kuyang’anila ubwino wonse wa Asilikali a Chitetezo kuti adzigwila ntchito zawo mofulumila ndi njila yapamwamba;

(d) kuwona kuti mamembala a Asilikali a Chitetezo akuvomeleza zolembedwa pa nthambi ya chigawo 208;

(e)

kukhazikitsa kumvetsetsana ndi ubale pakati pa a Asilikali a Chitetezo ndi anthu onse mdziko;

(f)

kuthandizila Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi Nduna yoyang’ana za Asilikali a Chitetezo; ndi

(g) ndikuchita zina zintchito zomwe zingapatsidwe ku Komishoni imeneyi ndi Nyumba ya Malamulo.

(2) Komishoni ya Asilikali a Chitetezo, povomelezedwa ndi Nduna yowona nkhani za zachitetezo, ikhoza kuyikako malamulo ena moyendelana ndi chigawo chaching’ono (1).

(3) kukhazikitsa malipilo, zina ndalama ndi mphatso zapadela za nthumwi za Komishoni yaChitetezo, Komishoni-yi iyenela kugwila ntchito movomelezedwe ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko mogwilizana ndi Nduna yoyang’anila za ndalama pambuyo pokambilana ndi Nduna yoyang’anila nkhani za Asilikali a Chitetezo. GAWO 3 A POLISI

219 Apolisi ndi ntchito zawo

(1) Pali nthambi ya A Polisi womwe ntchito yawo ndi—

(a)

kuyang’anila,kufufuza ndi kuteteza milandu;

(b) kusunga chitetezo cha m’dziko la Zimbabwe;

(c)

kuteteza ndi kusunga miwoyo ya anthu ndi katundu wa dziko;

(d) kusunga bata ndi mtendele pa lamulo; ndi

(e)

kukweza Lamulo lalikulu la m’dziko polimbikitsa lamulo popanda mantha ndi kukondela.

(2) Apolisi ayenela kugwila ntchito zawo akugwilizana ndi—

(a)

bungwe la chinsinsi lomwe lingakhazikitsidwe ndi Lamulo;

(b) bungwe lililonse limene lingakhazikitsidwe ndi Lamulo ndi cholinga chofuna kufufuza, kuyang’anitsitsa ndi kuteteza milandu ina; ndi

(c)

mabungwe akuzigawo zosiyana-siyana ndi amaiko apansi anakonzedwa ndi cholinga chothetsa kupalamulidwa kwa milandu.

(3) A Polisi sayenela kutenga mbali iliyense yokondela, pogwila ntchito ndi maiko ena onse, okhala ndi chikondi cha dziko, pogwila ntchito mokhulupilika ndi kukonda dziko, kugwila ntchito ndi ukatswili ndi kuvomeleza ku mphamvu za anthu malinga ndi Lamulo lalikulo.

(4) Lamulo liyenela kufotokozeledwa kutanthauza, kagwilitsidwe, kutsogoleledwa, lamulo, zoyenela pa ntchito, zaulemu ndi kukwezeledwa ndi kutsitsidwa pamipando kwa wogwila ntchito yaPolisi, moyendelana ndi chigawo 223, ndi zonse zokhudza kagwilidwe ka ntchito kwa a Polisi. 220 Nduna yoyanganila a Polisi Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha ndi kutchula Nduna yoyang’anila nkhani za aPolisi.

221 Wamkulu wotsogolela a Polisi

(1) Apolisi amatsogoleledwa ndi wamukulu mkulu wa a Polisi amene amasankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko mogwilizana ndi Nduna yoyang’anila nkhani za a Polisi.

(2) Wamukulu wotsogoleli a Polisi amakhala pa ntchito kwa zaka zisanu zomwe angawonjezedwe kamodzi.

(3) Munthu yemwe agwila ntchito ngati wamukulu wotsogoleli a Polisi sadzapatsidwanso ntchito yotsogolela nthambi ili yonse yoteteza dziko.

(4) Wamukulu wotsogolela a Polisi ayenela kugwila ntchito yake malinga ndi mfundo zolembedwa kuchokela kuNduna yoyang’ana za Polisi akupatsidwa udindowu kuchokela ku Mtsogoleli wadziko. 222 Kukhazikitsidwa ndi wogwila ntchito ku komishoni ya a Polisi

(1) Kuli Komishoni ya a Polisi yokhala ndi wapampando, yemwe ayenelanso kukhala wapampando ku Komishoni yoyang’anila wogwila ntchito muBoma, ndi ena wosachepela awili koma wosapambana asanu ndi mmodzi ndi ena wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko.

(2) Komishoni ya a Polisi ayenela kusankhidwa pachifukwa cha kudziwa kwawo ntchito ndi luso pakutsatila malamulo ndi ubwino, kutsogolela ntchito, kapena maphunzilo awo kapenanso kuyenela kwawo pa ntchito, ndi—

(a)

ndiye theke la anthuwa sayenela kukhala anagwilapo ntchito ya a Polisi kapena omwe anali kale wogwila ntchito ku a Polisi;

(b) m’modzi wawo akhale munthu yemwe anagwila ntchito yapampando wapamwamba ku Polisi kwa zaka zisanu. 223 Ntchito za komishoni ya a Polisi

(1) Komishoni ya a Polisi ili ndi ntchito zotsatilazi—

(a)

kulemba ntchito anthu oyenela pamaphunzilo awo ndi kuti agwile ntchito ku nthambi ya Polisi;

(b) kukhazikitsa malipilo, zina ndalama ndi mphatso zapadela, za a Apolisi;

(c)

kuchita kuti pakhale ubwino ndi kayendetsedwe ka ntchito kabwino ku nthambi ya Polisi ndikukhazikitsa ubwino wapamwamba;

(d) kuwona kuti a Polisi akugwila ncthito yawo bwino moyendelana ndi chigawo 208;

(e)

kukhazikitsa ubale wabwino pakati pa a Polisi ndi anthu;

(f)

kupereka malangizo kwa Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi Nduna pa nkhani zilizonse zokhudza Polisi; ndi

(g) kuchita zina ntchito zina zomwe Komishoni ingapatsidwe ndi Lamulo ladziko.

(2) Komishoni ya a Polisi, movomelezana ndi Unduna yoyang’anila nthambi ya Polisi, akhoza kukhazikitsa zotsatilidwa pa ntchito malinga ndi chigawoching’ono (1).

(3) Pakukhazikitsa malipilo, ndalama zina ndi mphatso zapadela za ogwila ntchito ya Polisi, Komishoni ya a Polisi iyenela kugwila ntchito movomelezedwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko mogwilizana ndi Nduna yoyanga’nila nkhani za a Polisi.

GAWO 4 NTHAMBI YA A POLISI YOFUFUZA NKHANI

224 Kukhazikidwa kwa nthambi ya a Polisi yofufuza nkhani

(1) Nthambi ya a Polisi yogwila nthito yaBoma mofufuza, kusiya kwa kwa nthambi zofufuza ku nthambi za a Polisi ndi Asilikazi a Chitetezo, iyenela kukhazikitsidwa pa Lamulo ladziko, kapena kusankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko kapena ndi bungwe la Nduna za Boma.

(2) Nthambi yonse yachitetezo yofufuza nkhani iyenela kugwila anthu onse moyanjana, kuthandizila anthu adziko lonse, kukonda dziko, kugwila ntchito panjila yapamwamba ndi kulemekeza anthu malinga ndi zolembedwa mu Lamulo limeneli. 225 Nduna yoyanganila nthambi ya chitetezo yofufuza nkhani Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha Nduna yoyang’ana za chitetezo chofufuza nkhani m’dziko. 226 Utsogoleli ndi kuyang’anila nthambi ya chitetezo chofufuza nkhani

(1) Nthambi ya a chitetezo chofufuza iyenela kutsogoleledwa ndi wa Mtsogoleli woyang’ana za Chitetezo chofufuza nkhani chokhazikitsidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko kwa zaka zisanu zomwe zingapatsidwe kachiwili.

(2) Mtsogoleli wa nthambi ya chitetezo chofufuza ayenela kugwila ntchito yake malinga ndi mfundo zolembedwa kuchokela ku Nduna yoyang’anila zachitetezo chofufuza akupatsidwa mphamvu ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko.

(3) Munthu yemwe agwila ntchito ngati Mtsogoleli wa nthambi ya chitetezo chofufuza sadzasankhidwa kukhala mtsogoleli wanthambi iliyense yachitetezo. GAWO 5 WOTHANDIZILA NDI KULANGA AKAIDI KUNDENDE

227 Nthambi ya wothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi akundende ndi ntchito yake

(1) Pali nthambi ya wothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi yomwe ntchito yake ndi—

(a)

kuteteza dziko kuchokela ku anthu amaganizo oyipa kudzela mukuyika anthuwa kundende ndi kuwathandizila atapezedwa ndi mlandu ndi ena woyenela kukhala kumeneko pa lamulo, ndikubwezeledwa kwawo pakati pa anthu; ndi

(b) kutsogolela ntchito ndi zonse za nthambi yothandizila akaidi ali mundende.

(2) Nthambi ya wothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi iyenela kugwila ntchito yake mosakondela mbali iliyonse, ithandizile anthu onse m’dziko, kukonda dziko, pa ukatswili ndi kulemekeza anthu m’dziko moyendelana ndi Lamulo.

(3) Lamulo liyenela kukhazikitsa, kayimidwe, utsogoleli, zokhudza za ntchito, zaulemu ndi kukwezelezedwa ndi kutsitsidwa kwa wogwila ntchito, ndi thumwi zina moyendelana ndi chigawo 231, ndi zina zofunikila kwa ogwila ntchito ku nthambi yothandizila kulanga akaidi ali ku ndende.

228 Nduna yoyang’anila nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kusankha ndikukhazikitsa Nduna yoyang’ana nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende. 229 Mtsogoleli wa nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende

(1) Nthambi ya wothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende ili paUlamulilo wa Mtsogoleli wa wothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko atakambilana ndi Nduna yoyang’anila nthambi ya wothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi akundende.

(2) Mtsogoleli wa nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende amakhala pa ntchito kwa zaka zisanu zomwe zingapatsidwe kachiwili.

(3) Munthu yemwe agwila ntchito ngati Mtsogoleli wa nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende sangasankhidwe kutsogolela nthambi yachitetezo iliyense.

(4) Mtsogoleli wa nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende amagwila ntchito yake pa mfundo zolembedwa ndikupatsidwa ndi Nduna yoyang’ana nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende atapatsidwa mphamvu ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko. 230 Kukhazikitsidwa ndi wogwila ntchito ku komishoni ya nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende

(1) Pali Komishoni ya nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende yokhala ndi wapampando, yemwe ayenela kukhala wapampando ku Komishoni ya anthu ogwila ntchioto m’Boma, ndi anthu wosachepela awili ndi okusapambana asanu ndi mmodzi osankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko.

(2) Komishoni ya nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende ayenela kusankhidwa chifukwa chakudziwa ndi luso pogwila ntchito, utsogoleli, nkhani za chitetezo, kapena chifukwa chamaphunzilo awo kapena kukwanila kwawo kusankhidwa; ndi—

(a)

theka la anthuwa asakhale omwe anagwila ntchito ku nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende;

(b) m’modzi waiwo akhale munthu yemwe anagwila ntchito yapamwamba ku nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende kwa zaka zisanu. 231 Ntchito ya komishoni ya nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akundende

(1) Nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende ili ndi ntchito zotsatila zi—

(a)

kulemba ntchito anthu woyenela, wodziwa ntchito ndi wophunzila kuti alowe ku nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende;

(b) kukhazikitsa ndi kuyang’anila zakagwilidwe kantchito, kukhazikitsa malipilo, zina ndalama ndi mphatso zapadela, za a Komishoni ya nthambi yothandizila akundende;

(c)

kuyang’anila kuti pali utsogoleli ndi ubwino wa nthambi wothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende ndikuwona kuti nthambi imeneyi ikukhala pamwamba;

(d) kuyang’anila kuti mamembala a nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende atsatile chigawo 208;

(e)

kubweletsa ubale pakati nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende ndi anthu mdziko;

(f)

2013

kupeleka maganizo kwa Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi Nduna pa nkhani zokhudza nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende; ndi

(g) kuchita zina ntchito zomwe zingapatsidwe ku Komishoni moyendelana ndi Lamulo ladziko.

(2) Komishoni ya nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende ikhoza, ndi thandizilo kuchokela ku Nduna yoyang’anila nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende ikhoza kukhazikitsa kayendesedwe kantchito ndichifukwa zoyendelana ndi chigawo (1).

(3) Mukukhazikitsa za malipilo, ndalama ndizina mphatso za mamembala a nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende iyenela kupatsidwa chilolezo ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko pogwilizana ndi Nduna yoyang’ana nkhani za nthambi yothandizila ndi kulanga akaidi kundende. MUTU 12 MAKOMISHONI OZIYIMILA OKHA NDI KUTHANDIZILA ULAMULILO WABWINO

GAWO 1 ZOSIYANA-SIYANA

232 Makomishoni oziyimila okha Otsatilawa ndi maKomishoni oziyimila okha—

(a)

Komishoni yoyang’anila chisankho;

(b) Komishoni yoyang’anila ufulu wa anthu;

(c)

Komishoni yazoyanjana pakati pa azimai ndi amuna;

(d) Komishoni ya mabungwe a nkhani; ndi

(e)

Komishoni ya za Bata ndi zokulukililana.

233 Zolinga za makomishoni oziyimila okha MaKomishoni oziyimila okha ali ndi zolinga zotsatilazi pamwamba pazomwe anapatsidwa—

(a)

kuthandizila ndi kulimbikitsa ufulu ndi ulamulilo wabwino;

(b) kusamalila ubwino ndi zofuna za anthu;

(c)

kupititsa mtsogolo utsogoleli wotsatila Lamulo;

(d) kubweletsa chilungamo ndi ubwino kumabungwe a Boma;

(e)

kuteteza zoyang’anila ulamulilo wabwino ndi mfundo Boma, mabungwe ndi utumikili wa Boma, ndi makampani otsoleledwa ndi boma; ndi

(f)

kuchita kuti zoyipa zilongosoledwe.

234 Wogwila ntchito kuma komishoni oziyimila okha MaKomishoni oziyimila okha ali ndi mphamvu zolemba anthu ntchito ndi, malinga ndi Lamulo amatsogolela kagwilidwe kantchito. 235 Ufulu wa ma komishoni

(1) Ma Komishoni odziyimilila okha—

(a)

apadela ndinso sakudalila utsogoleli wa aliyense;

(b) ayenela kugwila ntchito malinga ndi Lamulo limeneli; ndiye

(c)

ayenela kugwila ntchito zawo popanda mantha, kapena kufuna ndi kukana ena anthu;

ngakhale kuti amayankha kuNyumba yaMalamulo pofuna kugwila ntchito zawo bwino.

(2) Boma ndi mabungwe onse oyimilila Boma pa nkhani ina iliyonse iyenela, moyendelana ndi lamulo, liyenela kuthandizila ma komishoni odziyimilila okha kuti athe kudziyimilila momasuka, posatenga mbali, ubwino, ndi kugwila ntchito moyenela.

(3) Palibe munthu oyenela kulowelela pankhani za makomishoni oziyimila okha. 236 Mamembala makomishoni oziyimila okha sayenela kulowelela mu ndale

(1) Mamembala a maKomishoni oziyimila okha sayenela, pochita ntchito zawo—

(a) kutenga mbali;

(b) kupititsa mtsogolo zofuna za chipani cha ndale chili chonse;

(c) kusokoneza zofuna za zipani za ndale zokhalila pa Lamulo; kapena

(d) kusokoneza maufulu ndi kumasuka kwa munthu aliyense.

(2) Anthu omwe ali mamembala a a zipani za ndale omw akulowa ntchito kuma Komishoni amenewa ayenela kusiya kukhala mamembala azipani zi mofulumila ndiye mkati ma masiku makumi atatu atapatsidwa ntchito.

(3) Ngathi membala ya Komishoni yoziyimila yokha—

(a) atakhala membala ya chipani cha ndale kapena lina bungwe; kapena

(b) atakhala membala ya chipani cha ndale kapena lina bungwe pozikhazikitsa kukomishoni, akalephela kusiya kukhala membala mkati ma masiku makumi atatu akusankhidwa; ayima pomwepo kukhala membala ya komishoni yokhudzidwayo. 237 Kusankhidwa ndi Kuchotsedwa ntchito kwa mamembala a makomishoni oziyimila okha

(1) Pofuna kuchula munthu kuti asankhidwe kulowa ku Komishoni yoziyimila yokha, komiti yoyangana zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo iyenela—

(a)

kufalitsa uthenga wa ntchitoyo kumapepala a nkhani;

(b) kuyitana anthu kuti apatsile mayina a anthu omwe angafune;

(c)

kukhazikitsa mpikisano yosankha munthuyo;

(d) konza kaundandanda ka anthu omwe angadzalowenso pa mpikisano womalizila asanesankhe; ndi

(e)

kupeleka anthuwa kuMtsogoleli wadziko.

(2) Membala ya Komishoni yoziyimila yokha ikhoza kuchotsedwa ntchito kupela ngathi pa nkhani yakuti membala yi—

(a)

sakutha kuchita ntchito yake chifukwa cha kudwala pathupi kapena kuzungulila bongo;

(b) apezeka kuti sakudziwa ntchito yake;

(c)

apezeka ndi mlandu wosayendetsa bwino ncthito; kapena

(d) akhala wosayenela kukhala membala ya komishoni imeneyo.

(3) Njila yochotsa nayo wozenga milandu pantchito ndi njila yomwe akugwilitsa ntchito kuchotsa mamembala a Komishoni yoziyimila yokha.

GAWO 2 BUNGWE LA ZACHISANKHO

238 Kukhazikitsidwa kwa bungwe la zachisankho ndi wogwila ntchito kumeneko

(1) Pali Komishoni yodziwika kuti Komishoni ya Chisankho yokhalila ndi—

(a)

wapampando osankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko atalankhulana ndi Komishoni ya zakuzengedwa kwa milandu ndi Komiti yoyang’anila zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo; ndi

(b) ena anthu asanu ndi atatu osankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko kuchokela pa anthu osakhalila osafika khumi ndi awili opelekedwa ndi Komiti yoyang’anila zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo.

(2) Wapampando ku Komishoni yoyang’anila za chisankho ayenela kukhala wozenga milandu kapena emwe anali kale wozenga milandu kapena munthu wokwanila kusankhidwa kapena emwe angasankhidwe kukhala wozenga milandu.

(3) Ngathi kusankhidwa kwa wapampando ku Komishoni ya chisankho sakukhalila sikuli kugwilizana ndi Komishoni yoyang’ana za Malamulo, Mtsogolei wadziko ayenela kuchita kuti Komiti yazochitika kuNyumba yaMalamulo mofulumila.

(4) Mamembala a Komishoni ya zachisankho ayenela kukhala nzika ya Zimbabwe ndiye akhale wozigwila pabwalo ndi kunyumba, wodziwa ntchito ndi kukhala ndi luso.

(5) Mamembala a Komishoni yoyang’anila za chisankho agwila ntchito zawo kwa zaka zisanu ndi chimodzi, ndiye akhozanso kusankhidwa mbuyo momaliza nthawi imeneyi, palibe munthu emwe adzasankhidwenso kukhala ku Komishoni Yoziyimila yokha atagwila ntchito kwa zaka khumi ndi ziwili. 239 Ntchito ya komishoni yoyang’anila za chisankho Komishoni yachisankho imachita zintchito zotsatila zi—

(a)

kukonzekela, kuchita ndi kutsogolela—

(i) masankho ku ofesi ya Mtsogoleli wadziko ndi Nyumba yaMalamulo;

(ii) zisankho kumakansulo akumatauni ndi zigawo ndi akumidzi;

(iii) zisankho za konsolo ya mfumu zadziko yokhazikitsidwa ndi chigawo 285; ndi

(iv) zisankho zofunsa maganizo a anthu; ndikuwona kuti zisankho ndi zisankho zofunsa maganizo a anthu zachitika bwino, muchilungamo, popanda kachitidwe koyipa koma malinga pa lamulo;

(b) kutsogolela zisankho za Mtsogoleli wa Seneti ndi mneneli waNyumba yaMalamulo akuwona kuti zisankho zimenezi zachitika bwino, muchilungamo malinga ndi lamulo;

(c)

kulembetsa anthu odzavota;

(d) kukonza kaundula wa mayina a anthu ovota;

(e)

kusunga bwino mabuku okhala ndi mayina a anthu wovota;

(f)

kugamula malile a malo komwe anthu adzaponye mavoti;

(g) kukonza, kudinda ndi kumwazila mapepala olemba povota, ndikukhazikitsa zofunukila pa ntchito yoponya mavoti, kufuna moyika mavoti, kukhazikitsa ndi kuyanganila malo komwe anthu adzakhale akuponyela mavoti;

(h) kukhazikitsa maphunzilo pankhani yovota ndikuphunzitsila anthu zofunukila;

(i)

kulandila ndikulola anthu ofuna kuyanganila zakuponyedwa kwa mavoti;

(j)

kupatsila malangizo ku anthu wogwila ntchito muBoma kapena makansulo kuchitila kuti mavoti adziyenda bwino, omasuka, chilungamo ndikuchitidwa bwino; ndi

(k) kulandila madandaulo kuchokela ku anthu ndikulongosola madandaulo amenewa panjila yomwe akuganizila kuti ndiyabwino. 240 Kuchotsedwa pa mpikisano wosankhidwa ku komishoni yoyang’anila chisankho Pamwamba pa anthu onenedwa mu chigawo 320(3), anthu otsatilawa akhoza kusnkhidwa kulowa ku Komishoni yoyang’anila za chisankho—

(a)

wogwila ntchito zapabwalo kusiya kwa wozenga milandu;

(b) wogwila ntchito kumakansulo akumatauni akuluu, kuzigawo ndi makansulo akumidzi; ndi

(c)

mamembala a mabungwe ndi makampani a Boma.

241 Komishoni yoyanganila za chisankho iyenela kupatsila malipoti Pamwamba popatsila malipoti ndiyofunikila kuti apeleke malinga ndi chigawo cha 323, Komishoni yoyang’anila za chisankho iyenela kupatsila malipoti mosachedwa, kudzela ku Nduna yoyanganila ntchito zawo, kuNyumba ya Malamulo pankhani zakakachitidwe ka zisankho. GAWO 3 KOMISHONI YOYANG’ANILA MAUFULU A ANTHU

242 Kukhazikitsidwa kwa komishoni yoyang’anila za maufulu a anthu

(1) Pali Komishoni yodziwika kuti Komishoni yoyang’anila maufulu a anthu yokhalila ndi—

(a)

wapampando wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogolei wadziko akuthandizilidwa ndi Komishoni yoyang’anila za Kuzengedwa kwa milandu ndi akomiti ya zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo; ndi

(b) ena mamembala asanu ndi atatu osankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko kuchokela pa anthu osachepela khumi ndi awili ochokela ku Komiti yoyang’ana zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo.

(2) Wapampando ku Komishoni yoyang’anila za ufulu wa anthu ayenela kukhala munthu emwe akhala wokwanila kugwila ntchito ngati loya kwa zaka zisanu ndi ziwili mu Zimbabwe.

(3) Ngathi kusankhidwa kwa wapampando ku Komishoni yoyang’ana za ufulu wa anthu kukusiyana ndi zonenedwa ndi Komishoni yoyang’anila za Kuzengedwa kwa milandu, Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kutumiza uthenga ku Komiti yoyang’ana zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo mofulumila.

(4) Mamembala a Komishoni yoyang’ana za Ufulu mwaanthu ayenela kusankhidwa chifukwa chakudziwa kwawo zintchito, ubwino wawo, kudziwa kwawo ndi luso ndikuzindikila zopititsa mtsogolo nkhani za ufulu wa anthu.

243 Ntchito za komishoni yoyang’anila za ufulu wa anthu

(1) Komishoni yoyang’ana za ufulu wa anthu ili ndi ntchito zotsatila zi—

(a)

kupititsa mtsogolo phunziso zakuti anthu adziwe za ufulu wa anthu ndi kumasuka kwawo mumbali zonse zadziko;

(b) kupititsa mtsogolo zakutetezedwa, chitukuko ndi zaufulu wa anthu;

(c)

kuyangana, kuyang’anila, kupima ndikuwona kuti maufulu a anthu akuyang’anidwa bwino;

(d) kulandila madandaulo kuchokela ku anthu ndikulongosola madandaulo wa panjila zomwe akuwona kuti ndizoyenela;

(e)

kuteteza anthu achabe ku omwe akulepheela kugwilisa ntchito bwino mphamvu zawo ndikulephela kwa mabungwe ndi makampani a Boma kudzela ku wogwila ntchito kumeneko;

(f)

kufufuza ngathi patakhala zakupsyoledwa kwa lamulo pa zaufulu wa anthu ndi bungwe kapena wina munthu;

(g) kupeza njila kokhazikitsa nazo nkhani zimenezi, ndikuwilizila kumangidwa ngathi kuli koyenela, ngathi patakhala kusokonezedwa kwa ufulu wa munthu;

(h) kuuza Mtsogoleli wa a Polisi kuti afufuze nkhani zoganizila kuti palikusokonezedwa kwa ufulu wa anthu ndi njila zoyipa ndikupatsila malipoti ku Komishoni imeneyi zopeza zawo;

(i)

kuwilizila Nyumba yaMalamulo zopititsa mtsogolo zoyanganila maufulu a anthu;

(j)

kuchita kafuku-fuku mu nkhani zokhudza za ufulu wa anthu, chilungamo mu zakakhalidwe; ndi

(k) kuchezela ndi kuyanganitsa—

(i) mundende, kumalo kosungidwa anthu ndi a Polisi, kokhala anthu othawa kumayiko awo, kosungidwa anthu obwela nafuna pokhala;

(ii) malo komwe amasunga odwala bongo, ndi ena anthu; kuti awone kayimidwe ka malo amenewa ngathi ali woyenela kukhalako anthu, ndi kuwilizila Nduna yaBoma yokhudzidwa ndi zimenezi kuti.

(2) Mtsogoleli wa a Polisi ayenela kulandila zomwe akuwuzidwa ndi Komishoni imeneyi malinga ndi chigawocha (1)(h). 244 Malipoti okhudza komishoni yoyang’anila za ufulu

(1) Komishoni yoyang’anila za ufulu ikhoza kufuna munthu, bungwe kapena utumikili, waBoma kapena zina—

(a)

kuuza Komishoni imeneyi njila zomwe akutenga zopititsa mtsogolo maufulu a nthu malinga ndi dongosolo ya maufulu a anthu; ndi

(b) kupatsila Komishoni zomwe ingafune pofuna kupititsa ena anthu kumabwalo a zamilandu, malipotiwa adzipita ku mabungwe akuchigawo chakuno kapena kudziko lonse lapansi komwe Zimbabwe ndi membala.

(2) pamwamba pa malipoti iyenela kupatsila malinga ndi chigawo cha 323, Komishoni yoyang’anila zaufulu wa anthu,, akhoza kupeleka malipoti ku Nduna yoyanganila nkhani zimenezi kuNyumba ya Malamulo pankhani zaufulu wa anthu, zomwe komishoniyi ikuganiza kuti zibwele pamaso pa aphungu a Nyumba ya Malamulo.

GAWO 4 KOMISHONI YA ZAKUYANJANA KWA AMUNA NDI AKAZI

245 Kukhazikitsidwa ndi wogwila ntchito ku Komishoni yazakuyanjana pakati pa azimai ndi amuna

(1) Pali Komishoni yomwe akudziwa kuti Komishoni YaZakuyanjana pakati pa azimai ndi amuna yokhalila ndi anthu awa—

(a)

wapampando wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko atalankhulana ndi Komiti Yoyangana zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo; ndi ena

(b) asanu ndi atatu, komwe—

(i) asanu ndi awili akusankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko pa anthu osachepela khumi ndi awili wopatsidwa ndi Komiti Yoyangana zochitika kunyumba ya Malamulo; ndi

(ii) mmodzi ndiwotengedwa ndikuyikidwa ku gulu la Mfumu wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko.

(2) Mamembala a Komishoni yoyang’ana nkhani za azimai ndi amuna akusankhidwa chifukwa chakudziwa kwawo ntchito, chilungamo, kumvetsetsa nkhani zakuyanjana kwa amuna mukakhalidwe, muzachuma ndi zandale. Ndiye kuti pakahale azimai ndi amuna. 246 Ntchito za komishoni yazakuyanjana kwa azimai ndi amuna Komishoni Yazakuyanjana pakati pa azimai ndi amuna ili ndi ntchito zotsatilazi—

(a)

kuyangana nkhani zokhudza zoyanjana pakati pa amuna ndi azimai kuchitila kuti pazikhala kuyanjana paamipata ndi zina malinga ndi zopezeka muLamulo;

(b) kufufuza kupsyoledwa kwa maufulu a anthu zodalila ku nkhani za amuna ndi akazi;

(c)

kulandila, kutenga madandaulo a anthu ndikuwalongosola momwe angakwanitsile;

(d) kufufuza nkhani zokhudza zoyanjana pakati pa azimai ndi amuna, chilungamo muzakakhalidwe, ndi kuwilizila kusintha kwa ena malamulo pokhudza nkhani za azimai ndikubwelanso;

(e)

kuwilizila mabungwe ndi makampani panjila zotenga pofuna kubwelesa kuyanjana kwa mipata pakati pa zima ndi amuna;

(f)

kuyika ko mapologalamu otukula oyesa kubwelesa nkhani za amai ndi bambo;

(g) kuwilizila kuti omwe amapsyola malamulo okhudza zimenezi adzimangidwa;

(h) kufuna kukhazikitsila nkhani pakakhala vuto ndi;

(i)

kuchita zonse zoyenela kuti pakhale kuyanjana pakati pa amuna ndi akazi.

247 Malipoti a komishoni yoyang’anila zakuyanjana pakati pa azimai ndi amuna Pamwamba pa malipoti ikufunikanso kuti kupatsila, malinga ndi chigawo 323, Komishoni ya Zakuyanjana pakati pa azima ndi amuna iyenela, kudzela ku Nduna yoyanganila nkhani zimenezi, kupeleka malipoti kuNyumba ya Malamulo pa nkhani zokhudza zoyanjana kwa azimai ndi amuna, omwe malinga ndi komishoni imeneyi malipotiwa ayenela kufika kuNyumba ya Malamulo.

GAWO 5 KOMISHONI YOYANG’ANA ZA MABUNGWE A NKHANI

248 Kukhazikitsidwa ndi wogwila ntchito ku komishoni yoyang’anila za mabungwe a nkhani

(1) Pali komishoni yomwe akudziwa kuti Komishoni yoyang’anila za mabungwe a nkhani yokhalila ndi—

(a)

wapampando wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko akugwilizana ndi Komiti yoyang’anila zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo; ndi

(b) ena asanu ndi atatu osankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko akugwilizana nso ndi Komiti yoyang’ana zochitika kuNyumba ya Malamulo.

(2) Mamembala a Komishoni yoyang’anila za mabungwe a nkhani iyenela kukhala ndi anthu osankhidwa pakudziwa ntchito zawo, kumvetsetsa za maufulu a nthu kumabungwe a nkhani. 249 Ntchito ya komishoni yoyanganila za mabungwe a nkhani

(1) Komishoni imeneyi ili ndi ntchito zotsatila izi—

(a)

kupititsa mtsogolo, kukwezeleza ndi kukonza za maufulu muntchito ya utolankhani;

(b) kupititsa mtsogolo ndi kuwona kuti pali ubwino, kutsatil malamulo ndi zina zofunikila mu utolankhani;

(c)

kuyanganila zoulusidwa pawailesi ndi kanema kuwona kuti palichilungamo ndiye anthu onse omwe akufuna kulankhula pa wailesi akulankhula;

(d) kukhazikitsa njila zakagwilidwe ka ntchito za utolankhani;

(e)

kulandila ndikulongosola madandaulo a anthu mdziko, komwe kuli kotheka kuchitako kanthu ku atolankhani omwe kuchita zosayenela pa ntchito yautolankhani;

(f)

kuwona kuti anthu a mu Zimbabwe akupeza mawu ofunikila ndi njila zosiyana-siyana;

(g) kuwilizila ndi kugwilitsa ntchito zilankhulo zones zopezeka mdziko;

(h) kuwilizila kuti adzitenga ko njila zatsopano pogwila ntchito zautolankhani ndi kufalitsa uthenga;

(i)

kupititsa mtsogolo nkhani zakuti padzikhala mawu osiyana-siyana ndi mabungwe a nkhani ambili.

(j)

kuchita kafuku-fuku za ufulu wa utolankhani ndi kupelekela mauthenga ku anthu ndi kutukula la kayendesedwe ka nkhani zimenezi.

(2) Lamulo ladziko likhoza kupatsila mphamvu ku Komishoni yoyangana za mabungwe a nkhani kuchitila kuti—

(a)

kufufuza ndikuyesa kupeza za—

(i) kachitidwe kalikonse kofuna ku wopsvezela ufulu wa ntchito zautolankhani; ndi

(ii) zochitidwa ndi mabungwe a nkhani; ndi

(b) kukhazikitsa njila zopatsila zilango ku omwe akusokoneza lamulo la utolankhani.

(3) Lamulo ladziko likhoza kuyika mfundo zochita kuti ntchito zautolankhani ziziyenda bwino.

250 Malipoti a komishoni ya mabungwe a nkhani Powonjezela pa malipoti omwe ayenela kupatsa malinga ndi chigawo 323, Komishoni imeneyi ikhoza kudzelaku Nduna kupatsila malipoti kuNyumba ya Malamulo pa nkhani zokhudza zamabungwe autolankhani. GAWO 6 KOMISHONI YA BATA NDI KUKHULUKILILANA

251 Kukhazikitsidwa ndi wogwila ntchito ku komishoni yoyangana za bata ndi kukhululikilana

(1) Panthawi ya zaka khumi mbuyo mokukhazikitsidwa kwa Lamulo limeneli kudzakhala komishoni yokhalila ndi—

(a)

wapampando wosankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko akulankhulana ndi Komishoni yoyanganila zakazengedwe ka milandu ndi thandizilo la Komiti yakuNyumba ya Malamulo; ndi

(b) ena asanu ndi atatu osankhidwa ndi Mtsogoleli wadziko kuchokela pa undandanda wa anthu wosachepela khumi ndi awili wochokela ku Komiti YakuNyumba ya Malamulo.

(2) Ayenela kukhala munthu emwe akhala atakhala womaliza maphunzilo ake zaka zisanu ndi ziwili ngathi loya mu Zimbabwe.

(3) Ngathi munthu wosankhidwa kutenga mpando ku Komishoni yoyang’anila bata ndi kukhulukililana akulephela ku kwanitsila zofunidwa ndi Komiti ya Nyumba ya Malamulo, Mtsogoleli wadziko ayenela kuchita kuti komiti yaNyumba yaMalamulo iwuzidwe mofulumila.

(4) Mamembala a Komishoni ya Bata ndi Kukhulukililana ayenela kusankhidwa podziwa ntchito zawo, akhale a luso ndi kudziwa nkhani, kubweletsa anthu odana pamodzi, kuletsa zaphokoso ndi kuwilizila a bata. 252 Ntchito za komishoni ya bata ndi kukhulukililana Komishoni ya bata ndi kukhulukililana ili ndi ntchito izi—

(a)

kuchita kuti pakhale chilungamo mbuyo movutana, kukhulukililana;

(b) kukonza njila zobweletsa kugwilizana mdziko, ndikukhalila pa bata ndi mtendele ndi kugwilizana mu Zimbabwe ndikukhazikitsa maphokoso mubata ndi mtendele;

(c)

kubweletsa anthu pamodzi ndikuwawilizila kuti alankhule zoona pa zomwe zina chitika mdziko malinga ndi zakale kuti anthu akhazikitse ubwino;

(d) kukhazikitsa njila zosiyana-siyana kuti anthu azikhala limodzi kuchitila kuti anthu adzilankhulanapankhani za ndale, chikhalidwe, zachitukuko ndi zina;

(e)

kukonza mapologalamu ochita kuti anthu omwe anavutisidwa ndi zina zoyipa apeza thandizilo;

(f)

kulandila ndikulongosola madandaulo kuchokela ku anthu onse;

(g) kukhazikitsa njila zoyanganila kuti pasakhale phokoso ndi kusamvana zisanachitike ndi kulimbana, kuyikako njila zoletsa zimenezi;

(h) kupititsa mtsogolo za bata ndi mtendele ndikuphunzisila anthu kuyipa kwa phokoso;

(i)

kukhazikitsa mikangano pakati pa anthu osiyana-siyana ndi mabungwe osiyana-siyana, ndi anthu chabe; ndi

(j)

kuchita kuti pakhale malemba okwanila ochita kuti anthu ovutisidwa adzalandile thandizo ndi maipilo.

253 Malipoti a komishoni yoyanganila za bata ndi kukhulukililana Mukuwonjezela zimenezi ndi malipoti a komishoni imeneyi imafunikanso kuti ipatsile malipoti malinga ndi chigawo 323, ndiye akhozanso kupatsila malipoti kuNyumba ya Malamulo pa nkhani zokhudza zokhulukililana mudziko lonse kudzela kuNduna yoyang’anila nkhani zimenezi, malipoti amenewa ayenela kudzafika kuNyumba yaMalamulo pamaganizo a Komishoni imeneyi. MUTU 13 MABUNGWE OLIMBIKANA NDI UTANGALE NDI MILANDU

GAWO 1 KOMISHONI YOLIMBIKANA NDI UTANGALE MU ZIMBABWE

254 Kukhazikidwa kwa bungwe ndi wogwila ntchito ku Komishoni Yolimbikana ndi Ukatangale

(1) Pali komishoni yodziwika kuti Komishoni Yolimbikana ndi Utangale Komishoni yokhalila ndi anthu awa—

(a)

wapampando wosankhidwa ndi mtsogoleli wadziko akugwilizana ndi ndi komiti yoyan’anila za malalmulo ndi tsatane-tsatane wake; ndi

(b) asanu ndi atatu ena osankidwa ndi mtsogoleli wadziko kuchokela pa undandanda wa anthu wosachepera khumi ndi awili kuchokela ku komiti yoyan’nila za malamulo.

(2) Mamembala a bungwe loyan”nila za utangale la ayenela kusankhidwa chifukwa cha nzeru ndi luso potsogolela ndi kupititsa mtsogolo za kayendesedwe ndi kazengedwe ka milandu ndi kufufuza nkhani kapena pakuyenera kwawo kusankhidwa, ndi—

(a)

munthu yu akhaale wokwanila kugwila ntchito ngathi loya mudziko la Zimbabwe, ndiye akhale ataphunzira ndikukwanila pa ntchito yi kwa zaka zofika zisanu ndi ziwili;

(b) munthu akahale wokwanila pa ntchito yopititsa mtsogolo zoyan’anila za ndalama zodziwika mudziko la Zimbabwe, akhale pa ntchito yi kwa zaka zofika pa zisanu ndi ziwili; ndi

(c)

munthu yu akhale ndi zaka zofika khumi akugwila ntchito imeneyi yofufuza za milandu mmbuyo.

255 Ntchito ya bungwe la Lolimbikana ndi Utangale

(1) Bungwe lolimbikana ndi utangale limagwila ntchito zotsatila izi—

(a)

kufufuza ndi kutulusa poyera nkhani za ukatangale kumabungwe a boma ndi oziyimila pa okha;

(b) kufokesa utangale, kuba, kusokoneza, kusagwilisa bwino mphamvu ndi zina zosayenera kumabungwe a boma ndi oziyimira okha;

(c) kupitisa mtsogolo zoona, zakayendersedwe kabwino mu nkhani za ndalama kumabungwe a boma ndi oziyimila okha;

(d) kulandila ndi kumvera madandaulo kuchokela ku anthu onse mdziko ndi kulongosola madandaulo wo panjira zomwe akawone kuti ndizabwino;

(e)

kulondolela Mtsogoleli wa apolisi kuti akufufuza nkhani za ukatangale ndi kupatsila zotsatila zake kubungwe limeneli;

(f)
